  29func (b *BaseApi) SearchApp(c *gin.Context) {
  30	var req request.AppSearch
  31	if err := helper.CheckBindAndValidate(&req, c); err != nil {
  32		return
  33	}
  34	list, err := appService.PageApp(c, req)

  42"cmt">// @Tags App
  43"cmt">// @Summary Sync remote app list
  44"cmt">// @Success 200
  45"cmt">// @Security ApiKeyAuth
  46"cmt">// @Security Timestamp
  47"cmt">// @Router /apps/sync/remote [post]
  48"cmt">// @x-panel-log {"bodyKeys":[],"paramKeys":[],"BeforeFunctions":[],"formatZH":"应用商店同步","formatEN":"App store synchronization"}
  49func (b *BaseApi) SyncApp(c *gin.Context) {
  50	var req dto.OperateWithTask
  51	if err := helper.CheckBindAndValidate(&req, c); err != nil {
  52		return
  53	}
  54	res, err := appService.GetAppUpdate()
  55	if err != nil {
  56		helper.InternalServer(c, err)
  57		return
  58	}
  59	if !res.CanUpdate {
  60		if res.IsSyncing {
  61			helper.SuccessWithMsg(c, i18n.GetMsgByKey("AppStoreIsSyncing"))
  62		} else {
  63			helper.SuccessWithMsg(c, i18n.GetMsgByKey("AppStoreIsUpToDate"))
  64		}
  65		return
  66	}
  67	if err = appService.SyncAppListFromRemote(req.TaskID); err != nil {
  68		helper.InternalServer(c, err)
  69		return
  70	}
  71	helper.Success(c)
  72}

 212"cmt">// @Tags App
 213"cmt">// @Summary Get app icon by app_id
 214"cmt">// @Accept json
 215"cmt">// @Param key path string true "app key"
 216"cmt">// @Success 200 {file} file "app icon"
 217"cmt">// @Security ApiKeyAuth
 218"cmt">// @Security Timestamp
 219"cmt">// @Router /apps/icon/:key [get]
 220func (b *BaseApi) GetAppIcon(c *gin.Context) {
 221	appKey, err := helper.GetStrParamByKey(c, "key")
 222	if err != nil {
 223		helper.BadRequest(c, err)
 224		return
 225	}
 226	iconBytes, filename, etag, err := appService.GetAppIcon(appKey)
 227	if err != nil {
 228		helper.InternalServer(c, err)
 229		return
 230	}
 231
 232	if len(iconBytes) == 0 {
 233		c.Status(http.StatusNoContent)
 234		return
 235	}
 236
 237	c.Header("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=2592000")
 238
 239	if etag != "" && filename != "" {
 240		c.Header("ETag", etag)
 241		if c.GetHeader("If-None-Match") == etag {
 242			c.Status(http.StatusNotModified)
 243			return
 244		}
 245	}
 246
 247	c.Data(http.StatusOK, appicon.ContentTypePNG, iconBytes)
 248}
 249

  54"cmt">// @Tags App

  56"cmt">// @Accept json
  57"cmt">// @Param request body request.ReqWithID true "request"

  64	var req request.ReqWithID
  65	if err := helper.CheckBindAndValidate(&req, c); err != nil {
  66		return
  67	}
  68	if err := appIgnoreUpgradeService.Delete(req); err != nil {
  69		helper.InternalServer(c, err)
  70		return
  71	}
  72	helper.Success(c)
  73}

  18"cmt">// @Tags App
  19"cmt">// @Summary Page app installed
  20"cmt">// @Accept json
  21"cmt">// @Param request body request.AppInstalledSearch true "request"
  22"cmt">// @Success 200 {object} dto.PageResult
  23"cmt">// @Security ApiKeyAuth
  24"cmt">// @Security Timestamp
  25"cmt">// @Router /apps/installed/search [post]
  26func (b *BaseApi) SearchAppInstalled(c *gin.Context) {
  27	var req request.AppInstalledSearch
  28	if err := helper.CheckBindAndValidate(&req, c); err != nil {
  29		return
  30	}
  31	if req.All {
  32		list, err := appInstallService.SearchForWebsite(req)
  33		if err != nil {
  34			helper.InternalServer(c, err)
  35			return
  36		}
  37		helper.SuccessWithData(c, dto.PageResult{
  38			Items: list,
  39			Total: int64(len(list)),
  40		})
  41	} else {
  42		total, list, err := appInstallService.Page(req)
  43		if err != nil {
  44			helper.InternalServer(c, err)
  45			return
  46		}
  47		helper.SuccessWithData(c, dto.PageResult{
  48			Items: list,
  49			Total: total,
  50		})
  51	}
  52}

 195"cmt">// @Param key path string true "request"
 196"cmt">// @Success 200 {array} response.AppService
 197"cmt">// @Security ApiKeyAuth
 198"cmt">// @Security Timestamp
 199"cmt">// @Router /apps/services/:key [get]
 200func (b *BaseApi) GetServices(c *gin.Context) {

 205		return
 206	}
 207	helper.SuccessWithData(c, services)

  24	Key                  string  `json:"key" gorm:"not null;"`
  25	ShortDescZh          string  `json:"shortDescZh" yaml:"shortDescZh"`
  26	ShortDescEn          string  `json:"shortDescEn" yaml:"shortDescEn"`
  27	Description          string  `json:"description"`
  28	Icon                 string  `json:"icon"`
  29	Type                 string  `json:"type" gorm:"not null"`
  30	Status               string  `json:"status" gorm:"not null"`
  31	Required             string  `json:"required"`
  32	CrossVersionUpdate   bool    `json:"crossVersionUpdate" yaml:"crossVersionUpdate"`
  33	Limit                int     `json:"limit" gorm:"not null"`
  34	Website              string  `json:"website" gorm:"not null"`
  35	Github               string  `json:"github" gorm:"not null"`
  36	Document             string  `json:"document" gorm:"not null"`
  37	Recommend            int     `json:"recommend" gorm:"not null"`
  38	Resource             string  `json:"resource" gorm:"not null;default:remote"`
  39	ReadMe               string  `json:"readMe"`
  40	LastModified         int     `json:"lastModified"`
  41	Architectures        string  `json:"architectures"`
  42	MemoryRequired       int     `json:"memoryRequired"`
  43	GpuSupport           bool    `json:"gpuSupport"`
  44	RequiredPanelVersion float64 `json:"requiredPanelVersion"`
  45	BatchInstallSupport  bool    `json:"batchInstallSupport" yaml:"batchInstallSupport"`
  46
  47	Details []AppDetail `json:"-" gorm:"-:migration"`
  48	TagsKey []string    `json:"tags" yaml:"tags" gorm:"-"`
  49	AppTags []AppTag    `json:"-" gorm:"-:migration"`
  50}
  51
  52func (i *App) IsLocalApp() bool {
  53	return i.Resource == constant.ResourceLocal
  54}
  55func (i *App) IsCustomApp() bool {
  56	return i.Resource == constant.AppResourceCustom
  57}
  58
  59func (i *App) GetAppResourcePath() string {
  60	if i.IsLocalApp() {
  61		return filepath.Join(global.Dir.LocalAppResourceDir, strings.TrimPrefix(i.Key, "local"))
  62	}
  63	if i.IsCustomApp() {
  64		return filepath.Join(global.Dir.CustomAppResourceDir, i.Key)
  65	}
  66	return filepath.Join(global.Dir.RemoteAppResourceDir, i.Key)
  67}
  68
  69func getLang(c *gin.Context) string {
  70	lang := c.GetHeader("Accept-Language")
  71	if lang == "" {
  72		lang = "en"
  73	}
  74	return lang
  75}
  76
  77func (i *App) GetDescription(ctx *gin.Context) string {
  78	var translations = make(map[string]string)
  79	_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(i.Description), &translations)
  80	lang := strings.ToLower(getLang(ctx))
  81	if desc, ok := translations[lang]; ok && desc != "" {
  82		return desc
  83	}
  84	if lang == "zh" && i.ShortDescZh != "" {
  85		return i.ShortDescZh
  86	}
  87	if desc, ok := translations["en"]; ok && desc != "" {
  88		return desc
  89	}
  90	return i.ShortDescEn
  91}
  92

📄 app.go agent/app/repo/app.go

   1"cmt">// =============================================================================
   2"cmt">// 模块: App 应用商店 (agent/app/repo/app.go)
   3"cmt">// 文件: app.go — 主代码
   4"cmt">// 说明: 本文件为 1Panel 上游源码拷贝 + 中文注解, 源码 commit: dev-v2
   5"cmt">//       注解只增加 // 注释, 不改变 Go 语义, 文件仍可直接用 go build 编译
   6"cmt">// =============================================================================
   7
   8package repo
   9
  10import (
  11	"context"
  12	"fmt"
  13
  14	"github.com/1Panel-dev/1Panel/agent/app/model"
  15	"gorm.io/gorm"
  16	"gorm.io/gorm/clause"
  17)
  18
  19"cmt">// AppRepo (struct)
  20type AppRepo struct {
  21}
  22
  23"cmt">// IAppRepo (interface)
  24type IAppRepo interface {
  25	WithKey(key string) DBOption
  26	WithType(typeStr string) DBOption
  27	OrderByRecommend() DBOption
  28	GetRecommend() DBOption
  29	WithResource(resource string) DBOption
  30	WithNotLocal() DBOption
  31	WithByLikeName(name string) DBOption
  32	WithKeyIn(keys []string) DBOption
  33	WithArch(arch string) DBOption
  34	WithPanelVersion(panelVersion string) DBOption
  35
  36	Page(page, size int, opts ...DBOption) (int64, []model.App, error)
  37	GetFirst(opts ...DBOption) (model.App, error)
  38	GetBy(opts ...DBOption) ([]model.App, error)
  39	BatchCreate(ctx context.Context, apps []model.App) error
  40	Create(ctx context.Context, app *model.App) error
  41	Save(ctx context.Context, app *model.App) error
  42	BatchDelete(ctx context.Context, apps []model.App) error
  43	DeleteByIDs(ctx context.Context, ids []uint) error
  44	DeleteBy(opts ...DBOption) error
  45
  46	GetTopRecommend() ([]string, error)
  47}
  48
  49func NewIAppRepo() IAppRepo {
  50	return &AppRepo{}
  51}
  52
  53func (a AppRepo) WithByLikeName(name string) DBOption {
  54	return func(g *gorm.DB) *gorm.DB {
  55		if len(name) == 0 {
  56			return g
  57		}
  58		return g.Where("name like ? or  description like ? or short_desc_zh like ? or short_desc_en like ?", "%"+name+"%", "%"+name+"%", "%"+name+"%", "%"+name+"%")
  59	}
  60}
  61
  62func (a AppRepo) WithKey(key string) DBOption {
  63	return func(db *gorm.DB) *gorm.DB {
  64		return db.Where("key = ?", key)
  65	}
  66}
  67
  68func (a AppRepo) WithKeyIn(keys []string) DBOption {
  69	return func(db *gorm.DB) *gorm.DB {
  70		return db.Where("`key` in (?)", keys)
  71	}
  72}
  73
  74func (a AppRepo) WithType(typeStr string) DBOption {
  75	return func(g *gorm.DB) *gorm.DB {
  76		return g.Where("type = ?", typeStr)
  77	}
  78}
  79
  80func (a AppRepo) OrderByRecommend() DBOption {
  81	return func(g *gorm.DB) *gorm.DB {
  82		return g.Order("recommend asc")
  83	}
  84}
  85
  86func (a AppRepo) GetRecommend() DBOption {
  87	return func(g *gorm.DB) *gorm.DB {
  88		return g.Where("recommend < 9999")
  89	}
  90}
  91
  92func (a AppRepo) WithResource(resource string) DBOption {
  93	return func(g *gorm.DB) *gorm.DB {
  94		return g.Where("resource = ?", resource)
  95	}
  96}
  97
  98func (a AppRepo) WithNotLocal() DBOption {
  99	return func(g *gorm.DB) *gorm.DB {
 100		return g.Where("resource != 'local'")
 101	}
 102}
 103
 104func (a AppRepo) WithArch(arch string) DBOption {
 105	return func(g *gorm.DB) *gorm.DB {
 106		return g.Where("architectures like ?", fmt.Sprintf("%%%s%%", arch))
 107	}
 108}
 109
 110func (a AppRepo) WithPanelVersion(panelVersion string) DBOption {
 111	return func(g *gorm.DB) *gorm.DB {
 112		return g.Where("required_panel_version >= ?", panelVersion)
 113	}
 114}
 115
 116func (a AppRepo) Page(page, size int, opts ...DBOption) (int64, []model.App, error) {
 117	var apps []model.App
 118	db := getDb(opts...).Model(&model.App{})
 119	count := int64(0)
 120	db = db.Count(&count)
 121	err := db.Limit(size).Offset(size * (page - 1)).Preload("AppTags").Find(&apps).Error
 122	return count, apps, err
 123}
 124
 125func (a AppRepo) GetFirst(opts ...DBOption) (model.App, error) {
 126	var app model.App
 127	db := getDb(opts...).Model(&model.App{})
 128	if err := db.Preload("AppTags").First(&app).Error; err != nil {
 129		return app, err
 130	}
 131	return app, nil
 132}
 133
 134func (a AppRepo) GetBy(opts ...DBOption) ([]model.App, error) {
 135	var apps []model.App
 136	db := getDb(opts...).Model(&model.App{})
 137	if err := db.Preload("Details").Preload("AppTags").Find(&apps).Error; err != nil {
 138		return apps, err
 139	}
 140	return apps, nil
 141}
 142
 143func (a AppRepo) GetTopRecommend() ([]string, error) {
 144	var (
 145		apps  []model.App
 146		names []string
 147	)
 148	db := getDb().Model(&model.App{})
 149	if err := db.Order("recommend asc").Limit(6).Find(&apps).Error; err != nil {
 150		return names, err
 151	}
 152	for _, item := range apps {
 153		names = append(names, item.Key)
 154	}
 155	return names, nil
 156}
 157
 158func (a AppRepo) BatchCreate(ctx context.Context, apps []model.App) error {
 159	return getTx(ctx).Omit(clause.Associations).Create(&apps).Error
 160}
 161
 162func (a AppRepo) Create(ctx context.Context, app *model.App) error {
 163	return getTx(ctx).Omit(clause.Associations).Create(app).Error
 164}
 165
 166func (a AppRepo) Save(ctx context.Context, app *model.App) error {
 167	return getTx(ctx).Omit(clause.Associations).Save(app).Error
 168}
 169
 170func (a AppRepo) BatchDelete(ctx context.Context, apps []model.App) error {
 171	return getTx(ctx).Omit(clause.Associations).Delete(&apps).Error
 172}
 173
 174func (a AppRepo) DeleteByIDs(ctx context.Context, ids []uint) error {
 175	return getTx(ctx).Where("id in (?)", ids).Delete(&model.App{}).Error
 176}
 177
 178func (a AppRepo) DeleteBy(opts ...DBOption) error {
 179	return getDb().Delete(&model.App{}, opts).Error
 180}
 181

  30	DeleteByAppIds(ctx context.Context, appIds []uint) error

  56	}
  57}

  59func (a AppDetailRepo) GetFirst(opts ...DBOption) (model.AppDetail, error) {
  60	var detail model.AppDetail
  61	err := getDb(opts...).Model(&model.AppDetail{}).Find(&detail).Error
  62	return detail, err
  63}
  64
  65func (a AppDetailRepo) Update(ctx context.Context, detail model.AppDetail) error {
  66	return getTx(ctx).Save(&detail).Error
  67}
  68
  69func (a AppDetailRepo) BatchCreate(ctx context.Context, details []model.AppDetail) error {
  70	return getTx(ctx).Model(&model.AppDetail{}).Create(&details).Error
  71}
  72
  73func (a AppDetailRepo) DeleteByAppIds(ctx context.Context, appIds []uint) error {

  79}
  80
  81func (a AppDetailRepo) GetBy(opts ...DBOption) ([]model.AppDetail, error) {
  82	var details []model.AppDetail
  83	err := getDb(opts...).Find(&details).Error
  84	return details, err
  85}
  86
  87func (a AppDetailRepo) BatchUpdateBy(maps map[string]interface{}, opts ...DBOption) error {
  88	db := getDb(opts...).Model(&model.AppDetail{})
  89	if len(opts) == 0 {
  90		db = db.Where("1=1")
  91	}
  92	return db.Updates(&maps).Error
  93}
  94
  95func (a AppDetailRepo) BatchDelete(ctx context.Context, appDetails []model.AppDetail) error {
  96	return getTx(ctx).Omit(clause.Associations).Delete(&appDetails).Error
  97}

 161func (a *AppInstallRepo) Page(page, size int, opts ...DBOption) (int64, []model.AppInstall, error) {
 162	var apps []model.AppInstall
 163	db := getDb(opts...).Model(&model.AppInstall{})
 164	count := int64(0)

 167	return count, apps, err

 170func (a *AppInstallRepo) BatchUpdateBy(maps map[string]interface{}, opts ...DBOption) error {

 174	}
 175	return db.Updates(&maps).Error

 178"cmt">// RootInfo (struct)
 179type RootInfo struct {
 180	ID            uint   `json:"id"`
 181	Name          string `json:"name"`
 182	Status        string `json:"status"`
 183	Port          int64  `json:"port"`
 184	HttpsPort     int64  `json:"httpsPort"`
 185	UserName      string `json:"userName"`
 186	Password      string `json:"password"`
 187	UserPassword  string `json:"userPassword"`
 188	ContainerName string `json:"containerName"`
 189	ServiceName   string `json:"serviceName"`
 190	Param         string `json:"param"`
 191	Env           string `json:"env"`
 192	Key           string `json:"key"`
 193	Version       string `json:"version"`
 194	AppPath       string `json:"app_path"`
 195}
 196
 197func (a *AppInstallRepo) LoadBaseInfo(key string, name string) (*RootInfo, error) {
 198	var (
 199		app        model.App
 200		appInstall model.AppInstall
 201		info       RootInfo
 202	)
 203	if err := global.DB.Where("key = ?", key).First(&app).Error; err != nil {
 204		return nil, err
 205	}
 206	if len(name) == 0 {
 207		if err := global.DB.Where("app_id = ?", app.ID).First(&appInstall).Error; err != nil {
 208			return nil, err
 209		}
 210	} else {
 211		if err := global.DB.Where("app_id = ? AND name = ?", app.ID, name).First(&appInstall).Error; err != nil {
 212			return nil, err
 213		}
 214	}
 215	envMap := make(map[string]interface{})
 216	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(appInstall.Env), &envMap); err != nil {
 217		return nil, err
 218	}
 219	switch app.Key {
 220	case "mysql", "mariadb", constant.AppMysqlCluster:
 221		password, ok := envMap["PANEL_DB_ROOT_PASSWORD"].(string)
 222		if ok {
 223			info.Password = password
 224		}
 225	case "redis", constant.AppRedisCluster:
 226		password, ok := envMap["PANEL_REDIS_ROOT_PASSWORD"].(string)
 227		if ok {
 228			info.Password = password
 229		}
 230	case "mongodb", constant.AppPostgresql, constant.AppPostgresqlCluster:
 231		user, ok := envMap["PANEL_DB_ROOT_USER"].(string)
 232		if ok {
 233			info.UserName = user
 234		}
 235		password, ok := envMap["PANEL_DB_ROOT_PASSWORD"].(string)
 236		if ok {
 237			info.Password = password
 238		}
 239	}
 240
 241	userPassword, ok := envMap["PANEL_DB_USER_PASSWORD"].(string)
 242	if ok {
 243		info.UserPassword = userPassword
 244	}
 245	info.Port = int64(appInstall.HttpPort)
 246	info.HttpsPort = int64(appInstall.HttpsPort)
 247	info.ID = appInstall.ID
 248	info.ServiceName = appInstall.ServiceName
 249	info.ContainerName = appInstall.ContainerName
 250	info.Name = appInstall.Name
 251	info.Env = appInstall.Env
 252	info.Param = appInstall.Param
 253	info.Version = appInstall.Version
 254	info.Key = app.Key
 255	appInstall.App = app
 256	info.AppPath = appInstall.GetAppPath()
 257	info.Status = appInstall.Status
 258	return &info, nil
 259}
 260
 261func (a *AppInstallRepo) LoadInstallAppByKeyAndName(key string, name string) (*model.AppInstall, error) {
 262	var (
 263		app        model.App
 264		appInstall model.AppInstall
 265	)
 266	if err := global.DB.Where("key = ?", key).First(&app).Error; err != nil {
 267		return nil, err
 268	}
 269	if len(name) == 0 {
 270		if err := global.DB.Where("app_id = ?", app.ID).First(&appInstall).Error; err != nil {
 271			return nil, err
 272		}
 273	} else {
 274		if err := global.DB.Where("app_id = ? AND name = ?", app.ID, name).First(&appInstall).Error; err != nil {
 275			return nil, err
 276		}
 277	}
 278	return &appInstall, nil
 279}

   8package repo

  15"cmt">// LauncherRepo (struct)
  16type LauncherRepo struct{}
  17
  18"cmt">// ILauncherRepo (interface)
  19type ILauncherRepo interface {
  20	Get(opts ...DBOption) (model.AppLauncher, error)
  21	ListName(opts ...DBOption) ([]string, error)
  22	Create(launcher *model.AppLauncher) error
  23	Save(launcher *model.AppLauncher) error
  24	Delete(opts ...DBOption) error
  25
  26	GetQuickJump(opts ...DBOption) (model.QuickJump, error)
  27	ListQuickJump(withAll bool) []model.QuickJump
  28	UpdateQuicks(quicks []model.QuickJump) error
  29}
  30
  31func NewILauncherRepo() ILauncherRepo {
  32	return &LauncherRepo{}
  33}
  34
  35func (u *LauncherRepo) Get(opts ...DBOption) (model.AppLauncher, error) {
  36	var launcher model.AppLauncher
  37	db := global.DB
  38	for _, opt := range opts {
  39		db = opt(db)
  40	}
  41	err := db.First(&launcher).Error
  42	return launcher, err
  43}
  44func (u *LauncherRepo) ListName(opts ...DBOption) ([]string, error) {
  45	var ops []model.AppLauncher
  46	db := global.DB.Model(&model.AppLauncher{})
  47	for _, opt := range opts {
  48		db = opt(db)
  49	}
  50	_ = db.Find(&ops).Error
  51	var names []string
  52	for i := 0; i < len(ops); i++ {
  53		names = append(names, ops[i].Key)
  54	}
  55	return names, nil
  56}
  57
  58func (u *LauncherRepo) Create(launcher *model.AppLauncher) error {
  59	return global.DB.Create(launcher).Error
  60}
  61
  62func (u *LauncherRepo) Save(launcher *model.AppLauncher) error {
  63	return global.DB.Save(launcher).Error
  64}
  65
  66func (u *LauncherRepo) Delete(opts ...DBOption) error {
  67	db := global.DB
  68	for _, opt := range opts {
  69		db = opt(db)
  70	}
  71	return db.Delete(&model.AppLauncher{}).Error
  72}
  73
  74func (u *LauncherRepo) GetQuickJump(opts ...DBOption) (model.QuickJump, error) {
  75	var launcher model.QuickJump
  76	db := global.DB
  77	for _, opt := range opts {
  78		db = opt(db)
  79	}
  80	err := db.First(&launcher).Error
  81	return launcher, err
  82}
  83func (u *LauncherRepo) ListQuickJump(withAll bool) []model.QuickJump {
  84	var quicks []model.QuickJump
  85	if withAll {
  86		_ = global.DB.Find(&quicks).Error
  87	} else {
  88		_ = global.DB.Where("is_show = ?", true).Find(&quicks).Error
  89	}
  90	if !withAll && len(quicks) == 0 {
  91		return []model.QuickJump{
  92			{Name: "Agent", Title: "aiTools.agents.agent", Recommend: 1, IsShow: true, Router: "/ai/agents/agent"},
  93			{Name: "Website", Title: "menu.website", Recommend: 10, IsShow: true, Router: "/websites"},
  94			{Name: "Database", Title: "menu.database", Recommend: 30, IsShow: true, Router: "/databases"},
  95			{Name: "Cronjob", Title: "menu.cronjob", Recommend: 50, IsShow: false, Router: "/cronjobs"},
  96			{Name: "AppInstalled", Title: "home.appInstalled", Recommend: 70, IsShow: true, Router: "/apps/installed"},
  97		}
  98	}
  99
 100	return quicks
 101}
 102func (u *LauncherRepo) UpdateQuicks(quicks []model.QuickJump) error {
 103	tx := global.DB.Begin()
 104	for _, item := range quicks {
 105		if err := tx.Model(&model.QuickJump{}).Where("id = ?", item.ID).Updates(map[string]interface{}{
 106			"is_show": item.IsShow,
 107			"detail":  item.Detail,
 108			"alias":   item.Alias,
 109		}).Error; err != nil {
 110			tx.Rollback()
 111			return err
 112		}
 113	}
 114	tx.Commit()
 115	return nil
 116}

  63func (a AppTagRepo) GetByAppId(appId uint) ([]model.AppTag, error) {
  64	var appTags []model.AppTag
  65	if err := getDb().Where("app_id = ?", appId).Find(&appTags).Error; err != nil {
  66		return nil, err
  67	}
  68	return appTags, nil
  69}
  70
  71func (a AppTagRepo) GetByTagIds(tagIds []uint) ([]model.AppTag, error) {
  72	var appTags []model.AppTag
  73	if err := getDb().Where("tag_id in (?)", tagIds).Find(&appTags).Error; err != nil {
  74		return nil, err
  75	}
  76	return appTags, nil
  77}
  78
  79func (a AppTagRepo) DeleteBy(ctx context.Context, opts ...DBOption) error {
  80	return getTx(ctx, opts...).Delete(&model.AppTag{}).Error
  81}
  82
  83func (a AppTagRepo) GetFirst(ctx context.Context, opts ...DBOption) (*model.AppTag, error) {
  84	var appTag model.AppTag
  85	if err := getTx(ctx, opts...).First(&appTag).Error; err != nil {
  86		return nil, err
  87	}
  88	return &appTag, nil
  89
  90}

  78func (a AppService) PageApp(ctx *gin.Context, req request.AppSearch) (*response.AppRes, error) {
  79	var opts []repo.DBOption
  80	opts = append(opts, appRepo.OrderByRecommend())
  81	if req.Name != "" {
  82		opts = append(opts, appRepo.WithByLikeName(strings.TrimSpace(req.Name)))
  83	}
  84	if req.Type != "" {
  85		opts = append(opts, appRepo.WithType(req.Type))
  86	}
  87	if req.Recommend {
  88		opts = append(opts, appRepo.GetRecommend())
  89	}
  90	if req.Resource != "" && req.Resource != "all" {
  91		opts = append(opts, appRepo.WithResource(req.Resource))
  92	}
  93
  94	if req.ShowCurrentArch {
  95		info, err := NewIDashboardService().LoadOsInfo()
  96		if err != nil {
  97			return nil, err
  98		}
  99		kernelArch := info.KernelArch
 100		if kernelArch == "aarch64" {
 101			kernelArch = "arm64"
 102		}
 103		opts = append(opts, appRepo.WithArch(kernelArch))
 104	}
 105	if len(req.Tags) != 0 {
 106		tags, err := tagRepo.GetByKeys(req.Tags)
 107		if err != nil {
 108			return nil, err
 109		}
 110		var tagIds []uint
 111		for _, t := range tags {
 112			tagIds = append(tagIds, t.ID)
 113		}
 114		appTags, err := appTagRepo.GetByTagIds(tagIds)
 115		if err != nil {
 116			return nil, err
 117		}
 118		var appIds []uint
 119		for _, t := range appTags {
 120			appIds = append(appIds, t.AppId)
 121		}
 122		opts = append(opts, repo.WithByIDs(appIds))
 123	}
 124	res := &response.AppRes{}
 125
 126	total, apps, err := appRepo.Page(req.Page, req.PageSize, opts...)
 127	if err != nil {
 128		return nil, err
 129	}
 130	appDTOs := make([]*response.AppItem, 0)
 131	info := &dto.SettingInfo{}
 132	if req.Type == "php" {
 133		info, _ = NewISettingService().GetSettingInfo()
 134	}
 135	lang := strings.ToLower(common.GetLang(ctx))
 136	for _, ap := range apps {
 137		if req.Type == "php" {
 138			if !global.CONF.Base.IsOffline && (ap.RequiredPanelVersion == 0 || !common.CompareAppVersion(common.GetSystemVersion(info.SystemVersion), fmt.Sprintf("%f", ap.RequiredPanelVersion))) {
 139				continue
 140			}
 141		}
 142		appDTO := &response.AppItem{
 143			ID:                  ap.ID,
 144			Name:                ap.Name,
 145			Key:                 ap.Key,
 146			Limit:               ap.Limit,
 147			GpuSupport:          ap.GpuSupport,
 148			Recommend:           ap.Recommend,
 149			Description:         ap.GetDescription(ctx),
 150			Type:                ap.Type,
 151			BatchInstallSupport: ap.BatchInstallSupport,
 152		}
 153		appDTOs = append(appDTOs, appDTO)
 154		tags, err := getAppTags(ap.ID, lang)
 155		if err != nil {
 156			continue
 157		}
 158		for _, tag := range tags {
 159			appDTO.Tags = append(appDTO.Tags, tag.Name)
 160		}
 161		if ap.Type == constant.RuntimePHP || ap.Type == constant.RuntimeGo || ap.Type == constant.RuntimeNode || ap.Type == constant.RuntimePython || ap.Type == constant.RuntimeJava || ap.Type == constant.RuntimeDotNet {
 162			details, _ := appDetailRepo.GetBy(appDetailRepo.WithAppId(ap.ID))
 163			var ids []uint
 164			if len(details) == 0 {
 165				continue
 166			}
 167			for _, d := range details {
 168				ids = append(ids, d.ID)
 169			}
 170			runtimes, _ := runtimeRepo.List(runtimeRepo.WithDetailIdsIn(ids))
 171			appDTO.Installed = len(runtimes) > 0
 172		} else {
 173			installs, _ := appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background(), appInstallRepo.WithAppId(ap.ID))
 174			appDTO.Installed = len(installs) > 0
 175		}
 176	}
 177	res.Items = appDTOs
 178	res.Total = total
 179
 180	return res, nil
 181}

 183func (a AppService) GetAppTags(ctx *gin.Context) ([]response.TagDTO, error) {
 184	tags, err := tagRepo.All()
 185	if err != nil {
 186		return nil, err
 187	}
 188	var res []response.TagDTO
 189	lang := strings.ToLower(common.GetLang(ctx))
 190	for _, tag := range tags {
 191		tagDTO := response.TagDTO{
 192			ID:  tag.ID,
 193			Key: tag.Key,
 194		}
 195		var translations = make(map[string]string)
 196		_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(tag.Translations), &translations)
 197		if name, ok := translations[lang]; ok {
 198			tagDTO.Name = name
 199		}
 200		res = append(res, tagDTO)
 201	}
 202	return res, nil
 203}

 205func (a AppService) GetApp(ctx *gin.Context, key string) (*response.AppDTO, error) {
 206	var appDTO response.AppDTO
 207	if key == "postgres" {
 208		key = "postgresql"
 209	}
 210	app, err := appRepo.GetFirst(appRepo.WithKey(key))
 211	if err != nil {
 212		return nil, err
 213	}
 214	appDTO.App = app
 215	appDTO.App.Description = app.GetDescription(ctx)
 216	details, err := appDetailRepo.GetBy(appDetailRepo.WithAppId(app.ID))
 217	if err != nil {
 218		return nil, err
 219	}
 220	appDTO.Versions = getAppVersions(key, details)
 221	tags, err := getAppTags(app.ID, strings.ToLower(common.GetLang(ctx)))
 222	if err != nil {
 223		return nil, err
 224	}
 225	appDTO.Tags = tags
 226	return &appDTO, nil
 227}

 229func (a AppService) GetAppDetailByKey(appKey, version string) (response.AppDetailSimpleDTO, error) {

 231	app, err := appRepo.GetFirst(appRepo.WithKey(appKey))
 232	if err != nil {
 233		return appDetailDTO, err
 234	}
 235	appDetail, err := appDetailRepo.GetFirst(appDetailRepo.WithAppId(app.ID), appDetailRepo.WithVersion(version))
 236	if err != nil {
 237		return appDetailDTO, err
 238	}
 239	appDetailDTO.ID = appDetail.ID
 240	return appDetailDTO, nil

 243func (a AppService) GetAppDetail(appID uint, version, appType string) (response.AppDetailDTO, error) {
 244	var (
 245		appDetailDTO response.AppDetailDTO
 246		opts         []repo.DBOption
 247	)
 248	opts = append(opts, appDetailRepo.WithAppId(appID), appDetailRepo.WithVersion(version))
 249	detail, err := appDetailRepo.GetFirst(opts...)
 250	if err != nil {
 251		return appDetailDTO, err
 252	}
 253	appDetailDTO.AppDetail = detail
 254	appDetailDTO.Enable = true
 255
 256	if appType == "runtime" {
 257		app, err := appRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(appID))
 258		if err != nil {
 259			return appDetailDTO, err
 260		}
 261		fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 262
 263		versionPath := filepath.Join(app.GetAppResourcePath(), detail.Version)
 264		if !fileOp.Stat(versionPath) || detail.Update {
 265			if err = downloadApp(app, detail, nil, nil); err != nil && !fileOp.Stat(versionPath) {
 266				return appDetailDTO, err
 267			}
 268		}
 269		switch app.Type {
 270		case constant.RuntimePHP:
 271			paramsPath := filepath.Join(versionPath, "data.yml")
 272			if !fileOp.Stat(paramsPath) {
 273				return appDetailDTO, buserr.WithDetail("ErrFileNotExist", paramsPath, nil)
 274			}
 275			param, err := fileOp.GetContent(paramsPath)
 276			if err != nil {
 277				return appDetailDTO, err
 278			}
 279			paramMap := make(map[string]interface{})
 280			if err = yaml.Unmarshal(param, &paramMap); err != nil {
 281				return appDetailDTO, err
 282			}
 283			appDetailDTO.Params = paramMap["additionalProperties"]
 284			composePath := filepath.Join(versionPath, "docker-compose.yml")
 285			if !fileOp.Stat(composePath) {
 286				return appDetailDTO, buserr.WithDetail("ErrFileNotExist", composePath, nil)
 287			}
 288			compose, err := fileOp.GetContent(composePath)
 289			if err != nil {
 290				return appDetailDTO, err
 291			}
 292			composeMap := make(map[string]interface{})
 293			if err := yaml.Unmarshal(compose, &composeMap); err != nil {
 294				return appDetailDTO, err
 295			}
 296			if service, ok := composeMap["services"]; ok {
 297				servicesMap := service.(map[string]interface{})
 298				for k := range servicesMap {
 299					appDetailDTO.Image = k
 300				}
 301			}
 302		}
 303	} else {
 304		paramMap := make(map[string]interface{})
 305		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(detail.Params), &paramMap); err != nil {
 306			return appDetailDTO, err
 307		}
 308		appDetailDTO.Params = paramMap
 309	}
 310
 311	if appDetailDTO.DockerCompose == "" {
 312		filename := filepath.Base(appDetailDTO.DownloadUrl)
 313		dockerComposeUrl := fmt.Sprintf("%s%s", strings.TrimSuffix(appDetailDTO.DownloadUrl, filename), "docker-compose.yml")
 314		statusCode, composeRes, err := req_helper.HandleRequest(dockerComposeUrl, http.MethodGet, constant.TimeOut20s)
 315		if statusCode == http.StatusNotFound {
 316			return appDetailDTO, buserr.New("ErrAppVersionUnavailable")
 317		}
 318		if err != nil {
 319			return appDetailDTO, buserr.WithDetail("ErrGetCompose", err.Error(), err)
 320		}
 321		if statusCode > 200 {
 322			return appDetailDTO, buserr.WithDetail("ErrGetCompose", string(composeRes), err)
 323		}
 324		detail.DockerCompose = string(composeRes)
 325		_ = appDetailRepo.Update(context.Background(), detail)
 326		appDetailDTO.DockerCompose = string(composeRes)
 327	}
 328
 329	appDetailDTO.HostMode = isHostModel(appDetailDTO.DockerCompose)
 330
 331	app, err := appRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(detail.AppId))
 332	if err != nil {
 333		return appDetailDTO, err
 334	}
 335	if err := checkLimit(app); err != nil {
 336		appDetailDTO.Enable = false
 337	}
 338	appDetailDTO.Architectures = app.Architectures
 339	appDetailDTO.MemoryRequired = app.MemoryRequired
 340	appDetailDTO.GpuSupport = app.GpuSupport
 341	return appDetailDTO, nil
 342}
 343func (a AppService) GetAppDetailByID(id uint) (*response.AppDetailDTO, error) {
 344	res := &response.AppDetailDTO{}
 345	appDetail, err := appDetailRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(id))
 346	if err != nil {
 347		return nil, err
 348	}
 349	res.AppDetail = appDetail
 350	paramMap := make(map[string]interface{})
 351	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(appDetail.Params), &paramMap); err != nil {
 352		return nil, err
 353	}
 354	res.Params = paramMap
 355	res.HostMode = isHostModel(appDetail.DockerCompose)
 356	return res, nil
 357}

 359func (a AppService) Install(req request.AppInstallCreate, executeScript bool) (appInstall *model.AppInstall, err error) {
 360	return a.installWithHooks(req, executeScript, nil)
 361}
 362
 363func (a AppService) installWithHooks(req request.AppInstallCreate, executeScript bool, hooks *appInstallHooks) (appInstall *model.AppInstall, err error) {
 364	if err = docker.CreateDefaultDockerNetwork(); err != nil {
 365		err = buserr.WithDetail("Err1PanelNetworkFailed", err.Error(), nil)
 366		return
 367	}
 368	if list, _ := appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background(), repo.WithByLowerName(req.Name)); len(list) > 0 {
 369		err = buserr.New("ErrAppNameExist")
 370		return
 371	}
 372	var (
 373		httpPort  int
 374		httpsPort int
 375		appDetail model.AppDetail
 376		app       model.App
 377	)
 378	appDetail, err = appDetailRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(req.AppDetailId))
 379	if err != nil {
 380		return
 381	}
 382	app, err = appRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(appDetail.AppId))
 383	if err != nil {
 384		return
 385	}
 386	if DatabaseKeys[app.Key] > 0 {
 387		if existDatabases, _ := databaseRepo.GetList(repo.WithByName(req.Name)); len(existDatabases) > 0 {
 388			err = buserr.New("ErrRemoteExist")
 389			return
 390		}
 391	}
 392	if hostName, ok := req.Params["PANEL_DB_HOST"]; ok {
 393		database, _ := databaseRepo.Get(repo.WithByName(hostName.(string)))
 394		if database.AppInstallID > 0 {
 395			databaseInstall, _ := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(database.AppInstallID))
 396			if databaseInstall.Status != constant.StatusRunning {
 397				return nil, buserr.WithName("ErrAppIsDown", databaseInstall.Name)
 398			}
 399		}
 400	}
 401	for key := range req.Params {
 402		if !strings.Contains(key, "PANEL_APP_PORT") {
 403			continue
 404		}
 405		var port int
 406		port, err = checkPort(key, req.Params)
 407		if err != nil {
 408			return
 409		}
 410		if key == "PANEL_APP_PORT_HTTP" {
 411			httpPort = port
 412		}
 413		if key == "PANEL_APP_PORT_HTTPS" {
 414			httpsPort = port
 415		}
 416	}
 417
 418	if err = checkRequiredAndLimit(app); err != nil {
 419		return
 420	}
 421
 422	appInstall = &model.AppInstall{
 423		Name:        req.Name,
 424		AppId:       appDetail.AppId,
 425		AppDetailId: appDetail.ID,
 426		Version:     appDetail.Version,
 427		Status:      constant.StatusInstalling,
 428		HttpPort:    httpPort,
 429		HttpsPort:   httpsPort,
 430		App:         app,
 431	}
 432	composeMap := make(map[string]interface{})
 433	var composeRes []byte
 434	if req.EditCompose {
 435		if err = yaml.Unmarshal([]byte(req.DockerCompose), &composeMap); err != nil {
 436			return
 437		}
 438	} else {
 439		if appDetail.DockerCompose == "" {
 440			dockerComposeUrl := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/1panel/%s/%s/docker-compose.yml", global.AppRepoURL(), global.CONF.Base.Mode, app.Key, appDetail.Version)
 441			var statusCode int
 442			statusCode, composeRes, err = req_helper.HandleRequest(dockerComposeUrl, http.MethodGet, constant.TimeOut20s)
 443			if statusCode == http.StatusNotFound {
 444				err = buserr.New("ErrAppVersionUnavailable")
 445				return
 446			}
 447			if err != nil {
 448				return
 449			}
 450			appDetail.DockerCompose = string(composeRes)
 451			_ = appDetailRepo.Update(context.Background(), appDetail)
 452		}
 453		if err = yaml.Unmarshal([]byte(appDetail.DockerCompose), &composeMap); err != nil {
 454			return
 455		}
 456	}
 457
 458	value, ok := composeMap["services"]
 459	if !ok || value == nil {
 460		err = buserr.New("ErrFileParse")
 461		return
 462	}
 463	servicesMap := value.(map[string]interface{})
 464	containerName := constant.ContainerPrefix + app.Key + "-" + common.RandStr(4)
 465	if req.Advanced && req.ContainerName != "" {
 466		containerName = req.ContainerName
 467		appInstalls, _ := appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background(), appInstallRepo.WithContainerName(containerName))
 468		if len(appInstalls) > 0 {
 469			err = buserr.New("ErrContainerName")
 470			return
 471		}
 472		containerExist := false
 473		containerExist, err = checkContainerNameIsExist(req.ContainerName, appInstall.GetPath())
 474		if err != nil {
 475			return
 476		}
 477		if containerExist {
 478			err = buserr.New("ErrContainerName")
 479			return
 480		}
 481	}
 482	req.Params[constant.ContainerName] = containerName
 483	appInstall.ContainerName = containerName
 484
 485	index := 0
 486	serviceName := ""
 487	for k := range servicesMap {
 488		serviceName = k
 489		if index > 0 {
 490			continue
 491		}
 492		index++
 493	}
 494	newServiceName := strings.ToLower(appInstall.Name)
 495	if app.Limit == 0 && newServiceName != serviceName && len(servicesMap) == 1 {
 496		servicesMap[newServiceName] = servicesMap[serviceName]
 497		delete(servicesMap, serviceName)
 498		serviceName = newServiceName
 499	}
 500	appInstall.ServiceName = serviceName
 501
 502	if err = addDockerComposeCommonParam(composeMap, appInstall.ServiceName, req.AppContainerConfig, req.Params); err != nil {
 503		return
 504	}
 505	var (
 506		composeByte []byte
 507		paramByte   []byte
 508	)
 509
 510	composeByte, err = yaml.Marshal(composeMap)
 511	if err != nil {
 512		return
 513	}
 514	appInstall.DockerCompose = string(composeByte)
 515
 516	if hostName, ok := req.Params["PANEL_DB_HOST"]; ok {
 517		database, _ := databaseRepo.Get(repo.WithByName(hostName.(string)))
 518		if !reflect.DeepEqual(database, model.Database{}) {
 519			req.Params["PANEL_DB_HOST"] = database.Address
 520			req.Params["PANEL_DB_PORT"] = database.Port
 521			req.Params["PANEL_DB_HOST_NAME"] = hostName
 522			req.Params["DATABASE_NAME"] = database.Name
 523		}
 524	}
 525	if app.Key == "openresty" {
 526		req.Params["CONTAINER_PACKAGE_URL"] = "http:">//archive.ubuntu.com/ubuntu/"
 527		req.Params["RESTY_ADD_PACKAGE_BUILDDEPS"] = ""
 528		req.Params["RESTY_CONFIG_OPTIONS_MORE"] = ""
 529	}
 530	if app.Key == "openresty" && (app.Resource == "remote" || app.Resource == "custom") && common.CompareVersion(appDetail.Version, "1.27") {
 531		if dir, ok := req.Params["WEBSITE_DIR"]; ok {
 532			siteDir := dir.(string)
 533			if siteDir == "" || !strings.HasPrefix(siteDir, "/") {
 534				siteDir = path.Join(global.Dir.DataDir, dir.(string))
 535			}
 536			req.Params["WEBSITE_DIR"] = siteDir
 537		}
 538	}
 539	paramByte, err = json.Marshal(req.Params)
 540	if err != nil {
 541		return
 542	}
 543	appInstall.Env = string(paramByte)
 544
 545	var maxSort int
 546	global.DB.Model(&model.AppInstall{}).Where("favorite = ?", false).Select("COALESCE(MAX(sort_order),0)").Scan(&maxSort)
 547	appInstall.SortOrder = maxSort + 1
 548
 549	if err = appInstallRepo.Create(context.Background(), appInstall); err != nil {
 550		return
 551	}
 552
 553	installTask, err := task.NewTaskWithOps(appInstall.Name, task.TaskInstall, task.TaskScopeApp, req.TaskID, appInstall.ID)
 554	if err != nil {
 555		return
 556	}
 557
 558	if err = createLink(context.Background(), installTask, app, appInstall, req.Params); err != nil {
 559		return
 560	}
 561
 562	installApp := func(t *task.Task) error {
 563		if err = copyData(t, app, appDetail, appInstall, req); err != nil {
 564			return err
 565		}
 566		if hooks != nil && hooks.AfterCopyData != nil {
 567			if err = hooks.AfterCopyData(appInstall); err != nil {
 568				return err
 569			}
 570		}
 571		if executeScript {
 572			if err = runScript(t, appInstall, "init"); err != nil {
 573				return err
 574			}
 575		}
 576		if app.Key == "openresty" {
 577			if err = handleSiteDir(app, appDetail, req, t); err != nil {
 578				return err
 579			}
 580			if err = handleOpenrestyFile(appInstall); err != nil {
 581				return err
 582			}
 583		}
 584		if err = upApp(t, appInstall, req.PullImage); err != nil {
 585			return err
 586		}
 587		updateToolApp(appInstall)
 588		return nil
 589	}
 590
 591	handleAppStatus := func(t *task.Task) {
 592		appInstall.Status = constant.StatusUpErr
 593		appInstall.Message = installTask.Task.ErrorMsg
 594		_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), appInstall)
 595	}
 596
 597	installTask.AddSubTaskWithOps(task.GetTaskName(appInstall.Name, task.TaskInstall, task.TaskScopeApp), installApp, handleAppStatus, 0, time.Hour)
 598
 599	go func() {
 600		if taskErr := installTask.Execute(); taskErr != nil {
 601			appInstall.Status = constant.StatusInstallErr
 602			appInstall.Message = taskErr.Error()
 603			if strings.Contains(taskErr.Error(), "Timeout") && strings.Contains(taskErr.Error(), "Pulling") {
 604				appInstall.Message = buserr.New("PullImageTimeout").Error() + appInstall.Message
 605			}
 606			_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), appInstall)
 607		}
 608	}()
 609
 610	return
 611}

 613func (a AppService) SyncAppListFromLocal(TaskID string) {
 614	var (
 615		err        error
 616		dirEntries []os.DirEntry
 617		localApps  []model.App
 618	)
 619
 620	syncTask, err := task.NewTaskWithOps(i18n.GetMsgByKey("LocalApp"), task.TaskSync, task.TaskScopeAppStore, TaskID, 0)
 621	if err != nil {
 622		global.LOG.Errorf("Create sync task failed %v", err)
 623		return
 624	}
 625
 626	syncTask.AddSubTask(task.GetTaskName(i18n.GetMsgByKey("LocalApp"), task.TaskSync, task.TaskScopeAppStore), func(t *task.Task) (err error) {
 627		fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 628		localAppDir := global.Dir.LocalAppResourceDir
 629		if !fileOp.Stat(localAppDir) {
 630			return nil
 631		}
 632		dirEntries, err = os.ReadDir(localAppDir)
 633		if err != nil {
 634			return
 635		}
 636		for _, dirEntry := range dirEntries {
 637			if dirEntry.IsDir() {
 638				appDir := filepath.Join(localAppDir, dirEntry.Name())
 639				appDirEntries, err := os.ReadDir(appDir)
 640				if err != nil {
 641					t.Log(i18n.GetWithNameAndErr("ErrAppDirNull", dirEntry.Name(), err))
 642					continue
 643				}
 644				app, err := handleLocalApp(appDir)
 645				if err != nil {
 646					t.Log(i18n.GetWithNameAndErr("LocalAppErr", dirEntry.Name(), err))
 647					continue
 648				}
 649				var appDetails []model.AppDetail
 650				for _, appDirEntry := range appDirEntries {
 651					if appDirEntry.IsDir() {
 652						appDetail := model.AppDetail{
 653							Version: appDirEntry.Name(),
 654							Status:  constant.AppNormal,
 655						}
 656						versionDir := filepath.Join(appDir, appDirEntry.Name())
 657						if err = handleLocalAppDetail(versionDir, &appDetail); err != nil {
 658							t.Log(i18n.GetMsgWithMap("LocalAppVersionErr", map[string]interface{}{"name": app.Name, "version": appDetail.Version, "err": err.Error()}))
 659							continue
 660						}
 661						appDetails = append(appDetails, appDetail)
 662					}
 663				}
 664				if len(appDetails) > 0 {
 665					app.Details = appDetails
 666					localApps = append(localApps, *app)
 667				} else {
 668					t.Log(i18n.GetWithName("LocalAppVersionNull", app.Name))
 669				}
 670			}
 671		}
 672
 673		var (
 674			newApps    []model.App
 675			deleteApps []model.App
 676			updateApps []model.App
 677			oldAppIds  []uint
 678
 679			deleteAppIds     []uint
 680			deleteAppDetails []model.AppDetail
 681			newAppDetails    []model.AppDetail
 682			updateDetails    []model.AppDetail
 683
 684			appTags []*model.AppTag
 685		)
 686
 687		oldApps, _ := appRepo.GetBy(appRepo.WithResource(constant.AppResourceLocal))
 688		apps := make(map[string]model.App, len(oldApps))
 689		for _, old := range oldApps {
 690			old.Status = constant.AppTakeDown
 691			apps[old.Key] = old
 692		}
 693		for _, app := range localApps {
 694			if oldApp, ok := apps[app.Key]; ok {
 695				app.ID = oldApp.ID
 696				appDetails := make(map[string]model.AppDetail, len(oldApp.Details))
 697				for _, old := range oldApp.Details {
 698					old.Status = constant.AppTakeDown
 699					appDetails[old.Version] = old
 700				}
 701				for i, newDetail := range app.Details {
 702					version := newDetail.Version
 703					newDetail.Status = constant.AppNormal
 704					newDetail.AppId = app.ID
 705					oldDetail, exist := appDetails[version]
 706					if exist {
 707						newDetail.ID = oldDetail.ID
 708						delete(appDetails, version)
 709					}
 710					app.Details[i] = newDetail
 711				}
 712				for _, v := range appDetails {
 713					app.Details = append(app.Details, v)
 714				}
 715			}
 716			app.TagsKey = append(app.TagsKey, constant.AppResourceLocal)
 717			apps[app.Key] = app
 718		}
 719
 720		for _, app := range apps {
 721			if app.ID == 0 {
 722				newApps = append(newApps, app)
 723			} else {
 724				oldAppIds = append(oldAppIds, app.ID)
 725				if app.Status == constant.AppTakeDown {
 726					installs, _ := appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background(), appInstallRepo.WithAppId(app.ID))
 727					if len(installs) > 0 {
 728						updateApps = append(updateApps, app)
 729						continue
 730					}
 731					deleteAppIds = append(deleteAppIds, app.ID)
 732					deleteApps = append(deleteApps, app)
 733					deleteAppDetails = append(deleteAppDetails, app.Details...)
 734				} else {
 735					updateApps = append(updateApps, app)
 736				}
 737			}
 738
 739		}
 740
 741		tags, _ := tagRepo.All()
 742		tagMap := make(map[string]uint, len(tags))
 743		for _, tag := range tags {
 744			tagMap[tag.Key] = tag.ID
 745		}
 746
 747		tx, ctx := getTxAndContext()
 748		defer tx.Rollback()
 749		if len(newApps) > 0 {
 750			if err = appRepo.BatchCreate(ctx, newApps); err != nil {
 751				return
 752			}
 753		}
 754		for _, update := range updateApps {
 755			if err = appRepo.Save(ctx, &update); err != nil {
 756				return
 757			}
 758		}
 759		if len(deleteApps) > 0 {
 760			if err = appRepo.BatchDelete(ctx, deleteApps); err != nil {
 761				return
 762			}
 763			if err = appDetailRepo.DeleteByAppIds(ctx, deleteAppIds); err != nil {
 764				return
 765			}
 766		}
 767
 768		if err = appTagRepo.DeleteByAppIds(ctx, oldAppIds); err != nil {
 769			return
 770		}
 771		for _, newApp := range newApps {
 772			if newApp.ID > 0 {
 773				for _, detail := range newApp.Details {
 774					detail.AppId = newApp.ID
 775					newAppDetails = append(newAppDetails, detail)
 776				}
 777			}
 778		}
 779		for _, update := range updateApps {
 780			for _, detail := range update.Details {
 781				if detail.ID == 0 {
 782					detail.AppId = update.ID
 783					newAppDetails = append(newAppDetails, detail)
 784				} else {
 785					if detail.Status == constant.AppNormal {
 786						updateDetails = append(updateDetails, detail)
 787					} else {
 788						deleteAppDetails = append(deleteAppDetails, detail)
 789					}
 790				}
 791			}
 792		}
 793
 794		allApps := append(newApps, updateApps...)
 795		for _, app := range allApps {
 796			for _, t := range app.TagsKey {
 797				tagId, ok := tagMap[t]
 798				if ok {
 799					appTags = append(appTags, &model.AppTag{
 800						AppId: app.ID,
 801						TagId: tagId,
 802					})
 803				}
 804			}
 805		}
 806
 807		if len(newAppDetails) > 0 {
 808			if err = appDetailRepo.BatchCreate(ctx, newAppDetails); err != nil {
 809				return
 810			}
 811		}
 812
 813		for _, updateAppDetail := range updateDetails {
 814			if err = appDetailRepo.Update(ctx, updateAppDetail); err != nil {
 815				return
 816			}
 817		}
 818
 819		if len(deleteAppDetails) > 0 {
 820			if err = appDetailRepo.BatchDelete(ctx, deleteAppDetails); err != nil {
 821				return
 822			}
 823		}
 824
 825		if len(oldAppIds) > 0 {
 826			if err = appTagRepo.DeleteByAppIds(ctx, oldAppIds); err != nil {
 827				return
 828			}
 829		}
 830
 831		if len(appTags) > 0 {
 832			if err = appTagRepo.BatchCreate(ctx, appTags); err != nil {
 833				return
 834			}
 835		}
 836		tx.Commit()
 837		global.LOG.Infof("Synchronization of local applications completed")
 838		return nil
 839	}, nil)
 840	go func() {
 841		_ = syncTask.Execute()
 842	}()
 843}

 845func (a AppService) GetAppUpdate() (*response.AppUpdateRes, error) {
 846	res := &response.AppUpdateRes{
 847		CanUpdate: false,
 848	}
 849	mysql, _ := appRepo.GetFirst(appRepo.WithKey("mysql"))
 850	if !mysql.BatchInstallSupport {
 851		res.CanUpdate = true
 852		return res, nil
 853	}
 854
 855	versionUrl := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/1panel.json.version.txt", global.AppRepoURL(), global.CONF.Base.Mode)
 856	_, versionRes, err := req_helper.HandleRequest(versionUrl, http.MethodGet, constant.TimeOut20s)
 857	if err != nil {
 858		return nil, err
 859	}
 860	lastModifiedStr := string(versionRes)
 861	lastModified, err := strconv.Atoi(lastModifiedStr)
 862	if err != nil {
 863		return nil, err
 864	}
 865	setting, err := NewISettingService().GetSettingInfo()
 866	if err != nil {
 867		return nil, err
 868	}
 869	if setting.AppStoreSyncStatus == constant.StatusSyncing {
 870		res.IsSyncing = true
 871		return res, nil
 872	}
 873
 874	appStoreLastModified, _ := strconv.Atoi(setting.AppStoreLastModified)
 875	res.AppStoreLastModified = appStoreLastModified
 876	if setting.AppStoreLastModified == "" || lastModified != appStoreLastModified {
 877		res.CanUpdate = true
 878		return res, err
 879	}
 880	apps, _ := appRepo.GetBy(appRepo.WithResource(constant.AppResourceRemote))
 881	for _, app := range apps {
 882		if app.Icon == "" {
 883			res.CanUpdate = true
 884			return res, err
 885		}
 886		if appicon.IsIconFile(app.Icon) {
 887			fileName, _ := appicon.ParseIconField(app.Icon)
 888			if fileName == "" || !appicon.IconFileExists(fileName) {
 889				res.CanUpdate = true
 890				return res, err
 891			}
 892		}
 893	}
 894
 895	list, err := getAppList()
 896	if err != nil {
 897		return res, err
 898	}
 899	if list.Extra.Version != "" && setting.SystemVersion != list.Extra.Version && !common.CompareVersion(setting.SystemVersion, list.Extra.Version) {
 900		global.LOG.Errorf("The current version %s is too low to synchronize with the App Store. The minimum required version is %s", setting.SystemVersion, list.Extra.Version)
 901		return nil, buserr.New("ErrVersionTooLow")
 902	}
 903	res.AppList = list
 904	return res, nil
 905}
 906
 907func getAppFromRepo(downloadPath string) error {
 908	downloadUrl := downloadPath
 909	global.LOG.Infof("[AppStore] download file from %s", downloadUrl)
 910	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 911	packagePath := filepath.Join(global.Dir.ResourceDir, filepath.Base(downloadUrl))
 912	if err := files.DownloadFileWithProxy(downloadUrl, packagePath); err != nil {
 913		return err
 914	}
 915
 916	if err := fileOp.Decompress(context.Background(), packagePath, global.Dir.ResourceDir, files.SdkZip, ""); err != nil {
 917		return err
 918	}
 919	defer func() {
 920		_ = fileOp.DeleteFile(packagePath)
 921	}()
 922	return nil
 923}
 924
 925func getAppList() (*dto.AppList, error) {
 926	list := &dto.AppList{}
 927	if err := getAppFromRepo(fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/1panel.json.zip", global.AppRepoURL(), global.CONF.Base.Mode)); err != nil {
 928		return nil, err
 929	}
 930	listFile := filepath.Join(global.Dir.ResourceDir, "1panel.json")
 931	file, err := os.Open(listFile)
 932	if err != nil {
 933		return nil, err
 934	}
 935	defer file.Close()
 936	if err = json.NewDecoder(file).Decode(list); err != nil {
 937		return nil, err
 938	}
 939
 940	return list, nil
 941}

 949func deleteCustomApp() {
 950	installs, err := appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background())
 951	if err != nil {
 952		global.LOG.Errorf("[AppStore] deleteCustomApp: failed to list installs, skipping: %v", err)
 953		return
 954	}
 955	var appIDS []uint
 956	for _, install := range installs {
 957		appIDS = append(appIDS, install.AppId)
 958	}
 959	var ops []repo.DBOption
 960	if len(appIDS) > 0 {
 961		ops = append(ops, repo.WithByIDNotIn(appIDS))
 962	}
 963	apps, err := appRepo.GetBy(ops...)
 964	if err != nil {
 965		global.LOG.Errorf("[AppStore] deleteCustomApp: failed to get apps, skipping: %v", err)
 966		return
 967	}
 968	var deleteIDS []uint
 969	for _, app := range apps {
 970		if app.Resource == constant.AppResourceCustom {
 971			deleteIDS = append(deleteIDS, app.ID)
 972		}
 973	}
 974	if len(deleteIDS) == 0 {
 975		return
 976	}
 977	if err = appRepo.DeleteByIDs(context.Background(), deleteIDS); err != nil {
 978		global.LOG.Errorf("[AppStore] deleteCustomApp: failed to delete apps: %v", err)
 979	}
 980	if err = appDetailRepo.DeleteByAppIds(context.Background(), deleteIDS); err != nil {
 981		global.LOG.Errorf("[AppStore] deleteCustomApp: failed to delete app details: %v", err)
 982	}
 983}

 985func (a AppService) SyncAppListFromRemote(taskID string) (err error) {
 986	if xpack.MultiNodeProvider.IsUseCustomApp() {
 987		return nil
 988	}
 989
 990	appStoreSyncMu.Lock()
 991	global.LOG.Info("[AppStore] sync app from remote task create start")
 992	if appStoreSyncing {
 993		appStoreSyncMu.Unlock()
 994		global.LOG.Info("[AppStore] sync already in progress, skipping")
 995		return nil
 996	}
 997	appStoreSyncing = true
 998	appStoreSyncMu.Unlock()
 999
1000	syncTask, err := task.NewTaskWithOps(i18n.GetMsgByKey("App"), task.TaskSync, task.TaskScopeAppStore, taskID, 0)
1001	if err != nil {
1002		appStoreSyncMu.Lock()
1003		appStoreSyncing = false
1004		appStoreSyncMu.Unlock()
1005		return err
1006	}
1007
1008	var sharedCtx *appSyncContext
1009
1010	syncTask.AddSubTask(task.GetTaskName(i18n.GetMsgByKey("App"), task.TaskSync, task.TaskScopeAppStore), a.createSyncAppStoreTask(&sharedCtx), nil)
1011	syncTask.AddSubTask(i18n.GetMsgByKey("SyncAppDetail"), a.createSyncAppStoreMetaTask(&sharedCtx), nil)
1012
1013	go func() {
1014		defer func() {
1015			if r := recover(); r != nil {
1016				global.LOG.Errorf("[AppStore] sync goroutine recovered from panic: %v", r)
1017				if updateErr := NewISettingService().Update("AppStoreSyncStatus", constant.StatusError); updateErr != nil {
1018					global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] failed to update sync status after panic: %v", updateErr)
1019				}
1020			}
1021			appStoreSyncMu.Lock()
1022			appStoreSyncing = false
1023			appStoreSyncMu.Unlock()
1024		}()
1025		if err := syncTask.Execute(); err != nil {
1026			if updateErr := NewISettingService().Update("AppStoreLastModified", "0"); updateErr != nil {
1027				global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] failed to reset last modified: %v", updateErr)
1028			}
1029			if updateErr := NewISettingService().Update("AppStoreSyncStatus", constant.StatusError); updateErr != nil {
1030				global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] failed to update sync status to error: %v", updateErr)
1031			}
1032			return
1033		}
1034	}()
1035
1036	global.LOG.Info("[AppStore] sync app from remote task create ok")
1037	return nil
1038}
1039
1040func (a AppService) GetAppIcon(key string) ([]byte, string, string, error) {
1041	app, err := appRepo.GetFirst(appRepo.WithKey(key))
1042	if err != nil {
1043		return nil, "", "", err
1044	}
1045
1046	if appicon.IsIconFile(app.Icon) {
1047		fileName, etag := appicon.ParseIconField(app.Icon)
1048		iconBytes, err := appicon.ReadIconFile(fileName)
1049		if err != nil {
1050			global.LOG.Warnf("[AppIcon] read icon file failed key=%s, file=%s, err=%v", key, fileName, err)
1051			return nil, "", "", nil
1052		}
1053		return iconBytes, fileName, etag, nil
1054	}
1055
1056	iconBytes, err := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(app.Icon)
1057	if err != nil {
1058		global.LOG.Warnf("[AppIcon] decode base64 icon failed key=%s, err=%v", key, err)
1059		return nil, "", "", nil
1060	}
1061	return iconBytes, "", "", nil
1062}

  19"cmt">// AppIgnoreUpgradeService (struct)
  20type AppIgnoreUpgradeService struct {
  21}
  22
  23"cmt">// IAppIgnoreUpgradeService (interface)
  24type IAppIgnoreUpgradeService interface {
  25	List() ([]response.AppIgnoreUpgradeDTO, error)
  26	CreateAppIgnore(req request.AppIgnoreUpgradeReq) error
  27	Delete(req request.ReqWithID) error
  28}
  29
  30func NewIAppIgnoreUpgradeService() IAppIgnoreUpgradeService {
  31	return AppIgnoreUpgradeService{}
  32}
  33
  34func (a AppIgnoreUpgradeService) List() ([]response.AppIgnoreUpgradeDTO, error) {
  35	var res []response.AppIgnoreUpgradeDTO
  36	ignores, err := appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.List()
  37	if err != nil {
  38		return nil, err
  39	}
  40	for _, ignore := range ignores {
  41		dto := response.AppIgnoreUpgradeDTO{
  42			ID:          ignore.ID,
  43			AppID:       ignore.AppID,
  44			AppDetailID: ignore.AppDetailID,
  45			Scope:       ignore.Scope,
  46		}
  47		app, err := appRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(ignore.AppID))
  48		if errors.Is(err, gorm.ErrRecordNotFound) {
  49			_ = appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.Delete(repo.WithByID(ignore.ID))
  50			continue
  51		}
  52		dto.Name = app.Name
  53		if ignore.Scope == "version" {
  54			appDetail, err := appDetailRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(ignore.AppDetailID))
  55			if errors.Is(err, gorm.ErrRecordNotFound) {
  56				_ = appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.Delete(repo.WithByID(ignore.ID))
  57				continue
  58			}
  59			dto.Version = appDetail.Version
  60		}
  61		res = append(res, dto)
  62	}
  63	return res, nil
  64}
  65
  66func (a AppIgnoreUpgradeService) CreateAppIgnore(req request.AppIgnoreUpgradeReq) error {
  67	appIgnoreUpgrade := model.AppIgnoreUpgrade{
  68		AppID: req.AppID,
  69		Scope: req.Scope,
  70	}
  71	if req.Scope == "version" {
  72		appIgnoreUpgrade.AppDetailID = req.AppDetailID
  73	}
  74	if req.Scope == "all" {
  75		_ = appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.Delete(appInstallRepo.WithAppId(req.AppID))
  76	}
  77	if err := appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.Create(&appIgnoreUpgrade); err != nil {
  78		return err
  79	}
  80	return nil
  81}
  82
  83func (a AppIgnoreUpgradeService) Delete(req request.ReqWithID) error {
  84	return appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.Delete(repo.WithByID(req.ID))
  85}

  45"cmt">// AppInstallService (struct)
  46type AppInstallService struct {
  47}

  49"cmt">// IAppInstallService (interface)
  50type IAppInstallService interface {
  51	Page(req request.AppInstalledSearch) (int64, []response.AppInstallDTO, error)
  52	CheckExist(req request.AppInstalledInfo) (*response.AppInstalledCheck, error)
  53	LoadPort(req dto.OperationWithNameAndType) (int64, error)
  54	LoadConnInfo(req dto.OperationWithNameAndType) (response.DatabaseConn, error)
  55	SearchForWebsite(req request.AppInstalledSearch) ([]response.AppInstallDTO, error)
  56	Operate(req request.AppInstalledOperate) error
  57	Update(req request.AppInstalledUpdate) error
  58	SyncAll(systemInit bool) error
  59	GetServices(key string) ([]response.AppService, error)
  60	GetUpdateVersions(req request.AppUpdateVersion) ([]dto.AppVersion, error)
  61	GetParams(id uint) (*response.AppConfig, error)
  62	ChangeAppPort(req request.PortUpdate) error
  63	GetDefaultConfigByKey(key, name string) (string, error)
  64	DeleteCheck(installId uint) ([]dto.AppResource, error)
  65
  66	UpdateAppConfig(req request.AppConfigUpdate) error
  67	GetInstallList() ([]dto.AppInstallInfo, error)
  68	GetAppInstallInfo(appInstallID uint) (*response.AppInstallInfo, error)
  69	UpdateSort(req request.AppInstallSort) error
  70}
  71
  72func NewIAppInstalledService() IAppInstallService {
  73	return &AppInstallService{}
  74}

  76func (a *AppInstallService) GetInstallList() ([]dto.AppInstallInfo, error) {
  77	var datas []dto.AppInstallInfo
  78	appInstalls, err := appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background())
  79	if err != nil {
  80		return nil, err
  81	}
  82	for _, install := range appInstalls {
  83		datas = append(datas, dto.AppInstallInfo{ID: install.ID, Key: install.App.Key, Name: install.Name})
  84	}
  85	return datas, nil
  86}
  87
  88func (a *AppInstallService) Page(req request.AppInstalledSearch) (int64, []response.AppInstallDTO, error) {
  89	var (
  90		opts     []repo.DBOption
  91		total    int64
  92		installs []model.AppInstall
  93		err      error
  94	)
  95	opts = append(opts, repo.WithOrderRuleBy("favorite", "descending"))
  96	opts = append(opts, repo.WithOrderRuleBy("sort_order", "ascending"))
  97
  98	if req.Name != "" {
  99		opts = append(opts, repo.WithByLikeName(req.Name))
 100	}
 101
 102	if len(req.Tags) != 0 {
 103		tags, err := tagRepo.GetByKeys(req.Tags)
 104		if err != nil {
 105			return 0, nil, err
 106		}
 107		var tagIds []uint
 108		for _, t := range tags {
 109			tagIds = append(tagIds, t.ID)
 110		}
 111		appTags, err := appTagRepo.GetByTagIds(tagIds)
 112		if err != nil {
 113			return 0, nil, err
 114		}
 115		var appIds []uint
 116		for _, t := range appTags {
 117			appIds = append(appIds, t.AppId)
 118		}
 119
 120		opts = append(opts, appInstallRepo.WithAppIdsIn(appIds))
 121	}
 122
 123	if req.Update {
 124		installs, err = appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background(), opts...)
 125		if err != nil {
 126			return 0, nil, err
 127		}
 128	} else {
 129		total, installs, err = appInstallRepo.Page(req.Page, req.PageSize, opts...)
 130		if err != nil {
 131			return 0, nil, err
 132		}
 133	}
 134
 135	installDTOs, _ := handleInstalled(installs, req.Update, req.Sync, req.CheckUpdate)
 136	if req.Update {
 137		total = int64(len(installDTOs))
 138	}
 139	return total, installDTOs, nil
 140}
 141
 142func (a *AppInstallService) CheckExist(req request.AppInstalledInfo) (*response.AppInstalledCheck, error) {
 143	res := &response.AppInstalledCheck{
 144		IsExist: false,
 145	}
 146
 147	app, err := appRepo.GetFirst(appRepo.WithKey(req.Key))
 148	if err != nil {
 149		return res, nil
 150	}
 151	res.App = app.Name
 152
 153	var appInstall model.AppInstall
 154	if len(req.Name) == 0 {
 155		appInstall, _ = appInstallRepo.GetFirst(appInstallRepo.WithAppId(app.ID))
 156	} else {
 157		appInstall, _ = appInstallRepo.GetFirst(appInstallRepo.WithAppId(app.ID), repo.WithByName(req.Name))
 158	}
 159
 160	if reflect.DeepEqual(appInstall, model.AppInstall{}) {
 161		return res, nil
 162	}
 163	if err = syncAppInstallStatus(&appInstall, false); err != nil {
 164		return nil, err
 165	}
 166
 167	res.ContainerName = appInstall.ContainerName
 168	res.Name = appInstall.Name
 169	res.Version = appInstall.Version
 170	res.CreatedAt = appInstall.CreatedAt
 171	res.Status = appInstall.Status
 172	res.AppInstallID = appInstall.ID
 173	res.IsExist = true
 174	res.InstallPath = path.Join(global.Dir.AppInstallDir, appInstall.App.Key, appInstall.Name)
 175	res.HttpPort = appInstall.HttpPort
 176	res.HttpsPort = appInstall.HttpsPort
 177
 178	if appInstall.App.Key == "openresty" {
 179		websiteDir, _ := settingRepo.GetValueByKey("WEBSITE_DIR")
 180		res.WebsiteDir = websiteDir
 181	}
 182
 183	return res, nil
 184}
 185
 186func (a *AppInstallService) LoadPort(req dto.OperationWithNameAndType) (int64, error) {
 187	app, err := appInstallRepo.LoadBaseInfo(req.Type, req.Name)
 188	if err != nil {
 189		return int64(0), nil
 190	}
 191	return app.Port, nil
 192}
 193
 194func (a *AppInstallService) LoadConnInfo(req dto.OperationWithNameAndType) (response.DatabaseConn, error) {
 195	var data response.DatabaseConn
 196	app, err := appInstallRepo.LoadBaseInfo(req.Type, req.Name)
 197	if err != nil {
 198		return data, nil
 199	}
 200	data.Status = app.Status
 201	data.Username = app.UserName
 202	data.Password = app.Password
 203	data.ServiceName = app.ServiceName
 204	data.Port = app.Port
 205	data.ContainerName = app.ContainerName
 206	return data, nil
 207}
