 209func (a *AppInstallService) SearchForWebsite(req request.AppInstalledSearch) ([]response.AppInstallDTO, error) {
 210	var (
 211		installs []model.AppInstall
 212		err      error
 213		opts     []repo.DBOption
 214	)
 215	if req.Type != "" {
 216		if req.Type == "proxy" {
 217			phpApps, _ := appRepo.GetBy(appRepo.WithType("php"))
 218			var ids []uint
 219			for _, app := range phpApps {
 220				ids = append(ids, app.ID)
 221			}
 222			runtimeApps, _ := appRepo.GetBy(appRepo.WithType("runtime"))
 223			for _, app := range runtimeApps {
 224				ids = append(ids, app.ID)
 225			}
 226			opts = append(opts, appInstallRepo.WithAppIdsNotIn(ids))
 227		} else {
 228			apps, err := appRepo.GetBy(appRepo.WithType(req.Type))
 229			if err != nil {
 230				return nil, err
 231			}
 232			var ids []uint
 233			for _, app := range apps {
 234				ids = append(ids, app.ID)
 235			}
 236			if req.Unused {
 237				opts = append(opts, appInstallRepo.WithIdNotInWebsite())
 238			}
 239			opts = append(opts, appInstallRepo.WithAppIdsIn(ids))
 240		}
 241		installs, err = appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background(), opts...)
 242		if err != nil {
 243			return nil, err
 244		}
 245	} else {
 246		installs, err = appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background())
 247		if err != nil {
 248			return nil, err
 249		}
 250	}
 251
 252	return handleInstalled(installs, false, true, false)
 253}
 254
 255func (a *AppInstallService) Operate(req request.AppInstalledOperate) error {
 256	install, err := appInstallRepo.GetFirstByCtx(context.Background(), repo.WithByID(req.InstallId))
 257	if err != nil {
 258		return err
 259	}
 260	if !req.ForceDelete && !files.NewFileOp().Stat(install.GetPath()) {
 261		return buserr.New("ErrInstallDirNotFound")
 262	}
 263	dockerComposePath := install.GetComposePath()
 264	switch req.Operate {
 265	case constant.Rebuild:
 266		return rebuildApp(install)
 267	case constant.Start:
 268		out, err := compose.Up(dockerComposePath)
 269		if err != nil {
 270			return handleErr(install, err, out)
 271		}
 272		return syncAppInstallStatus(&install, false)
 273	case constant.Stop:
 274		out, err := compose.Stop(dockerComposePath)
 275		if err != nil {
 276			return handleErr(install, err, out)
 277		}
 278		return syncAppInstallStatus(&install, false)
 279	case constant.Restart:
 280		out, err := compose.Restart(dockerComposePath)
 281		if err != nil {
 282			return handleErr(install, err, out)
 283		}
 284		return syncAppInstallStatus(&install, false)
 285	case constant.Delete:
 286		deleteReq := request.AppInstallDelete{
 287			Install:      install,
 288			DeleteBackup: req.DeleteBackup,
 289			ForceDelete:  req.ForceDelete,
 290			DeleteDB:     req.DeleteDB,
 291			DeleteImage:  req.DeleteImage,
 292			TaskID:       req.TaskID,
 293		}
 294		if err = deleteAppInstall(deleteReq); err != nil && !req.ForceDelete {
 295			return err
 296		}
 297		return nil
 298	case constant.Sync:
 299		return syncAppInstallStatus(&install, true)
 300	case constant.Upgrade:
 301		upgradeReq := request.AppInstallUpgrade{
 302			InstallID:     install.ID,
 303			DetailID:      req.DetailId,
 304			Backup:        req.Backup,
 305			PullImage:     req.PullImage,
 306			DeleteImage:   req.DeleteImage,
 307			DockerCompose: req.DockerCompose,
 308			TaskID:        req.TaskID,
 309		}
 310		return upgradeInstall(upgradeReq)
 311	case constant.Reload:
 312		return opNginx(install.ContainerName, constant.NginxReload)
 313	case constant.Favorite:
 314		install.Favorite = req.Favorite
 315		var maxSort int
 316		global.DB.Model(&model.AppInstall{}).Where("favorite = ?", req.Favorite).Select("COALESCE(MAX(sort_order),0)").Scan(&maxSort)
 317		install.SortOrder = maxSort + 1
 318		return appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &install)
 319	default:
 320		return errors.New("operate not support")
 321	}
 322}
 323
 324func (a *AppInstallService) UpdateAppConfig(req request.AppConfigUpdate) error {
 325	installed, err := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(req.InstallID))
 326	if err != nil {
 327		return err
 328	}
 329	installed.WebUI = ""
 330	if req.WebUI != "" {
 331		installed.WebUI = req.WebUI
 332	}
 333	return appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &installed)
 334}
 335
 336func (a *AppInstallService) UpdateSort(req request.AppInstallSort) error {
 337	for _, item := range req.Items {
 338		if err := appInstallRepo.BatchUpdateBy(map[string]interface{}{"sort_order": item.SortOrder}, repo.WithByID(item.InstallID)); err != nil {
 339			return err
 340		}
 341	}
 342	return nil
 343}
 344
 345func (a *AppInstallService) Update(req request.AppInstalledUpdate) error {
 346	installed, err := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(req.InstallId))
 347	if err != nil {
 348		return err
 349	}
 350	oldInstalled := installed
 351	port, ok := req.Params["PANEL_APP_PORT_HTTP"]
 352	if ok {
 353		portN := int(math.Ceil(port.(float64)))
 354		if portN != installed.HttpPort {
 355			httpPort, err := checkPort("PANEL_APP_PORT_HTTP", req.Params)
 356			if err != nil {
 357				return err
 358			}
 359			installed.HttpPort = httpPort
 360		}
 361	}
 362	ports, ok := req.Params["PANEL_APP_PORT_HTTPS"]
 363	if ok {
 364		portN := int(math.Ceil(ports.(float64)))
 365		if portN != installed.HttpsPort {
 366			httpsPort, err := checkPort("PANEL_APP_PORT_HTTPS", req.Params)
 367			if err != nil {
 368				return err
 369			}
 370			installed.HttpsPort = httpsPort
 371		}
 372	}
 373
 374	backupDockerCompose := installed.DockerCompose
 375	if req.Advanced {
 376		composeMap := make(map[string]interface{})
 377		if req.EditCompose {
 378			if err = yaml.Unmarshal([]byte(req.DockerCompose), &composeMap); err != nil {
 379				return err
 380			}
 381		} else {
 382			if err = yaml.Unmarshal([]byte(installed.DockerCompose), &composeMap); err != nil {
 383				return err
 384			}
 385		}
 386		if err = addDockerComposeCommonParam(composeMap, installed.ServiceName, req.AppContainerConfig, req.Params); err != nil {
 387			return err
 388		}
 389		composeByte, err := yaml.Marshal(composeMap)
 390		if err != nil {
 391			return err
 392		}
 393		installed.DockerCompose = string(composeByte)
 394		if req.ContainerName == "" {
 395			req.Params[constant.ContainerName] = installed.ContainerName
 396		} else {
 397			req.Params[constant.ContainerName] = req.ContainerName
 398			if installed.ContainerName != req.ContainerName {
 399				exist, _ := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(appInstallRepo.WithContainerName(req.ContainerName), appInstallRepo.WithIDNotIs(installed.ID))
 400				if exist.ID > 0 {
 401					return buserr.New("ErrContainerName")
 402				}
 403				containerExist, err := checkContainerNameIsExist(req.ContainerName, installed.GetPath())
 404				if err != nil {
 405					return err
 406				}
 407				if containerExist {
 408					return buserr.New("ErrContainerName")
 409				}
 410				installed.ContainerName = req.ContainerName
 411			}
 412		}
 413	}
 414
 415	envPath := path.Join(installed.GetPath(), ".env")
 416	oldEnvMaps, err := godotenv.Read(envPath)
 417	if err != nil {
 418		return err
 419	}
 420	backupEnvMaps := oldEnvMaps
 421	handleMap(req.Params, oldEnvMaps)
 422	paramByte, err := json.Marshal(oldEnvMaps)
 423	if err != nil {
 424		return err
 425	}
 426	installed.Env = string(paramByte)
 427	if err := env.Write(oldEnvMaps, envPath); err != nil {
 428		return err
 429	}
 430	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 431	_ = fileOp.WriteFile(installed.GetComposePath(), strings.NewReader(installed.DockerCompose), constant.DirPerm)
 432	if err := rebuildApp(installed); err != nil {
 433		_ = env.Write(backupEnvMaps, envPath)
 434		_ = fileOp.WriteFile(installed.GetComposePath(), strings.NewReader(backupDockerCompose), constant.DirPerm)
 435		return err
 436	}
 437	installed.Status = constant.StatusRunning
 438	_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &installed)
 439
 440	proxyChanged := hasAppInstallProxyPassChanged(&oldInstalled, &installed)
 441	currentProxy, currentProxyErr := getAppInstallProxyPass(&installed)
 442	website, _ := websiteRepo.GetFirst(websiteRepo.WithAppInstallId(installed.ID))
 443	if proxyChanged && website.ID != 0 && currentProxyErr == nil {
 444		website.Proxy = currentProxy
 445		if err := websiteRepo.SaveWithoutCtx(&website); err != nil {
 446			global.LOG.Error(buserr.WithErr("ErrUpdateBuWebsite", err).Error())
 447		}
 448	}
 449	if proxyChanged && website.ID != 0 && website.Status == constant.StatusRunning && currentProxyErr == nil {
 450		go func(website model.Website, proxy string) {
 451			nginxInstall, err := getNginxFull(&website)
 452			if err != nil {
 453				global.LOG.Error(buserr.WithErr("ErrUpdateBuWebsite", err).Error())
 454				return
 455			}
 456			config := nginxInstall.SiteConfig.Config
 457			servers := config.FindServers()
 458			if len(servers) == 0 {
 459				global.LOG.Error(buserr.WithErr("ErrUpdateBuWebsite", errors.New("nginx config is not valid")).Error())
 460				return
 461			}
 462			server := servers[0]
 463			server.UpdateRootProxy([]string{proxy})
 464
 465			if err := nginx.WriteConfig(config, nginx.IndentedStyle); err != nil {
 466				global.LOG.Error(buserr.WithErr("ErrUpdateBuWebsite", err).Error())
 467				return
 468			}
 469			if err := nginxCheckAndReload(nginxInstall.SiteConfig.OldContent, config.FilePath, nginxInstall.Install.ContainerName); err != nil {
 470				global.LOG.Error(buserr.WithErr("ErrUpdateBuWebsite", err).Error())
 471				return
 472			}
 473		}(website, currentProxy)
 474	}
 475	if proxyChanged && website.ID != 0 && currentProxyErr != nil {
 476		global.LOG.Error(buserr.WithErr("ErrUpdateBuWebsite", currentProxyErr).Error())
 477	}
 478	return nil
 479}
 480
 481func (a *AppInstallService) SyncAll(systemInit bool) error {
 482	allList, err := appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background())
 483	if err != nil {
 484		return err
 485	}
 486	for _, i := range allList {
 487		if i.Status == constant.StatusInstalling || i.Status == constant.StatusUpgrading || i.Status == constant.StatusRebuilding || i.Status == constant.StatusUninstalling {
 488			if systemInit {
 489				i.Status = constant.StatusError
 490				i.Message = "1Panel restart causes the task to terminate"
 491				_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &i)
 492			}
 493			continue
 494		}
 495		if i.Status == constant.StatusWaitingRestart {
 496			dockerComposePath := i.GetComposePath()
 497			_, _ = compose.Up(dockerComposePath)
 498			i.Status = constant.StatusRunning
 499			_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &i)
 500			continue
 501		}
 502		if !systemInit {
 503			if err = syncAppInstallStatus(&i, false); err != nil {
 504				global.LOG.Errorf("sync install app[%s] error,mgs: %s", i.Name, err.Error())
 505			}
 506		}
 507	}
 508	return nil
 509}
 510
 511func (a *AppInstallService) GetServices(key string) ([]response.AppService, error) {
 512	var res []response.AppService
 513	if DatabaseKeys[key] > 0 {
 514		types := []string{key}
 515		if key == constant.AppPostgres {
 516			key = constant.AppPostgresql
 517		}
 518		switch key {
 519		case constant.AppMysql:
 520			types = []string{constant.AppMysql, constant.AppMysqlCluster}
 521		case constant.AppPostgresql:
 522			types = []string{constant.AppPostgresql, constant.AppPostgresqlCluster}
 523		case constant.AppRedis:
 524			types = []string{constant.AppRedis, constant.AppRedisCluster}
 525		}
 526
 527		dbs, _ := databaseRepo.GetList(repo.WithTypes(types))
 528		if len(dbs) == 0 {
 529			return res, nil
 530		}
 531		for _, db := range dbs {
 532			service := response.AppService{
 533				Label: db.Name,
 534				Value: db.Name,
 535			}
 536			if db.AppInstallID > 0 {
 537				install, err := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(db.AppInstallID))
 538				if err != nil {
 539					return nil, err
 540				}
 541				paramMap := make(map[string]string)
 542				if install.Param != "" {
 543					_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(install.Param), &paramMap)
 544				}
 545				service.Config = paramMap
 546				service.From = constant.AppResourceLocal
 547				service.Status = install.Status
 548			} else {
 549				service.From = constant.AppResourceRemote
 550				service.Status = constant.StatusRunning
 551				service.Config = map[string]string{"PANEL_DB_ROOT_PASSWORD": db.Password, "PANEL_DB_ROOT_USER": db.Username}
 552			}
 553			res = append(res, service)
 554		}
 555	} else {
 556		app, err := appRepo.GetFirst(appRepo.WithKey(key))
 557		if err != nil {
 558			return nil, err
 559		}
 560		installs, err := appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background(), appInstallRepo.WithAppId(app.ID), appInstallRepo.WithStatus(constant.StatusRunning))
 561		if err != nil {
 562			return nil, err
 563		}
 564		for _, install := range installs {
 565			paramMap := make(map[string]string)
 566			if install.Param != "" {
 567				_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(install.Param), &paramMap)
 568			}
 569			res = append(res, response.AppService{
 570				Label:  install.Name,
 571				Value:  install.ServiceName,
 572				Config: paramMap,
 573				Status: install.Status,
 574			})
 575		}
 576	}
 577	return res, nil
 578}
 579
 580func (a *AppInstallService) GetUpdateVersions(req request.AppUpdateVersion) ([]dto.AppVersion, error) {
 581	install, err := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(req.AppInstallID))
 582	var versions []dto.AppVersion
 583	if err != nil {
 584		return versions, err
 585	}
 586	app, err := appRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(install.AppId))
 587	if err != nil {
 588		return versions, err
 589	}
 590	details, err := appDetailRepo.GetBy(appDetailRepo.WithAppId(app.ID))
 591	if err != nil {
 592		return versions, err
 593	}
 594	for _, detail := range details {
 595		ignores, _ := appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.List(runtimeRepo.WithDetailId(detail.ID), appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.WithScope("version"))
 596		if len(ignores) > 0 {
 597			continue
 598		}
 599		if common.IsCrossVersion(install.Version, detail.Version) && !app.CrossVersionUpdate {
 600			continue
 601		}
 602		canUpgrade := common.CompareVersion(detail.Version, install.Version)
 603		if app.Key == vllmAppKeyForUpgrade {
 604			currentImage := loadVllmImageFromEnv(install.Env)
 605			canUpgrade = isVllmUpgradeCandidate(install.Version, detail.Version, currentImage)
 606		}
 607		if canUpgrade {
 608			var newCompose string
 609			if req.UpdateVersion != "" && req.UpdateVersion == detail.Version && detail.DockerCompose == "" && !app.IsLocalApp() {
 610				filename := filepath.Base(detail.DownloadUrl)
 611				dockerComposeUrl := fmt.Sprintf("%s%s", strings.TrimSuffix(detail.DownloadUrl, filename), "docker-compose.yml")
 612				statusCode, composeRes, err := req_helper.HandleRequest(dockerComposeUrl, http.MethodGet, constant.TimeOut20s)
 613				if statusCode == http.StatusNotFound {
 614					return versions, buserr.New("ErrAppVersionUnavailable")
 615				}
 616				if err != nil {
 617					return versions, err
 618				}
 619				if statusCode > 200 {
 620					return versions, err
 621				}
 622				detail.DockerCompose = string(composeRes)
 623				_ = appDetailRepo.Update(context.Background(), detail)
 624			}
 625			newCompose, err = getUpgradeCompose(install, detail)
 626			if err != nil {
 627				return versions, err
 628			}
 629			if app.Key == constant.AppMysql {
 630				majorVersion := getMajorVersion(install.Version)
 631				if !strings.HasPrefix(detail.Version, majorVersion) {
 632					continue
 633				}
 634			}
 635			versions = append(versions, dto.AppVersion{
 636				Version:       detail.Version,
 637				DetailId:      detail.ID,
 638				DockerCompose: newCompose,
 639			})
 640		}
 641	}
 642	sort.Slice(versions, func(i, j int) bool {
 643		return common.CompareVersion(versions[i].Version, versions[j].Version)
 644	})
 645	return versions, nil
 646}
 647
 648func (a *AppInstallService) ChangeAppPort(req request.PortUpdate) error {
 649	if common.ScanPort(int(req.Port)) {
 650		return buserr.WithDetail("ErrPortInUsed", req.Port, nil)
 651	}
 652
 653	appInstall, err := appInstallRepo.LoadBaseInfo(req.Key, req.Name)
 654	if err != nil {
 655		return nil
 656	}
 657
 658	if err := updateInstallInfoInDB(req.Key, req.Name, "port", strconv.FormatInt(req.Port, 10)); err != nil {
 659		return nil
 660	}
 661
 662	appRess, _ := appInstallResourceRepo.GetBy(appInstallResourceRepo.WithLinkId(appInstall.ID))
 663	for _, appRes := range appRess {
 664		appInstall, err := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(appRes.AppInstallId))
 665		if err != nil {
 666			return err
 667		}
 668		if _, err := compose.Restart(fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/%s/docker-compose.yml", global.Dir.AppInstallDir, appInstall.App.Key, appInstall.Name)); err != nil {
 669			global.LOG.Errorf("docker-compose restart %s[%s] failed, err: %v", appInstall.App.Key, appInstall.Name, err)
 670		}
 671	}
 672
 673	return nil
 674}
 675
 676func (a *AppInstallService) DeleteCheck(installID uint) ([]dto.AppResource, error) {
 677	var res []dto.AppResource
 678	appInstall, err := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(installID))
 679	if err != nil {
 680		return nil, err
 681	}
 682	websites, _ := websiteRepo.GetBy(websiteRepo.WithAppInstallId(appInstall.ID))
 683	for _, website := range websites {
 684		res = append(res, dto.AppResource{
 685			Type: "website",
 686			Name: website.PrimaryDomain,
 687		})
 688	}
 689	resources, _ := appInstallResourceRepo.GetBy(appInstallResourceRepo.WithLinkId(appInstall.ID), repo.WithByFrom(constant.AppResourceLocal))
 690	for _, resource := range resources {
 691		linkInstall, _ := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(resource.AppInstallId))
 692		if linkInstall.ID > 0 {
 693			res = append(res, dto.AppResource{
 694				Type: "app",
 695				Name: linkInstall.Name,
 696			})
 697		} else {
 698			_ = appInstallResourceRepo.DeleteBy(context.Background(), appInstallResourceRepo.WithAppInstallId(resource.AppInstallId))
 699		}
 700	}
 701	return res, nil
 702}

 704func (a *AppInstallService) GetDefaultConfigByKey(key, name string) (string, error) {
 705	baseInfo, err := appInstallRepo.LoadBaseInfo(key, name)
 706	if err != nil {
 707		return "", err
 708	}
 709
 710	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 711	filePath := path.Join(global.Dir.AppResourceDir, "remote", baseInfo.Key, baseInfo.Version, "conf")
 712	if !fileOp.Stat(filePath) {
 713		filePath = path.Join(global.Dir.AppResourceDir, baseInfo.Key, "versions", baseInfo.Version, "conf")
 714	}
 715	if !fileOp.Stat(filePath) {
 716		return "", buserr.New("ErrPathNotFound")
 717	}
 718
 719	if key == constant.AppMysql || key == constant.AppMariaDB || key == constant.AppMysqlCluster {
 720		filePath = path.Join(filePath, "my.cnf")
 721	}
 722	if key == constant.AppRedis || key == constant.AppRedisCluster {
 723		filePath = path.Join(filePath, "redis.conf")
 724	}
 725	if key == constant.AppOpenresty {
 726		filePath = path.Join(filePath, "nginx.conf")
 727	}
 728	contentByte, err := os.ReadFile(filePath)
 729	if err != nil {
 730		return "", err
 731	}
 732	return string(contentByte), nil
 733}
 734
 735func (a *AppInstallService) GetParams(id uint) (*response.AppConfig, error) {
 736	var (
 737		params  []response.AppParam
 738		appForm dto.AppForm
 739		envs    = make(map[string]interface{})
 740		res     response.AppConfig
 741	)
 742	install, err := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(id))
 743	if err != nil {
 744		return nil, err
 745	}
 746	detail, err := appDetailRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(install.AppDetailId))
 747	if err != nil {
 748		return nil, err
 749	}
 750	if err = json.Unmarshal([]byte(detail.Params), &appForm); err != nil {
 751		return nil, err
 752	}
 753	if err = json.Unmarshal([]byte(install.Env), &envs); err != nil {
 754		return nil, err
 755	}
 756	for _, form := range appForm.FormFields {
 757		if v, ok := envs[form.EnvKey]; ok {
 758			appParam := response.AppParam{
 759				Edit:     false,
 760				Key:      form.EnvKey,
 761				Rule:     form.Rule,
 762				Type:     form.Type,
 763				Multiple: form.Multiple,
 764				Required: form.Required,
 765			}
 766			if form.Edit {
 767				appParam.Edit = true
 768			}
 769			appParam.LabelZh = form.LabelZh
 770			appParam.LabelEn = form.LabelEn
 771			appParam.Label = form.Label
 772			appParam.Value = v
 773			if form.Type == "service" {
 774				appInstall, _ := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(appInstallRepo.WithServiceName(v.(string)))
 775				appParam.ShowValue = appInstall.Name
 776			} else if form.Type == "select" {
 777				if form.Multiple {
 778					if v == "" {
 779						appParam.Value = []string{}
 780					} else {
 781						if str, ok := v.(string); ok {
 782							appParam.Value = strings.Split(str, ",")
 783						}
 784					}
 785				} else {
 786					for _, fv := range form.Values {
 787						if fv.Value == v {
 788							appParam.ShowValue = fv.Label
 789							break
 790						}
 791					}
 792				}
 793				appParam.Values = form.Values
 794			} else if form.Type == "apps" {
 795				if m, ok := form.Child.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
 796					result := make(map[string]string)
 797					for key, value := range m {
 798						if strVal, ok := value.(string); ok {
 799							result[key] = strVal
 800						}
 801					}
 802					if envKey, ok := result["envKey"]; ok {
 803						serviceName := envs[envKey]
 804						if serviceName != nil {
 805							appInstall, _ := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(appInstallRepo.WithServiceName(serviceName.(string)))
 806							appParam.ShowValue = appInstall.Name
 807						}
 808					}
 809				}
 810			}
 811
 812			params = append(params, appParam)
 813		} else {
 814			params = append(params, response.AppParam{
 815				Edit:     form.Edit,
 816				Key:      form.EnvKey,
 817				Rule:     form.Rule,
 818				Type:     form.Type,
 819				LabelZh:  form.LabelZh,
 820				LabelEn:  form.LabelEn,
 821				Value:    form.Default,
 822				Values:   form.Values,
 823				Multiple: form.Multiple,
 824				Required: form.Required,
 825				Label:    form.Label,
 826			})
 827		}
 828	}
 829
 830	config := getAppCommonConfig(envs)
 831	config.DockerCompose = install.DockerCompose
 832	res.Params = params
 833	if config.ContainerName == "" {
 834		config.ContainerName = install.ContainerName
 835	}
 836	res.AppContainerConfig = config
 837	res.HostMode = isHostModel(install.DockerCompose)
 838	res.RestartPolicy = getRestartPolicy(install.DockerCompose)
 839	res.WebUI = install.WebUI
 840	res.Type = install.App.Type
 841	if rawCompose, err := getUpgradeCompose(install, detail); err == nil {
 842		res.RawCompose = rawCompose
 843	}
 844	return &res, nil
 845}
 846
 847func syncAppInstallStatus(appInstall *model.AppInstall, force bool) error {
 848	if appInstall.Status == constant.StatusInstalling || appInstall.Status == constant.StatusRebuilding || appInstall.Status == constant.StatusUpgrading || appInstall.Status == constant.StatusUninstalling {
 849		return nil
 850	}
 851	cli, err := docker.NewClient()
 852	if err != nil {
 853		return err
 854	}
 855	defer cli.Close()
 856
 857	var (
 858		containers     []container.Summary
 859		containersMap  map[string]container.Summary
 860		containerNames = strings.Split(appInstall.ContainerName, ",")
 861	)
 862	containers, err = cli.ListContainersByName(containerNames)
 863	if err != nil {
 864		return err
 865	}
 866	containersMap = make(map[string]container.Summary)
 867	for _, con := range containers {
 868		containersMap[con.Names[0]] = con
 869	}
 870	synAppInstall(containersMap, appInstall, force)
 871	return nil
 872}
 873
 874func SyncAppInstallStatus(appInstall *model.AppInstall, force bool) error {
 875	return syncAppInstallStatus(appInstall, force)
 876}
 877
 878func updateInstallInfoInDB(appKey, appName, param string, value interface{}) error {
 879	if param != "password" && param != "port" && param != "user-password" {
 880		return nil
 881	}
 882	appInstall, err := appInstallRepo.LoadBaseInfo(appKey, appName)
 883	if err != nil {
 884		return nil
 885	}
 886	envPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/.env", appInstall.AppPath, appInstall.Name)
 887	lineBytes, err := os.ReadFile(envPath)
 888	if err != nil {
 889		return err
 890	}
 891
 892	envKey := ""
 893	switch param {
 894	case "password":
 895		if appKey == "mysql" || appKey == "mariadb" || appKey == "postgresql" || appKey == "mongodb" {
 896			envKey = "PANEL_DB_ROOT_PASSWORD="
 897		} else {
 898			envKey = "PANEL_REDIS_ROOT_PASSWORD="
 899		}
 900	case "port":
 901		envKey = "PANEL_APP_PORT_HTTP="
 902	default:
 903		envKey = "PANEL_DB_USER_PASSWORD="
 904	}
 905	files := strings.Split(string(lineBytes), "\n")
 906	var newFiles []string
 907	for _, line := range files {
 908		if strings.HasPrefix(line, envKey) {
 909			newFiles = append(newFiles, fmt.Sprintf("%s%v", envKey, value))
 910		} else {
 911			newFiles = append(newFiles, line)
 912		}
 913	}
 914	file, err := os.OpenFile(envPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_TRUNC, constant.FilePerm)
 915	if err != nil {
 916		return err
 917	}
 918	defer file.Close()
 919	_, err = file.WriteString(strings.Join(newFiles, "\n"))
 920	if err != nil {
 921		return err
 922	}
 923
 924	oldVal, newVal := "", ""
 925	if param == "password" {
 926		oldVal = fmt.Sprintf("\"PANEL_DB_ROOT_PASSWORD\":\"%v\"", appInstall.Password)
 927		newVal = fmt.Sprintf("\"PANEL_DB_ROOT_PASSWORD\":\"%v\"", value)
 928		if appKey == "redis" {
 929			oldVal = fmt.Sprintf("\"PANEL_REDIS_ROOT_PASSWORD\":\"%v\"", appInstall.Password)
 930			newVal = fmt.Sprintf("\"PANEL_REDIS_ROOT_PASSWORD\":\"%v\"", value)
 931		}
 932		_ = appInstallRepo.BatchUpdateBy(map[string]interface{}{
 933			"param": strings.ReplaceAll(appInstall.Param, oldVal, newVal),
 934			"env":   strings.ReplaceAll(appInstall.Env, oldVal, newVal),
 935		}, repo.WithByID(appInstall.ID))
 936		if appKey == "mysql" || appKey == "postgresql" || appKey == "mongodb" {
 937			return nil
 938		}
 939	}
 940	if param == "user-password" {
 941		oldVal = fmt.Sprintf("\"PANEL_DB_USER_PASSWORD\":\"%v\"", appInstall.UserPassword)
 942		newVal = fmt.Sprintf("\"PANEL_DB_USER_PASSWORD\":\"%v\"", value)
 943		_ = appInstallRepo.BatchUpdateBy(map[string]interface{}{
 944			"param": strings.ReplaceAll(appInstall.Param, oldVal, newVal),
 945			"env":   strings.ReplaceAll(appInstall.Env, oldVal, newVal),
 946		}, repo.WithByID(appInstall.ID))
 947	}
 948	if param == "port" {
 949		oldVal = fmt.Sprintf("\"PANEL_APP_PORT_HTTP\":%v", appInstall.Port)
 950		newVal = fmt.Sprintf("\"PANEL_APP_PORT_HTTP\":%v", value)
 951		_ = appInstallRepo.BatchUpdateBy(map[string]interface{}{
 952			"param":     strings.ReplaceAll(appInstall.Param, oldVal, newVal),
 953			"env":       strings.ReplaceAll(appInstall.Env, oldVal, newVal),
 954			"http_port": value,
 955		}, repo.WithByID(appInstall.ID))
 956	}
 957
 958	ComposeFile := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/%s/docker-compose.yml", global.Dir.AppInstallDir, appKey, appInstall.Name)
 959	stdout, err := compose.Down(ComposeFile)
 960	if err != nil {
 961		return errors.New(stdout)
 962	}
 963	stdout, err = compose.Up(ComposeFile)
 964	if err != nil {
 965		return errors.New(stdout)
 966	}
 967	return nil
 968}
 969
 970func (a *AppInstallService) GetAppInstallInfo(installID uint) (*response.AppInstallInfo, error) {
 971	appInstall, _ := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(installID))
 972	if appInstall.ID == 0 {
 973		return &response.AppInstallInfo{
 974			Status: constant.StatusDeleted,
 975		}, nil
 976	}
 977	_ = syncAppInstallStatus(&appInstall, false)
 978	appInstall, _ = appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(installID))
 979	var envMap map[string]interface{}
 980	err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(appInstall.Env), &envMap)
 981	if err != nil {
 982		return nil, err
 983	}
 984	res := &response.AppInstallInfo{
 985		ID:          appInstall.ID,
 986		Name:        appInstall.Name,
 987		Version:     appInstall.Version,
 988		Container:   appInstall.ContainerName,
 989		HttpPort:    appInstall.HttpPort,
 990		Status:      appInstall.Status,
 991		Message:     appInstall.Message,
 992		AppKey:      appInstall.App.Key,
 993		Env:         envMap,
 994		ComposePath: appInstall.GetComposePath(),
 995	}
 996	for k, v := range envMap {
 997		if strings.Contains(strings.ToUpper(k), "PANEL_APP_PORT") {
 998			var port int
 999			switch val := v.(type) {
1000			case int:
1001				port = val
1002			case string:
1003				port, err = strconv.Atoi(val)
1004				if err != nil {
1005					continue
1006				}
1007			default:
1008				continue
1009			}
1010			res.AppPorts = append(res.AppPorts, port)
1011		}
1012	}
1013	return res, nil
1014}

  34type appSyncContext struct {
  35	task           *task.Task
  36	httpClient     http.Client
  37	baseRemoteUrl  string
  38	systemVersion  string
  39	appsMap        map[string]model.App
  40	settingService ISettingService

  48func (a AppService) createSyncAppStoreTask(sharedCtx **appSyncContext) func(t *task.Task) error {
  49	return func(t *task.Task) (err error) {
  50		t.LogStart(i18n.GetMsgByKey("AppStore") + " " + i18n.GetMsgByKey("TaskSync"))
  51
  52		updateRes, err := a.GetAppUpdate()
  53		if err != nil {
  54			t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("CheckAppStoreUpdate"), err)
  55			return err
  56		}
  57		if !updateRes.CanUpdate {
  58			if updateRes.IsSyncing {
  59				t.Log(i18n.GetMsgByKey("AppStoreIsSyncing"))
  60				return nil
  61			}
  62			global.LOG.Infof("[AppStore] Appstore is up to date")
  63			t.Log(i18n.GetMsgByKey("AppStoreIsUpToDate"))
  64			*sharedCtx = &appSyncContext{skipMetaSync: true}
  65			t.LogSuccess(i18n.GetMsgByKey("AppStore") + " " + i18n.GetMsgByKey("TaskSync"))
  66			return nil
  67		}
  68
  69		list := &dto.AppList{}
  70		if updateRes.AppList == nil {
  71			list, err = getAppList()
  72			if err != nil {
  73				t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("DownloadAppList"), err)
  74				return err
  75			}
  76		} else {
  77			list = updateRes.AppList
  78		}
  79
  80		settingService := NewISettingService()
  81		if err := settingService.Update("AppStoreSyncStatus", constant.StatusSyncing); err != nil {
  82			global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] failed to update sync status to syncing: %v", err)
  83		}
  84
  85		setting, err := settingService.GetSettingInfo()
  86		if err != nil {
  87			t.LogFailedWithErr("GetSettingInfo", err)
  88			return err
  89		}
  90
  91		ctx := &appSyncContext{
  92			task:           t,
  93			httpClient:     http.Client{Timeout: time.Duration(constant.TimeOut20s) * time.Second, Transport: xpack.MultiNodeProvider.LoadRequestTransport()},
  94			baseRemoteUrl:  fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/1panel", global.AppRepoURL(), global.CONF.Base.Mode),
  95			systemVersion:  setting.SystemVersion,
  96			settingService: settingService,
  97			list:           list,
  98			appTags:        make([]*model.AppTag, 0),
  99			pendingIcons:   make(map[string]string),
 100		}
 101
 102		if err = SyncTags(list.Extra); err != nil {
 103			t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("SyncTags"), err)
 104			return err
 105		}
 106		deleteCustomApp()
 107
 108		oldApps, err := appRepo.GetBy(appRepo.WithNotLocal())
 109		if err != nil {
 110			t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("LoadLocalApps"), err)
 111			return err
 112		}
 113		ctx.oldAppIds = make([]uint, 0, len(oldApps))
 114		for _, old := range oldApps {
 115			ctx.oldAppIds = append(ctx.oldAppIds, old.ID)
 116		}
 117
 118		ctx.appsMap, ctx.pendingIcons = getApps(oldApps, list.Apps, setting.SystemVersion, t)
 119
 120		var addCount, updateCount, deleteCount int
 121		if err = ctx.classifyAndPersistAppsWithStats(&addCount, &updateCount, &deleteCount); err != nil {
 122			t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("PersistApps"), err)
 123			return err
 124		}
 125
 126		if err := settingService.Update("AppStoreSyncStatus", constant.StatusSyncSuccess); err != nil {
 127			global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] failed to update sync status to success: %v", err)
 128		}
 129		if err := settingService.Update("AppStoreLastModified", strconv.Itoa(list.LastModified)); err != nil {
 130			global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] failed to update last modified: %v", err)
 131		}
 132		global.LOG.Infof("[AppStore] Appstore sync completed")
 133
 134		*sharedCtx = ctx
 135		t.LogSuccess(i18n.GetMsgByKey("AppStore") + " " + i18n.GetMsgByKey("TaskSync"))
 136		return nil
 137	}
 138}

 140"cmt">// appWorkItem (struct)
 141type appWorkItem struct {
 142	appDef  dto.AppDefine
 143	app     model.App
 144	iconUrl string
 145	hadIcon bool
 146}
 147
 148type appWorkResult struct {
 149	appKey     string
 150	app        model.App
 151	iconStatus int
 152	hadIcon    bool
 153	httpFailed bool
 154}
 155
 156func (c *appSyncContext) processOneApp(item appWorkItem) appWorkResult {
 157	app := item.app
 158	l := item.appDef
 159	result := appWorkResult{
 160		appKey:  l.AppProperty.Key,
 161		hadIcon: item.hadIcon,
 162	}
 163
 164	if item.hadIcon {
 165		status, iconField := c.downloadAppIcon(item.iconUrl, l.AppProperty.Key, app.Icon)
 166		result.iconStatus = status
 167		switch status {
 168		case http.StatusOK:
 169			app.Icon = iconField
 170		case http.StatusNotModified:
 171		default:
 172			result.httpFailed = true
 173		}
 174	}
 175
 176	app.TagsKey = l.AppProperty.Tags
 177	if l.AppProperty.Recommend > 0 {
 178		app.Recommend = l.AppProperty.Recommend
 179	} else {
 180		app.Recommend = 9999
 181	}
 182	app.ReadMe = l.ReadMe
 183	app.LastModified = l.LastModified
 184
 185	versions := l.Versions
 186	detailsMap := getAppDetails(app.Details, versions)
 187	for _, v := range versions {
 188		version := v.Name
 189		detail := detailsMap[version]
 190		versionUrl := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/%s", c.baseRemoteUrl, app.Key, version)
 191
 192		paramByte, _ := json.Marshal(v.AppForm)
 193		var appForm dto.AppForm
 194		_ = json.Unmarshal(paramByte, &appForm)
 195
 196		if appForm.SupportVersion > 0 && common.CompareVersion(strconv.FormatFloat(appForm.SupportVersion, 'f', -1, 64), c.systemVersion) {
 197			delete(detailsMap, version)
 198			continue
 199		}
 200
 201		if _, ok := InitTypes[app.Type]; ok {
 202			dockerComposeUrl := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s", versionUrl, "docker-compose.yml")
 203			_, composeRes, err := req_helper.HandleRequestWithClient(&c.httpClient, dockerComposeUrl, http.MethodGet, constant.TimeOut20s)
 204			if err == nil {
 205				detail.DockerCompose = string(composeRes)
 206			} else {
 207				result.httpFailed = true
 208			}
 209		} else {
 210			detail.DockerCompose = ""
 211		}
 212
 213		detail.Params = string(paramByte)
 214		detail.DownloadUrl = fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s", versionUrl, app.Key+"-"+version+".tar.gz")
 215		detail.DownloadCallBackUrl = v.DownloadCallBackUrl
 216		detail.Update = true
 217		detail.LastModified = v.LastModified
 218		detailsMap[version] = detail
 219	}
 220
 221	newDetails := make([]model.AppDetail, 0, len(detailsMap))
 222	for _, detail := range detailsMap {
 223		newDetails = append(newDetails, detail)
 224	}
 225	app.Details = newDetails
 226
 227	result.app = app
 228	return result
 229}
 230
 231func (c *appSyncContext) syncAppIconsAndDetails() error {
 232	total := len(c.list.Apps)
 233	global.LOG.Infof("[AppStore] sync app detail start, total apps: %d", total)
 234
 235	workItems := make([]appWorkItem, 0, total)
 236	for _, l := range c.list.Apps {
 237		app, ok := c.appsMap[l.AppProperty.Key]
 238		if !ok {
 239			continue
 240		}
 241		iconUrl, hasPending := c.pendingIcons[l.AppProperty.Key]
 242		workItems = append(workItems, appWorkItem{
 243			appDef:  l,
 244			app:     app,
 245			iconUrl: iconUrl,
 246			hadIcon: hasPending,
 247		})
 248	}
 249
 250	totalWork := len(workItems)
 251	if totalWork == 0 {
 252		return nil
 253	}
 254
 255	const maxWorkers = 4
 256	var (
 257		failFlag atomic.Bool
 258		workCh   = make(chan int, maxWorkers)
 259		resultCh = make(chan appWorkResult, maxWorkers)
 260		wg       sync.WaitGroup
 261	)
 262
 263	wg.Add(maxWorkers)
 264	for range maxWorkers {
 265		go func() {
 266			defer wg.Done()
 267			for idx := range workCh {
 268				result := c.processOneApp(workItems[idx])
 269				resultCh <- result
 270				if result.httpFailed {
 271					failFlag.Store(true)
 272				}
 273			}
 274		}()
 275	}
 276	go func() { wg.Wait(); close(resultCh) }()
 277
 278	var fed atomic.Int32
 279	go func() {
 280		for i := range workItems {
 281			if failFlag.Load() {
 282				break
 283			}
 284			workCh <- i
 285			fed.Store(int32(i + 1))
 286		}
 287		close(workCh)
 288	}()
 289
 290	var (
 291		completed     int
 292		icon200Count  int
 293		icon304Count  int
 294		iconFailCount int
 295	)
 296	milestones := [4]int{totalWork / 4, totalWork / 2, totalWork * 3 / 4, totalWork}
 297	nextMS := 0
 298	checkProgress := func() {
 299		if nextMS < len(milestones) && completed >= milestones[nextMS] {
 300			c.task.LogWithProgress(i18n.GetMsgByKey("SyncAppDetail"), completed, totalWork)
 301			nextMS++
 302		}
 303	}
 304
 305	applyResult := func(result appWorkResult) {
 306		c.appsMap[result.appKey] = result.app
 307		if result.hadIcon {
 308			switch result.iconStatus {
 309			case http.StatusOK:
 310				icon200Count++
 311			case http.StatusNotModified:
 312				icon304Count++
 313			default:
 314				iconFailCount++
 315			}
 316		}
 317		completed++
 318		checkProgress()
 319	}
 320
 321	for result := range resultCh {
 322		applyResult(result)
 323	}
 324
 325	fedCount := int(fed.Load())
 326	if failFlag.Load() && fedCount < len(workItems) {
 327		global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] HTTP failure detected, falling back to serial for remaining %d apps", len(workItems)-fedCount)
 328		for i := fedCount; i < len(workItems); i++ {
 329			result := c.processOneApp(workItems[i])
 330			applyResult(result)
 331		}
 332	}
 333
 334	global.LOG.Infof("[AppStore] icon download completed - total: %d, success(200): %d, cached(304): %d, failed: %d",
 335		total, icon200Count, icon304Count, iconFailCount)
 336
 337	return nil
 338}
 339
 340func (c *appSyncContext) downloadAppIcon(iconUrl, appKey, oldIcon string) (status int, iconField string) {
 341	iconFileName, existingEtag := appicon.ParseIconField(oldIcon)
 342
 343	reqHeaders := make(map[string]string)
 344	if existingEtag != "" && iconFileName != "" && appicon.IconFileExists(iconFileName) {
 345		reqHeaders["If-None-Match"] = existingEtag
 346	}
 347
 348	resp, err := req_helper.HandleRequestWithHeaders(&c.httpClient, iconUrl, http.MethodGet, constant.TimeOut20s, reqHeaders)
 349	if err != nil {
 350		global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] request icon failed url=%s, err=%v", iconUrl, err)
 351		return 0, ""
 352	}
 353
 354	if resp.StatusCode == http.StatusNotModified {
 355		return http.StatusNotModified, ""
 356	}
 357
 358	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
 359		global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] download icon failed url=%s, status=%d", iconUrl, resp.StatusCode)
 360		return 0, ""
 361	}
 362
 363	if len(resp.Body) == 0 {
 364		global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] download icon empty body url=%s", iconUrl)
 365		return 0, ""
 366	}
 367
 368	if resp.Body[0] == '<' {
 369		global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] download icon got HTML response url=%s", iconUrl)
 370		return 0, ""
 371	}
 372
 373	contentType := resp.Header.Get("Content-Type")
 374	ct := strings.TrimSpace(strings.Split(contentType, ";")[0])
 375	if strings.ToLower(ct) != "image/png" {
 376		global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] unexpected icon content-type: %s, expected image/png, url=%s", ct, iconUrl)
 377	}
 378
 379	fileName, err := appicon.WriteIconFile(appKey, resp.Body)
 380	if err != nil {
 381		global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] write icon file failed appKey=%s, err=%v", appKey, err)
 382		return 0, ""
 383	}
 384
 385	newEtag := resp.Header.Get("ETag")
 386	iconField = appicon.BuildIconField(fileName, newEtag)
 387
 388	return http.StatusOK, iconField
 389}
 390
 391func (a AppService) createSyncAppStoreMetaTask(sharedCtx **appSyncContext) func(t *task.Task) error {
 392	return func(t *task.Task) (err error) {
 393		t.LogStart(i18n.GetMsgByKey("SyncAppDetail"))
 394		ctx := *sharedCtx
 395		if ctx == nil {
 396			global.LOG.Warnf("[AppStore] meta sync skipped: shared context is nil")
 397			t.Log(i18n.GetMsgByKey("SyncAppDetail") + " skipped: shared context is nil")
 398			return nil
 399		}
 400
 401		if ctx.skipMetaSync {
 402			global.LOG.Infof("[AppStore] meta sync skipped: no update needed")
 403			t.Log(i18n.GetMsgByKey("SyncAppDetail") + " skipped: no update needed")
 404			return nil
 405		}
 406
 407		if ctx.list == nil || ctx.appsMap == nil {
 408			global.LOG.Errorf("[AppStore] meta sync failed: shared context data not initialized")
 409			err := fmt.Errorf("shared context data not initialized")
 410			t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("SyncAppDetail"), err)
 411			return err
 412		}
 413
 414		t.Logf("%s: %d apps", i18n.GetMsgByKey("SyncAppDetail"), len(ctx.list.Apps))
 415
 416		ctx.task = t
 417		ctx.appTags = make([]*model.AppTag, 0)
 418
 419		if err = ctx.syncAppIconsAndDetails(); err != nil {
 420			t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("SyncAppDetail"), err)
 421			return err
 422		}
 423
 424		if err = ctx.classifyAndPersistApps(); err != nil {
 425			t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("PersistAppDetails"), err)
 426			return err
 427		}
 428
 429		global.LOG.Infof("[AppStore] Appstore meta sync completed")
 430		return nil
 431	}
 432}
 433
 434func (c *appSyncContext) classifyAndPersistApps() (err error) {
 435	var addCount, updateCount, deleteCount int
 436	return c.classifyAndPersistAppsWithStats(&addCount, &updateCount, &deleteCount)
 437}
 438
 439func (c *appSyncContext) classifyAndPersistAppsWithStats(addCount, updateCount, deleteCount *int) (err error) {
 440	tags, _ := tagRepo.All()
 441	var (
 442		addAppArray    []model.App
 443		updateAppArray []model.App
 444		deleteAppArray []model.App
 445		deleteIds      []uint
 446		tagMap         = make(map[string]uint, len(tags))
 447	)
 448
 449	for _, v := range c.appsMap {
 450		if v.ID == 0 {
 451			addAppArray = append(addAppArray, v)
 452		} else {
 453			if v.Status == constant.AppTakeDown {
 454				installs, _ := appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background(), appInstallRepo.WithAppId(v.ID))
 455				if len(installs) > 0 {
 456					updateAppArray = append(updateAppArray, v)
 457					continue
 458				}
 459				deleteAppArray = append(deleteAppArray, v)
 460				deleteIds = append(deleteIds, v.ID)
 461			} else {
 462				updateAppArray = append(updateAppArray, v)
 463			}
 464		}
 465	}
 466
 467	if len(addAppArray) > 0 {
 468		addKeys := make([]string, 0, len(addAppArray))
 469		for _, app := range addAppArray {
 470			addKeys = append(addKeys, app.Key)
 471		}
 472		existingApps, _ := appRepo.GetBy(appRepo.WithKeyIn(addKeys))
 473		if len(existingApps) > 0 {
 474			existingMap := make(map[string]model.App, len(existingApps))
 475			for _, e := range existingApps {
 476				existingMap[e.Key] = e
 477			}
 478			filteredAdd := make([]model.App, 0, len(addAppArray))
 479			for _, app := range addAppArray {
 480				if existing, ok := existingMap[app.Key]; ok {
 481					app.ID = existing.ID
 482					if len(app.Details) == 0 {
 483						app.Details = existing.Details
 484					}
 485					updateAppArray = append(updateAppArray, app)
 486				} else {
 487					filteredAdd = append(filteredAdd, app)
 488				}
 489			}
 490			addAppArray = filteredAdd
 491		}
 492	}
 493
 494	*addCount = len(addAppArray)
 495	*updateCount = len(updateAppArray)
 496	*deleteCount = len(deleteAppArray)
 497
 498	tx, ctx := getTxAndContext()
 499	defer func() {
 500		if err != nil {
 501			tx.Rollback()
 502			return
 503		}
 504	}()
 505
 506	if len(addAppArray) > 0 {
 507		if err = appRepo.BatchCreate(ctx, addAppArray); err != nil {
 508			return
 509		}
 510	}
 511
 512	if len(deleteAppArray) > 0 {
 513		if err = appRepo.BatchDelete(ctx, deleteAppArray); err != nil {
 514			return
 515		}
 516		if err = appDetailRepo.DeleteByAppIds(ctx, deleteIds); err != nil {
 517			return
 518		}
 519	}
 520
 521	for _, tag := range tags {
 522		tagMap[tag.Key] = tag.ID
 523	}
 524
 525	if len(updateAppArray) > 0 {
 526		for _, update := range updateAppArray {
 527			if err = appRepo.Save(ctx, &update); err != nil {
 528				return
 529			}
 530		}
 531	}
 532
 533	apps := append(addAppArray, updateAppArray...)
 534
 535	var (
 536		addDetails    []model.AppDetail
 537		updateDetails []model.AppDetail
 538		deleteDetails []model.AppDetail
 539	)
 540
 541	totalDetails := 0
 542	for _, app := range apps {
 543		for _, tag := range app.TagsKey {
 544			tagId, ok := tagMap[tag]
 545			if ok {
 546				exist, _ := appTagRepo.GetFirst(ctx, appTagRepo.WithByTagID(tagId), appTagRepo.WithByAppID(app.ID))
 547				if exist == nil {
 548					c.appTags = append(c.appTags, &model.AppTag{
 549						AppId: app.ID,
 550						TagId: tagId,
 551					})
 552				}
 553			}
 554		}
 555
 556		for _, d := range app.Details {
 557			totalDetails++
 558			d.AppId = app.ID
 559			if d.ID == 0 {
 560				addDetails = append(addDetails, d)
 561			} else {
 562				if d.Status == constant.AppTakeDown {
 563					runtime, _ := runtimeRepo.GetFirst(ctx, runtimeRepo.WithDetailId(d.ID))
 564					if runtime != nil {
 565						updateDetails = append(updateDetails, d)
 566						continue
 567					}
 568					installs, _ := appInstallRepo.ListBy(ctx, appInstallRepo.WithDetailIdsIn([]uint{d.ID}))
 569					if len(installs) > 0 {
 570						updateDetails = append(updateDetails, d)
 571						continue
 572					}
 573					deleteDetails = append(deleteDetails, d)
 574				} else {
 575					updateDetails = append(updateDetails, d)
 576				}
 577			}
 578		}
 579	}
 580
 581	if len(addDetails) > 0 {
 582		if err = appDetailRepo.BatchCreate(ctx, addDetails); err != nil {
 583			return
 584		}
 585	}
 586
 587	if len(deleteDetails) > 0 {
 588		if err = appDetailRepo.BatchDelete(ctx, deleteDetails); err != nil {
 589			return
 590		}
 591	}
 592
 593	if len(updateDetails) > 0 {
 594		for _, u := range updateDetails {
 595			if err = appDetailRepo.Update(ctx, u); err != nil {
 596				return
 597			}
 598		}
 599	}
 600
 601	if len(c.oldAppIds) > 0 {
 602		if err = appTagRepo.DeleteByAppIds(ctx, deleteIds); err != nil {
 603			return
 604		}
 605	}
 606
 607	if len(c.appTags) > 0 {
 608		if err = appTagRepo.BatchCreate(ctx, c.appTags); err != nil {
 609			return
 610		}
 611	}
 612
 613	if err = tx.Commit().Error; err != nil {
 614		return
 615	}
 616	return nil
 617}

📄 app_upgrade.go agent/app/service/app_upgrade.go

  41type appUpgradePhase int
  42
  43const (
  44	appUpgradePreparing appUpgradePhase = iota
  45	appUpgradePrepared
  46	appUpgradeStopped
  47	appUpgradeBackedUp
  48	appUpgradeDown
  49	appUpgradeMutated
  50	appUpgradeStarted
  51	appUpgradeReady
  52	appUpgradeCommitted
  53)

  59"cmt">// appUpgradeSnapshot (interface)
  60type appUpgradeSnapshot interface {
  61	Restore() error
  62	Cleanup()
  63}
  64
  65type upgradeFileSnapshot struct {
  66	installPath string
  67	backupPath  string
  68	paths       []string
  69	existing    map[string]bool
  70}
  71
  72type appUpgradeContext struct {
  73	req       request.AppInstallUpgrade
  74	original  model.AppInstall
  75	candidate model.AppInstall
  76	detail    model.AppDetail
  77
  78	phase         appUpgradePhase
  79	stopAttempted bool
  80	downAttempted bool
  81	rollbackErr   error
  82
  83	detailDir        string
  84	stageDir         string
  85	envContent       []byte
  86	oldEnvContent    []byte

  88	oldImageIDs      []appImageID

  94func upgradeInstall(req request.AppInstallUpgrade) error {
  95	install, err := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(req.InstallID))
  96	if err != nil {
  97		return err
  98	}
  99	if install.Status == constant.StatusUpgrading {
 100		return buserr.New("TaskIsExecuting")
 101	}
 102	if err = task.CheckScopeTaskIsExecuting(task.TaskScopeApp, install.ID); err != nil {
 103		return err
 104	}
 105	if _, loaded := appUpgradeLocks.LoadOrStore(install.ID, struct{}{}); loaded {
 106		return buserr.New("TaskIsExecuting")
 107	}
 108	releaseLock := true
 109	defer func() {
 110		if releaseLock {
 111			appUpgradeLocks.Delete(install.ID)
 112		}
 113	}()
 114
 115	detail, err := appDetailRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(req.DetailID))
 116	if err != nil {
 117		return err
 118	}
 119	if install.App.Key == vllmAppKeyForUpgrade && !isVllmUpgradeVersionAllowed(install.Version, detail.Version, loadVllmImageFromEnv(install.Env)) {
 120		return errors.New("vLLM can only upgrade within the same image type")
 121	}
 122	if install.Version == detail.Version {
 123		return errors.New("two version is same")
 124	}
 125
 126	upgradeTask, err := task.NewTaskWithOps(install.Name, task.TaskUpgrade, task.TaskScopeApp, req.TaskID, install.ID)
 127	if err != nil {
 128		return err
 129	}
 130	ctx := &appUpgradeContext{
 131		req:              req,
 132		original:         install,
 133		candidate:        install,
 134		detail:           detail,
 135		phase:            appUpgradePreparing,
 136		oldDockerCompose: install.DockerCompose,
 137	}
 138	upgradeTask.AddSubTaskWithOps(i18n.GetMsgByKey("UpgradePrepare"), ctx.prepare, nil, 0, 0)
 139	upgradeTask.AddSubTaskWithOps(
 140		task.GetTaskName(install.Name, task.TaskUpgrade, task.TaskScopeApp),
 141		ctx.cutover,
 142		func(t *task.Task) {
 143			ctx.rollbackErr = ctx.rollback(t)
 144		},
 145		0,
 146		0,
 147	)
 148
 149	upgradingInstall := install
 150	upgradingInstall.Status = constant.StatusUpgrading
 151	upgradingInstall.Message = ""
 152	if err = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &upgradingInstall); err != nil {
 153		return err
 154	}
 155
 156	releaseLock = false
 157	go func() {
 158		defer appUpgradeLocks.Delete(install.ID)
 159		defer ctx.cleanup()
 160		taskErr := upgradeTask.Execute()
 161		if taskErr == nil {
 162			return
 163		}
 164		if ctx.rollbackErr != nil {
 165			taskErr = fmt.Errorf("%w; %s: %v", taskErr, i18n.GetMsgByKey("UpgradeRollbackFailed"), ctx.rollbackErr)
 166			upgradeTask.Task.ErrorMsg = taskErr.Error()
 167			_ = repo.NewITaskRepo().Update(context.Background(), upgradeTask.Task)
 168		}
 169		if !ctx.stopAttempted || ctx.rollbackErr == nil {
 170			restored := ctx.original
 171			_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &restored)
 172			return
 173		}
 174		failed := ctx.original
 175		failed.Status = constant.StatusUpgradeErr
 176		failed.Message = taskErr.Error()
 177		_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &failed)
 178	}()
 179	return nil
 180}
 181
 182func (u *appUpgradeContext) prepare(t *task.Task) error {
 183	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 184	u.detailDir = path.Join(u.original.App.GetAppResourcePath(), u.detail.Version)
 185	if u.original.App.Resource == constant.AppResourceRemote {
 186		if err := downloadApp(u.original.App, u.detail, nil, t.Logger); err != nil {
 187			return err
 188		}
 189	}
 190	if !fileOp.Stat(u.detailDir) {
 191		return buserr.WithName("ErrFileNotFound", u.detailDir)
 192	}
 193	if u.detail.DockerCompose == "" {
 194		composeContent, err := fileOp.GetContent(path.Join(u.detailDir, "docker-compose.yml"))
 195		if err != nil {
 196			return err
 197		}
 198		u.detail.DockerCompose = string(composeContent)
 199		_ = appDetailRepo.Update(context.Background(), u.detail)
 200	}
 201	if strings.TrimSpace(u.detail.DockerCompose) == "" && strings.TrimSpace(u.req.DockerCompose) == "" {
 202		return buserr.WithName("ErrFileNotFound", "docker-compose.yml")
 203	}
 204
 205	var err error
 206	u.oldEnvContent, err = fileOp.GetContent(u.original.GetEnvPath())
 207	if err != nil {
 208		return err
 209	}
 210	u.stageDir, err = os.MkdirTemp(u.original.GetAppPath(), "."+u.original.Name+"-upgrade-")
 211	if err != nil {
 212		return err
 213	}
 214	if err = fileOp.CopyDirWithNewName(u.detailDir, u.stageDir, "."); err != nil {
 215		return err
 216	}
 217	if err = copyUpgradeStageFile(u.original.GetPath(), u.stageDir, ".env"); err != nil {
 218		return err
 219	}
 220	if u.original.App.Key == constant.AppOpenclaw {
 221		if err = copyUpgradeStageFile(u.original.GetPath(), u.stageDir, path.Join("data", "conf", "openclaw.json")); err != nil {
 222			return err
 223		}
 224	}
 225	if u.original.App.Key == constant.AppOpenresty {
 226		for _, relativePath := range []string{
 227			nginxModuleBuildDir,
 228			nginxModuleModulesDir,
 229			path.Join(nginxModuleConfDir, nginxModuleEnabledConfDir),
 230		} {
 231			if err = copyUpgradeStageFile(u.original.GetPath(), u.stageDir, relativePath); err != nil {
 232				return err
 233			}
 234		}
 235	}
 236
 237	stagedInstall := u.original
 238	stagedInstall.Name = path.Base(u.stageDir)
 239	stagedInstall.Version = u.detail.Version
 240	stagedInstall.AppDetailId = u.req.DetailID
 241	if stagedInstall.App.Key == vllmAppKeyForUpgrade {
 242		envs := make(map[string]interface{})
 243		if err = json.Unmarshal([]byte(stagedInstall.Env), &envs); err != nil {
 244			return err
 245		}
 246		image := buildVllmUpgradeImage(loadVllmImageFromEnv(stagedInstall.Env), u.original.Version, u.detail.Version)
 247		envs[vllmImageEnvKey] = image
 248		paramBytes, marshalErr := json.Marshal(envs)
 249		if marshalErr != nil {
 250			return marshalErr
 251		}
 252		stagedInstall.Env = string(paramBytes)
 253	}
 254	if err = migrateOpenclawProtocolUpgrade(&stagedInstall, u.original.Version, u.detail.Version); err != nil {
 255		return err
 256	}
 257
 258	u.candidate = stagedInstall
 259	u.candidate.Name = u.original.Name
 260	u.candidate.DockerCompose, err = renderUpgradeCompose(u.candidate, u.detail, u.req.DockerCompose)
 261	if err != nil {
 262		return err
 263	}
 264	if strings.TrimSpace(u.candidate.DockerCompose) == "" {
 265		return buserr.WithName("ErrFileNotFound", "docker-compose.yml")
 266	}
 267
 268	u.envContent, err = renderUpgradeEnv(&u.candidate, u.oldEnvContent)
 269	if err != nil {
 270		return err
 271	}
 272	if err = writeUpgradeFile(path.Join(u.stageDir, ".env"), u.envContent, constant.FilePerm); err != nil {
 273		return err
 274	}
 275	if err = writeUpgradeFile(path.Join(u.stageDir, "docker-compose.yml"), []byte(u.candidate.DockerCompose), constant.FilePerm); err != nil {
 276		return err
 277	}
 278	project, err := docker.GetComposeProject(u.original.Name, u.stageDir, []byte(u.candidate.DockerCompose), u.envContent, false)
 279	if err != nil {
 280		return err
 281	}
 282	hasBuild := false
 283	for _, service := range project.Services {
 284		if service.Image == "" && service.Build == nil {
 285			return fmt.Errorf("compose service %s has neither image nor build configuration", service.Name)
 286		}
 287		hasBuild = hasBuild || service.Build != nil
 288	}
 289	if u.req.DeleteImage {
 290		dockerClient, clientErr := docker.NewClient()
 291		if clientErr != nil {
 292			return clientErr
 293		}
 294		u.oldImageIDs, err = getAppImageIDsByCompose(dockerClient, u.oldEnvContent, []byte(u.oldDockerCompose))
 295		dockerClient.Close()
 296		if err != nil {
 297			return err
 298		}
 299	}
 300
 301	images := make([]string, 0, len(project.Services))
 302	for _, service := range project.Services {
 303		if service.Image != "" {
 304			images = append(images, service.Image)
 305		}
 306	}
 307	if err = prepareUpgradeImages(t, images, u.req.PullImage); err != nil {
 308		return err
 309	}
 310	if u.candidate.App.Key == constant.AppOpenresty {
 311		if err = u.prepareOpenresty(t, stagedInstall); err != nil {
 312			return err
 313		}
 314		if err = verifyUpgradeImages(images); err != nil {
 315			return err
 316		}
 317	} else if hasBuild {
 318		logStr := fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", i18n.GetMsgByKey("TaskBuild"), i18n.GetMsgByKey("Image"))
 319		t.LogStart(logStr)
 320		if err = compose.BuildWithTask(path.Join(u.stageDir, "docker-compose.yml"), project.Name, t); err != nil {
 321			t.LogFailedWithErr(logStr, err)
 322			return err
 323		}
 324		t.LogSuccess(logStr)
 325		if err = verifyUpgradeImages(images); err != nil {
 326			return err
 327		}
 328	}
 329	if u.original.App.Resource == constant.AppResourceRemote {
 330		go RequestDownloadCallBack(u.detail.DownloadCallBackUrl)
 331	}
 332	u.phase = appUpgradePrepared
 333	return nil
 334}
 335
 336func (u *appUpgradeContext) prepareOpenresty(t *task.Task, stagedInstall model.AppInstall) error {
 337	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 338	detailBuildDir := path.Join(u.detailDir, nginxModuleBuildDir)
 339	installBuildDir := path.Join(u.stageDir, nginxModuleBuildDir)
 340	if !fileOp.Stat(installBuildDir) {
 341		if err := fileOp.CreateDir(installBuildDir, constant.DirPerm); err != nil {
 342			return err
 343		}
 344	}
 345	if err := copyAppDetailMissing(fileOp, detailBuildDir, installBuildDir); err != nil {
 346		return err
 347	}
 348	if err := fileOp.DeleteDir(path.Join(installBuildDir, nginxModuleTmpDir)); err != nil {
 349		return err
 350	}
 351	if err := fileOp.CopyDir(path.Join(detailBuildDir, nginxModuleTmpDir), installBuildDir); err != nil {
 352		return err
 353	}
 354	for _, fileName := range []string{"Dockerfile", "nginx.conf", "nginx.vh.default.conf"} {
 355		if err := fileOp.CopyFile(path.Join(detailBuildDir, fileName), installBuildDir); err != nil {
 356			return err
 357		}
 358	}
 359	if err := syncNginxModuleBuilder(detailBuildDir, installBuildDir); err != nil {
 360		return err
 361	}
 362	targetCatalogSource := path.Join(detailBuildDir, nginxModuleCatalogFile)
 363	if !fileOp.Stat(targetCatalogSource) {
 364		return fmt.Errorf("target OpenResty module catalog not found: %s", targetCatalogSource)
 365	}
 366	targetCatalogPath := path.Join(installBuildDir, nginxModuleCatalogPendingFile)
 367	if err := stageNginxModuleCatalog(targetCatalogSource, targetCatalogPath); err != nil {
 368		return err
 369	}
 370	stagedInstall.Name = path.Base(u.stageDir)
 371	stagedInstall.Version = u.candidate.Version
 372	stagedInstall.Env = u.candidate.Env
 373	stagedInstall.DockerCompose = u.candidate.DockerCompose
 374	return buildNginx(t, stagedInstall, targetCatalogPath)
 375}
 376
 377func (u *appUpgradeContext) cutover(t *task.Task) error {
 378	u.stopAttempted = true
 379	t.LogStart(i18n.GetMsgByKey("UpgradeStop"))
 380	if out, err := compose.Stop(u.original.GetComposePath()); err != nil {
 381		if out != "" {
 382			err = fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", out, err)
 383		}
 384		t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("UpgradeStop"), err)
 385		return err
 386	}
 387	t.LogSuccess(i18n.GetMsgByKey("UpgradeStop"))
 388	u.phase = appUpgradeStopped
 389
 390	var err error
 391	if u.original.App.Key == constant.AppOpenresty {
 392		u.snapshot, err = createOpenrestyUpgradeSnapshot(u.original.GetPath())
 393	} else {
 394		snapshotPaths := []string{".env", "docker-compose.yml", "scripts"}
 395		if u.original.App.Key == constant.AppOpenclaw {
 396			snapshotPaths = append(snapshotPaths, path.Join("data", "conf", "openclaw.json"))
 397		}
 398		u.snapshot, err = createUpgradeFileSnapshot(u.original.GetPath(), snapshotPaths)
 399	}
 400	if err != nil {
 401		return err
 402	}
 403
 404	if u.req.Backup {
 405		if err = u.backup(t); err != nil {
 406			return err
 407		}
 408		u.phase = appUpgradeBackedUp
 409	} else {
 410		t.Log(i18n.GetMsgByKey("UpgradeBackupDisabled"))
 411	}
 412
 413	u.downAttempted = true
 414	if out, downErr := compose.Down(u.original.GetComposePath()); downErr != nil {
 415		if out != "" {
 416			downErr = fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", out, downErr)
 417		}
 418		return downErr
 419	}
 420	u.phase = appUpgradeDown
 421
 422	u.phase = appUpgradeMutated
 423	if err = u.applyStagedFiles(); err != nil {
 424		return err
 425	}
 426	if err = writeUpgradeFile(u.original.GetEnvPath(), u.envContent, constant.FilePerm); err != nil {
 427		return err
 428	}
 429	if err = runScript(t, &u.candidate, "upgrade"); err != nil {
 430		return err
 431	}
 432	if err = writeUpgradeFile(u.original.GetComposePath(), []byte(u.candidate.DockerCompose), constant.FilePerm); err != nil {
 433		return err
 434	}
 435
 436	logStr := fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", i18n.GetMsgByKey("Run"), i18n.GetMsgByKey("App"))
 437	t.LogStart(logStr)
 438	if out, upErr := compose.UpWithoutBuild(u.original.GetComposePath()); upErr != nil {
 439		if out != "" {
 440			upErr = fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", out, upErr)
 441		}
 442		t.LogFailedWithErr(logStr, upErr)
 443		return upErr
 444	}
 445	t.LogSuccess(logStr)
 446	u.phase = appUpgradeStarted
 447
 448	t.LogStart(i18n.GetMsgByKey("UpgradeWaitReady"))
 449	containerNames, err := waitAppContainersReady(context.Background(), u.candidate)
 450	if err != nil {
 451		t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("UpgradeWaitReady"), err)
 452		return err
 453	}
 454	t.LogSuccess(i18n.GetMsgByKey("UpgradeWaitReady"))
 455	u.phase = appUpgradeReady
 456	u.candidate.ContainerName = strings.Join(containerNames, ",")
 457	u.candidate.Status = constant.StatusRunning
 458	u.candidate.Message = ""
 459
 460	if u.candidate.App.Key == constant.AppOpenresty {
 461		liveCatalogPath := path.Join(u.candidate.GetPath(), nginxModuleBuildDir, nginxModuleCatalogPendingFile)
 462		if err = commitStaticNginxModuleBuilds(u.candidate, liveCatalogPath, t); err != nil {
 463			return err
 464		}
 465		activeCatalogPath := path.Join(u.candidate.GetPath(), nginxModuleBuildDir, nginxModuleCatalogFile)
 466		if err = activateNginxModuleCatalogAndCommit(liveCatalogPath, activeCatalogPath, func() error {
 467			return appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &u.candidate)
 468		}); err != nil {
 469			return err
 470		}
 471	} else if err = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &u.candidate); err != nil {
 472		return err
 473	}
 474	u.phase = appUpgradeCommitted
 475	u.deleteOldImages(t)
 476	return nil
 477}
 478
 479func (u *appUpgradeContext) backup(t *task.Task) error {
 480	fileName := fmt.Sprintf("upgrade_backup_%s_%s.tar.gz", u.original.Name, time.Now().Format(constant.DateTimeSlimLayout)+common.RandStrAndNum(5))
 481	record, err := backupAppWithParentTask(&u.original, t, fileName)
 482	if err != nil {
 483		return buserr.WithNameAndErr("ErrAppBackup", u.original.Name, err)
 484	}
 485	u.backupFile = path.Join(global.Dir.LocalBackupDir, record.FileDir, record.FileName)
 486	info, err := os.Stat(u.backupFile)
 487	if err != nil || info.Size() == 0 || record.Status != constant.StatusSuccess {
 488		if err == nil {
 489			err = errors.New("backup archive is empty or incomplete")
 490		}
 491		markBackupFailed(record.ID, err)
 492		return buserr.WithNameAndErr("ErrAppBackup", u.original.Name, err)
 493	}
 494
 495	backupRecordService := NewIBackupRecordService()
 496	backups, _ := backupRecordService.ListAppRecords(u.original.App.Key, u.original.Name, "upgrade_backup")
 497	if len(backups) > 3 {
 498		deleteIDs := make([]uint, 0, len(backups)-3)
 499		for _, backup := range backups[:len(backups)-3] {
 500			deleteIDs = append(deleteIDs, backup.ID)
 501		}
 502		_ = backupRecordService.BatchDeleteRecord(deleteIDs)
 503	}
 504	return nil
 505}
 506
 507func (u *appUpgradeContext) applyStagedFiles() error {
 508	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 509	if err := copyAppDetailMissingTracked(fileOp, u.detailDir, u.original.GetPath(), &u.createdPaths); err != nil {
 510		return err
 511	}
 512	if err := replaceUpgradePath(u.stageDir, u.original.GetPath(), "scripts"); err != nil {
 513		return err
 514	}
 515	if u.original.App.Key == constant.AppOpenclaw {
 516		if err := replaceUpgradePath(u.stageDir, u.original.GetPath(), path.Join("data", "conf", "openclaw.json")); err != nil {
 517			return err
 518		}
 519	}
 520	if u.original.App.Key == constant.AppOpenresty {
 521		for _, relativePath := range []string{
 522			nginxModuleBuildDir,
 523			nginxModuleModulesDir,
 524			path.Join(nginxModuleConfDir, nginxModuleEnabledConfDir),
 525		} {
 526			if err := replaceUpgradePath(u.stageDir, u.original.GetPath(), relativePath); err != nil {
 527				return err
 528			}
 529		}
 530	}
 531	return nil
 532}
 533
 534func (u *appUpgradeContext) rollback(t *task.Task) (rollbackErr error) {
 535	if !u.stopAttempted {
 536		return nil
 537	}
 538	logStr := i18n.GetWithName("AppRecover", u.original.Name)
 539	t.LogStart(logStr)
 540	defer func() {
 541		if rollbackErr != nil {
 542			t.LogFailedWithErr(logStr, rollbackErr)
 543		} else {
 544			t.LogSuccess(logStr)
 545		}
 546	}()
 547	if !u.downAttempted {
 548		if out, err := compose.Operate(u.original.GetComposePath(), "start"); err != nil {
 549			if out != "" {
 550				err = fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", out, err)
 551			}
 552			return err
 553		}
 554		return u.finishRollback()
 555	}
 556	if u.phase < appUpgradeMutated {
 557		if out, err := compose.UpWithoutBuild(u.original.GetComposePath()); err != nil {
 558			if out != "" {
 559				err = fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", out, err)
 560			}
 561			return err
 562		}
 563		return u.finishRollback()
 564	}
 565
 566	if out, err := compose.Down(u.original.GetComposePath()); err != nil {
 567		if out != "" {
 568			err = fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", out, err)
 569		}
 570		rollbackErr = err
 571	}
 572	if u.backupFile != "" {
 573		_ = u.restoreManagedFiles()
 574		if err := handleAppRecover(&u.original, t, u.backupFile, true, "", ""); err != nil {
 575			_, _ = compose.UpWithoutBuild(u.original.GetComposePath())
 576			return errors.Join(rollbackErr, err)
 577		}
 578	} else {
 579		if err := u.restoreManagedFiles(); err != nil {
 580			return errors.Join(rollbackErr, err)
 581		}
 582		if out, err := compose.UpWithoutBuild(u.original.GetComposePath()); err != nil {
 583			if out != "" {
 584				err = fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", out, err)
 585			}
 586			return errors.Join(rollbackErr, err)
 587		}
 588	}
 589	return errors.Join(rollbackErr, u.finishRollback())
 590}
 591
 592func (u *appUpgradeContext) finishRollback() error {
 593	if _, err := waitAppContainersReady(context.Background(), u.original); err != nil {
 594		return err
 595	}
 596	restored := u.original
 597	if err := appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &restored); err != nil {
 598		return err
 599	}
 600	return nil
 601}
 602
 603func (u *appUpgradeContext) restoreManagedFiles() error {
 604	var restoreErr error
 605	if u.snapshot != nil {
 606		restoreErr = u.snapshot.Restore()
 607	}
 608	for index := len(u.createdPaths) - 1; index >= 0; index-- {
 609		if err := os.RemoveAll(u.createdPaths[index]); err != nil {
 610			restoreErr = errors.Join(restoreErr, err)
 611		}
 612	}
 613	return restoreErr
 614}
 615
 616func (u *appUpgradeContext) deleteOldImages(t *task.Task) {
 617	if !u.req.DeleteImage {
 618		return
 619	}
 620	excludeImages, err := docker.GetImagesFromDockerCompose(u.envContent, []byte(u.candidate.DockerCompose))
 621	if err != nil {
 622		t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("TaskDelete")+i18n.GetMsgByKey("Image"), err)
 623		return
 624	}
 625	dockerClient, err := docker.NewClient()
 626	if err != nil {
 627		t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("TaskDelete")+i18n.GetMsgByKey("Image"), err)
 628		return
 629	}
 630	defer dockerClient.Close()
 631	if err = deleteAppImagesByIDs(t, dockerClient, u.oldImageIDs, excludeImages); err != nil {
 632		t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("TaskDelete")+i18n.GetMsgByKey("Image"), err)
 633	}
 634}
 635
 636func (u *appUpgradeContext) cleanup() {
 637	if u.snapshot != nil {
 638		u.snapshot.Cleanup()
 639	}
 640	if u.stageDir != "" {
 641		_ = os.RemoveAll(u.stageDir)
 642	}
 643}
 644
 645"cmt">// upgradeImageClient (interface)
 646type upgradeImageClient interface {
 647	PullImageWithProcess(*task.Task, string) error
 648	ImageExists(string) (bool, error)
 649	Close()
 650}
 651
 652func prepareUpgradeImages(t *task.Task, images []string, pull bool) error {
 653	dockerClient, err := docker.NewClient()
 654	if err != nil {
 655		return err
 656	}
 657	return prepareUpgradeImagesWithClient(t, dockerClient, images, pull)
 658}
 659
 660func prepareUpgradeImagesWithClient(t *task.Task, dockerClient upgradeImageClient, images []string, pull bool) error {
 661	defer dockerClient.Close()
 662	seen := make(map[string]struct{}, len(images))
 663	for _, image := range images {
 664		image = strings.TrimSpace(image)
 665		if image == "" {
 666			continue
 667		}
 668		if _, ok := seen[image]; ok {
 669			continue
 670		}
 671		seen[image] = struct{}{}
 672		if pull {
 673			if t != nil {
 674				t.Log(i18n.GetWithName("PullImageStart", image))
 675			}
 676			if pullErr := dockerClient.PullImageWithProcess(t, image); pullErr != nil {
 677				if exists, _ := dockerClient.ImageExists(image); exists {
 678					if t != nil {
 679						t.Log(i18n.GetMsgByKey("UseExistImage"))
 680					}
 681					continue
 682				}
 683				return buserr.WithNameAndErr("ErrDockerPullImage", "", pullErr)
 684			}
 685		}
 686		exists, inspectErr := dockerClient.ImageExists(image)
 687		if inspectErr != nil || !exists {
 688			return buserr.WithNameAndErr("ErrDockerPullImage", "", fmt.Errorf("image %s is not available locally: %v", image, inspectErr))
 689		}
 690		if pull && t != nil {
 691			t.LogSuccess(i18n.GetMsgByKey("PullImage"))
 692		}
 693	}
 694	return nil
 695}
 696
 697func verifyUpgradeImages(images []string) error {
 698	dockerClient, err := docker.NewClient()
 699	if err != nil {
 700		return err
 701	}
 702	defer dockerClient.Close()
 703	for _, image := range images {
 704		exists, inspectErr := dockerClient.ImageExists(image)
 705		if inspectErr != nil || !exists {
 706			return buserr.WithNameAndErr("ErrDockerPullImage", "", fmt.Errorf("image %s is not available locally: %v", image, inspectErr))
 707		}
 708	}
 709	return nil
 710}
 711
 712func renderUpgradeEnv(install *model.AppInstall, original []byte) ([]byte, error) {
 713	originalEnv := make(map[string]string)
 714	if len(original) > 0 {
 715		var err error
 716		originalEnv, err = godotenv.UnmarshalBytes(original)
 717		if err != nil {
 718			return nil, err
 719		}
 720	}
 721	params := make(map[string]string, len(originalEnv))
 722	maps.Copy(params, originalEnv)
 723	envs := make(map[string]interface{})
 724	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(install.Env), &envs); err != nil {
 725		return nil, err
 726	}
 727	handleMap(envs, params)
 728	if install.App.Key == constant.AppOpenresty {
 729		for _, key := range []string{"CONTAINER_PACKAGE_URL", "RESTY_ADD_PACKAGE_BUILDDEPS", "RESTY_CONFIG_OPTIONS_MORE"} {
 730			if value, ok := originalEnv[key]; ok {
 731				params[key] = value
 732			}
 733		}
 734		if websiteDir := strings.TrimSpace(originalEnv["WEBSITE_DIR"]); websiteDir != "" {
 735			params["WEBSITE_DIR"] = websiteDir
 736		}
 737		websiteDir := strings.TrimSpace(params["WEBSITE_DIR"])
 738		if websiteDir == "" {
 739			websiteDir = NewISettingService().GetWebsiteDir()
 740		}
 741		if !path.IsAbs(websiteDir) {
 742			websiteDir = path.Join(global.Dir.DataDir, websiteDir)
 743		}
 744		params["WEBSITE_DIR"] = websiteDir
 745		envs["WEBSITE_DIR"] = websiteDir
 746		content, marshalErr := json.Marshal(envs)
 747		if marshalErr != nil {
 748			return nil, marshalErr
 749		}
 750		install.Env = string(content)
 751	}
 752	content, err := godotenv.Marshal(params)
 753	if err != nil {
 754		return nil, err
 755	}
 756	return []byte(content), nil
 757}
 758
 759func renderUpgradeCompose(install model.AppInstall, detail model.AppDetail, customCompose string) (string, error) {
 760	if customCompose != "" {
 761		return customCompose, nil
 762	}
 763	if install.App.Key == vllmAppKeyForUpgrade {
 764		return install.DockerCompose, nil
 765	}
 766	return getUpgradeCompose(install, detail)
 767}
 768
 769func writeUpgradeFile(filePath string, content []byte, mode os.FileMode) error {
 770	tmp, err := os.CreateTemp(path.Dir(filePath), "."+path.Base(filePath)+".*")
 771	if err != nil {
 772		return err
 773	}
 774	tmpPath := tmp.Name()
 775	defer os.Remove(tmpPath)
 776	if err = tmp.Chmod(mode); err == nil {
 777		_, err = tmp.Write(content)
 778	}
 779	if err == nil {
 780		err = tmp.Sync()
 781	}
 782	if closeErr := tmp.Close(); err == nil {
 783		err = closeErr
 784	}
 785	if err != nil {
 786		return err
 787	}
 788	return os.Rename(tmpPath, filePath)
 789}
 790
 791func copyUpgradeStageFile(sourceRoot, targetRoot, relativePath string) error {
 792	source := path.Join(sourceRoot, relativePath)
 793	if _, err := os.Stat(source); err != nil {
 794		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
 795			return nil
 796		}
 797		return err
 798	}
 799	target := path.Join(targetRoot, relativePath)
 800	_ = os.RemoveAll(target)
 801	return copyOpenrestyUpgradeSnapshotEntry(source, target)
 802}
 803
 804func replaceUpgradePath(sourceRoot, targetRoot, relativePath string) error {
 805	source := path.Join(sourceRoot, relativePath)
 806	if _, err := os.Stat(source); err != nil {
 807		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
 808			return nil
 809		}
 810		return err
 811	}
 812	target := path.Join(targetRoot, relativePath)
 813	if err := os.RemoveAll(target); err != nil {
 814		return err
 815	}
 816	return copyOpenrestyUpgradeSnapshotEntry(source, target)
 817}
 818
 819func createUpgradeFileSnapshot(installPath string, paths []string) (*upgradeFileSnapshot, error) {
 820	backupPath, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "1panel-app-upgrade-*")
 821	if err != nil {
 822		return nil, err
 823	}
 824	snapshot := &upgradeFileSnapshot{
 825		installPath: installPath,
 826		backupPath:  backupPath,
 827		paths:       paths,
 828		existing:    make(map[string]bool, len(paths)),
 829	}
 830	for _, relativePath := range paths {
 831		source := path.Join(installPath, relativePath)
 832		if _, err = os.Stat(source); err != nil {
 833			if os.IsNotExist(err) {
 834				continue
 835			}
 836			snapshot.Cleanup()
 837			return nil, err
 838		}
 839		snapshot.existing[relativePath] = true
 840		if err = copyOpenrestyUpgradeSnapshotEntry(source, path.Join(backupPath, relativePath)); err != nil {
 841			snapshot.Cleanup()
 842			return nil, err
 843		}
 844	}
 845	return snapshot, nil
 846}
 847
 848func (s *upgradeFileSnapshot) Restore() error {
 849	for _, relativePath := range s.paths {
 850		target := path.Join(s.installPath, relativePath)
 851		if err := os.RemoveAll(target); err != nil {
 852			return err
 853		}
 854		if !s.existing[relativePath] {
 855			continue
 856		}
 857		if err := copyOpenrestyUpgradeSnapshotEntry(path.Join(s.backupPath, relativePath), target); err != nil {
 858			return err
 859		}
 860	}
 861	return nil
 862}
 863
 864func (s *upgradeFileSnapshot) Cleanup() {
 865	if s != nil && s.backupPath != "" {
 866		_ = os.RemoveAll(s.backupPath)
 867	}
 868}

 871type appContainerReadinessClient interface {
 872	ContainerList(context.Context, container.ListOptions) ([]container.Summary, error)
 873	ContainerInspect(context.Context, string) (container.InspectResponse, error)
 874}
 875
 876func waitAppContainersReady(ctx context.Context, install model.AppInstall) ([]string, error) {
 877	client, err := docker.NewDockerClient()
 878	if err != nil {
 879		return nil, err
 880	}
 881	defer client.Close()
 882	return waitAppContainersReadyWithClient(ctx, client, install)
 883}
 884
 885func waitAppContainersReadyWithClient(ctx context.Context, client appContainerReadinessClient, install model.AppInstall) ([]string, error) {
 886	envContent, err := os.ReadFile(install.GetEnvPath())
 887	if err != nil {
 888		envContent, err = renderUpgradeEnv(&install, nil)
 889		if err != nil {
 890			return nil, err
 891		}
 892	}
 893	project, err := docker.GetComposeProject(install.Name, install.GetPath(), []byte(install.DockerCompose), envContent, false)
 894	if err != nil {
 895		return nil, err
 896	}
 897	expectedServices := make(map[string]struct{})
 898	for _, service := range project.Services {
 899		if !skipCheckStatus(service) {
 900			expectedServices[service.Name] = struct{}{}
 901		}
 902	}
 903	if len(expectedServices) == 0 {
 904		return strings.Split(install.ContainerName, ","), nil
 905	}
 906	options := container.ListOptions{
 907		All: true,
 908		Filters: filters.NewArgs(
 909			filters.Arg("label", composeWorkdirLabel+"="+install.GetPath()),
 910		),
 911	}
 912	containers, err := client.ContainerList(ctx, options)
 913	if err != nil {
 914		return nil, err
 915	}
 916	foundServices := make(map[string]bool, len(expectedServices))
 917	containerNames := make([]string, 0, len(containers))
 918	for _, item := range containers {
 919		serviceName := item.Labels[composeServiceLabel]
 920		if _, ok := expectedServices[serviceName]; !ok {
 921			continue
 922		}
 923		if err = waitContainerReady(ctx, client, item.ID); err != nil {
 924			return nil, fmt.Errorf("container %s is not ready: %w", serviceName, err)
 925		}
 926		foundServices[serviceName] = true
 927		if len(item.Names) > 0 {
 928			containerNames = append(containerNames, strings.TrimPrefix(item.Names[0], "/"))
 929		}
 930	}
 931	for serviceName := range expectedServices {
 932		if !foundServices[serviceName] {
 933			return nil, fmt.Errorf("container for service %s was not created", serviceName)
 934		}
 935	}
 936	sort.Strings(containerNames)
 937	return containerNames, nil
 938}

  64func checkPort(key string, params map[string]interface{}) (int, error) {
  65	port, ok := params[key]
  66	if ok {
  67		portN := 0
  68		var err error
  69		switch p := port.(type) {
  70		case string:
  71			portN, err = strconv.Atoi(p)
  72			if err != nil {
  73				return portN, nil
  74			}
  75		case float64:
  76			portN = int(math.Ceil(p))
  77		case int:
  78			portN = p
  79		}
  80
  81		oldInstalled, _ := appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background(), appInstallRepo.WithPort(portN))
  82		if len(oldInstalled) > 0 {
  83			var apps []string
  84			for _, install := range oldInstalled {
  85				apps = append(apps, install.App.Name)
  86			}
  87			return portN, buserr.WithMap("ErrPortInOtherApp", map[string]interface{}{"port": portN, "apps": apps}, nil)
  88		}
  89		if common.ScanPort(portN) {
  90			return portN, buserr.WithDetail("ErrPortInUsed", portN, nil)
  91		} else {
  92			return portN, nil
  93		}
  94	}
  95	return 0, nil
  96}
  97
  98func isPortInUse(port int, protocol string) bool {
  99	return common.ScanPortWithProto(port, normalizeComposeProtocol(protocol))
 100}
 101
 102func checkPortExist(port int) error {
 103	return checkPortExistWithProtocol(port, "")
 104}
 105
 106func checkPortExistWithProtocol(port int, protocol string) error {
 107	errMap := make(map[string]interface{})
 108	errMap["port"] = port
 109	appInstall, _ := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(appInstallRepo.WithPort(port))
 110	if appInstall.ID > 0 {
 111		errMap["type"] = i18n.GetMsgByKey("TYPE_APP")
 112		errMap["name"] = appInstall.Name
 113		return buserr.WithMap("ErrPortExist", errMap, nil)
 114	}
 115	runtime, _ := runtimeRepo.GetFirst(context.Background(), runtimeRepo.WithPort(port))
 116	if runtime != nil {
 117		errMap["type"] = i18n.GetMsgByKey("TYPE_RUNTIME")
 118		errMap["name"] = runtime.Name
 119		return buserr.WithMap("ErrPortExist", errMap, nil)
 120	}
 121	domain, _ := websiteDomainRepo.GetFirst(websiteDomainRepo.WithPort(port))
 122	if domain.ID > 0 {
 123		errMap["type"] = i18n.GetMsgByKey("TYPE_DOMAIN")
 124		errMap["name"] = domain.Domain
 125		return buserr.WithMap("ErrPortExist", errMap, nil)
 126	}
 127	if isPortInUse(port, protocol) {
 128		return buserr.WithDetail("ErrPortInUsed", port, nil)
 129	}
 130	return nil
 131}

 133var DatabaseKeys = map[string]uint{
 134	constant.AppMysql:             3306,
 135	constant.AppMariaDB:           3306,
 136	constant.AppPostgresql:        5432,
 137	constant.AppPostgres:          5432,
 138	constant.AppMongodb:           27017,
 139	constant.AppRedis:             6379,
 140	constant.AppMemcached:         11211,
 141	constant.AppMysqlCluster:      3306,
 142	constant.AppPostgresqlCluster: 5432,
 143	constant.AppRedisCluster:      6379,
 144}

 146var ToolKeys = map[string]uint{
 147	"minio": 9001,
 148}
