 150func CreateDB(ctx context.Context, app model.App, appInstall *model.AppInstall, params map[string]interface{}) error {
 151	database := &model.Database{
 152		AppInstallID: appInstall.ID,
 153		Name:         appInstall.Name,
 154		Type:         app.Key,
 155		Version:      appInstall.Version,
 156		From:         "local",
 157		Address:      appInstall.ServiceName,
 158		Port:         DatabaseKeys[app.Key],
 159	}
 160	detail, err := appDetailRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(appInstall.AppDetailId))
 161	if err != nil {
 162		return err
 163	}
 164	formFields := &dto.AppForm{}
 165	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(detail.Params), formFields); err != nil {
 166		return err
 167	}
 168	for _, form := range formFields.FormFields {
 169		if form.EnvKey == "PANEL_APP_PORT_HTTP" {
 170			portFloat, ok := form.Default.(float64)
 171			if ok {
 172				database.Port = uint(int(portFloat))
 173			}
 174			break
 175		}
 176	}
 177
 178	switch app.Key {
 179	case constant.AppMysql, constant.AppMariaDB, constant.AppPostgresql, constant.AppMongodb, constant.AppMysqlCluster, constant.AppPostgresqlCluster:
 180		if password, ok := params["PANEL_DB_ROOT_PASSWORD"]; ok {
 181			if password != "" {
 182				database.Password = password.(string)
 183				if app.Key == "mysql" || app.Key == "mariadb" || app.Key == constant.AppMysqlCluster {
 184					database.Username = "root"
 185				}
 186				if rootUser, ok := params["PANEL_DB_ROOT_USER"]; ok {
 187					database.Username = rootUser.(string)
 188				}
 189				authParam := dto.AuthParam{
 190					RootPassword: password.(string),
 191					RootUser:     database.Username,
 192				}
 193				authByte, err := json.Marshal(authParam)
 194				if err != nil {
 195					return err
 196				}
 197				appInstall.Param = string(authByte)
 198
 199			}
 200		}
 201	case constant.AppRedis, constant.AppRedisCluster:
 202		if password, ok := params["PANEL_REDIS_ROOT_PASSWORD"]; ok {
 203			authParam := dto.RedisAuthParam{
 204				RootPassword: "",
 205			}
 206			if password != "" {
 207				authParam.RootPassword = password.(string)
 208				database.Password = password.(string)
 209			}
 210			authByte, err := json.Marshal(authParam)
 211			if err != nil {
 212				return err
 213			}
 214			appInstall.Param = string(authByte)
 215		}
 216	}
 217	return databaseRepo.Create(ctx, database)
 218}
 219
 220func createLink(ctx context.Context, installTask *task.Task, app model.App, appInstall *model.AppInstall, params map[string]interface{}) error {
 221	deleteAppLink := func(t *task.Task) {
 222		del := dto.DelAppLink{
 223			Ctx:         ctx,
 224			Install:     appInstall,
 225			ForceDelete: true,
 226		}
 227		_ = deleteLink(del)
 228	}
 229	if DatabaseKeys[app.Key] > 0 {
 230		handleDataBaseApp := func(task *task.Task) error {
 231			return CreateDB(ctx, app, appInstall, params)
 232		}
 233		installTask.AddSubTask(i18n.GetMsgByKey("HandleDatabaseApp"), handleDataBaseApp, deleteAppLink)
 234	}
 235	if ToolKeys[app.Key] > 0 {
 236		if app.Key == "minio" {
 237			authParam := dto.MinioAuthParam{}
 238			if password, ok := params["PANEL_MINIO_ROOT_PASSWORD"]; ok {
 239				authParam.RootPassword = password.(string)
 240			}
 241			if rootUser, ok := params["PANEL_MINIO_ROOT_USER"]; ok {
 242				authParam.RootUser = rootUser.(string)
 243			}
 244			authByte, err := json.Marshal(authParam)
 245			if err != nil {
 246				return err
 247			}
 248			appInstall.Param = string(authByte)
 249		}
 250	}
 251
 252	var dbConfig dto.AppDatabase
 253	if app.Type == "website" || app.Type == "tool" {
 254		paramByte, err := json.Marshal(params)
 255		if err != nil {
 256			return err
 257		}
 258		if err = json.Unmarshal(paramByte, &dbConfig); err != nil {
 259			return err
 260		}
 261	}
 262
 263	if !reflect.DeepEqual(dbConfig, dto.AppDatabase{}) && dbConfig.ServiceName != "" {
 264		createAppDataBase := func(rootTask *task.Task) error {
 265			hostName := params["PANEL_DB_HOST_NAME"]
 266			if hostName == nil || hostName.(string) == "" {
 267				return nil
 268			}
 269			database, _ := databaseRepo.Get(repo.WithByName(hostName.(string)))
 270			if database.ID == 0 {
 271				return nil
 272			}
 273			var resourceId uint
 274			if dbConfig.DbName != "" && dbConfig.DbUser != "" && dbConfig.Password != "" {
 275				switch database.Type {
 276				case constant.AppPostgresql, constant.AppPostgres, constant.AppPostgresqlCluster:
 277					oldPostgresqlDb, _ := postgresqlRepo.Get(repo.WithByName(dbConfig.DbName), repo.WithByFrom(constant.ResourceLocal))
 278					if oldPostgresqlDb.ID == 0 {
 279						oldPostgresqlDb, _ = postgresqlRepo.Get(repo.WithByName(dbConfig.DbName), postgresqlRepo.WithByPostgresqlName(dbConfig.DatabaseName), repo.WithByFrom(constant.AppResourceRemote))
 280					}
 281					resourceId = oldPostgresqlDb.ID
 282					if oldPostgresqlDb.ID > 0 {
 283						if oldPostgresqlDb.Username != dbConfig.DbUser || oldPostgresqlDb.Password != dbConfig.Password {
 284							return buserr.New("ErrDbUserNotValid")
 285						}
 286					} else {
 287						var createPostgresql dto.PostgresqlDBCreate
 288						createPostgresql.Name = dbConfig.DbName
 289						createPostgresql.Username = dbConfig.DbUser
 290						createPostgresql.Database = database.Name
 291						createPostgresql.Format = "UTF8"
 292						createPostgresql.Password = dbConfig.Password
 293						createPostgresql.From = database.From
 294						createPostgresql.SuperUser = true
 295						pgdb, err := NewIPostgresqlService().Create(ctx, createPostgresql)
 296						if err != nil {
 297							return err
 298						}
 299						resourceId = pgdb.ID
 300					}
 301				case constant.AppMysql, constant.AppMariaDB, constant.AppMysqlCluster:
 302					oldMysqlDb, _ := mysqlRepo.Get(repo.WithByName(dbConfig.DbName), repo.WithByFrom(constant.ResourceLocal))
 303					if oldMysqlDb.ID == 0 {
 304						oldMysqlDb, _ = mysqlRepo.Get(repo.WithByName(dbConfig.DbName), mysqlRepo.WithByMysqlName(dbConfig.DatabaseName), repo.WithByFrom(constant.AppResourceRemote))
 305					}
 306					resourceId = oldMysqlDb.ID
 307					if oldMysqlDb.ID > 0 {
 308						if err := ensureAppMysqlDBUser(database, dbConfig); err != nil {
 309							return err
 310						}
 311					} else {
 312						var createMysql dto.MysqlDBCreate
 313						createMysql.Name = dbConfig.DbName
 314						createMysql.Username = dbConfig.DbUser
 315						createMysql.Database = database.Name
 316						createMysql.Format = dbConfig.Format
 317						createMysql.Permission = "%"
 318						createMysql.Password = dbConfig.Password
 319						createMysql.From = database.From
 320						createMysql.Collation = dbConfig.Collation
 321						mysqldb, err := NewIMysqlService().Create(ctx, createMysql)
 322						if err != nil {
 323							return err
 324						}
 325						resourceId = mysqldb.ID
 326					}
 327				}
 328			}
 329			var installResource model.AppInstallResource
 330			installResource.ResourceId = resourceId
 331			installResource.AppInstallId = appInstall.ID
 332			if database.AppInstallID > 0 {
 333				installResource.LinkId = database.AppInstallID
 334			} else {
 335				installResource.LinkId = database.ID
 336			}
 337			installResource.Key = database.Type
 338			installResource.From = database.From
 339			return appInstallResourceRepo.Create(ctx, &installResource)
 340		}
 341		installTask.AddSubTask(task.GetTaskName(dbConfig.DbName, task.TaskCreate, task.TaskScopeDatabase), createAppDataBase, deleteAppLink)
 342	}
 343	return nil
 344}
 345
 346func deleteAppInstall(deleteReq request.AppInstallDelete) error {
 347	install := deleteReq.Install
 348	op := files.NewFileOp()
 349	appDir := install.GetPath()
 350
 351	uninstallTask, err := task.NewTaskWithOps(install.Name, task.TaskUninstall, task.TaskScopeApp, deleteReq.TaskID, install.ID)
 352	if err != nil {
 353		return err
 354	}
 355
 356	uninstall := func(t *task.Task) error {
 357		install.Status = constant.StatusUninstalling
 358		_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &install)
 359		dir, _ := os.Stat(appDir)
 360		if dir != nil {
 361			logStr := i18n.GetMsgByKey("Stop") + i18n.GetMsgByKey("App")
 362			t.Log(logStr)
 363
 364			out, err := compose.Down(install.GetComposePath())
 365			if err != nil && !deleteReq.ForceDelete {
 366				return handleErr(install, err, out)
 367			}
 368			t.LogSuccess(logStr)
 369			if err = runScript(t, &install, "uninstall"); err != nil {
 370				_, _ = compose.Up(install.GetComposePath())
 371				return err
 372			}
 373			if deleteReq.DeleteImage {
 374				content, err := op.GetContent(install.GetEnvPath())
 375				if err != nil {
 376					return err
 377				}
 378				if err = deleteAppImagesByCompose(t, content, []byte(install.DockerCompose), nil); err != nil {
 379					return err
 380				}
 381			}
 382		}
 383		tx, ctx := helper.GetTxAndContext()
 384		defer tx.Rollback()
 385		if err = appInstallRepo.Delete(ctx, install); err != nil {
 386			return err
 387		}
 388		appKey := install.App.Key
 389		if isAgentAppKey(appKey) {
 390			_ = agentRepo.DeleteByAppInstallIDWithCtx(ctx, install.ID)
 391		}
 392
 393		resources, _ := appInstallResourceRepo.GetBy(appInstallResourceRepo.WithAppInstallId(install.ID))
 394		if len(resources) > 0 {
 395			if deleteReq.DeleteDB {
 396				del := dto.DelAppLink{
 397					Ctx:         ctx,
 398					Install:     &install,
 399					ForceDelete: deleteReq.ForceDelete,
 400					Task:        uninstallTask,
 401				}
 402				t.LogWithOps(task.TaskDelete, i18n.GetMsgByKey("Database"))
 403				if err = deleteLink(del); err != nil {
 404					t.LogFailedWithOps(task.TaskDelete, i18n.GetMsgByKey("Database"), err)
 405					if !deleteReq.ForceDelete {
 406						return err
 407					}
 408				}
 409				t.LogSuccessWithOps(task.TaskDelete, i18n.GetMsgByKey("Database"))
 410			} else {
 411				_ = appInstallResourceRepo.DeleteBy(ctx, appInstallResourceRepo.WithAppInstallId(install.ID))
 412			}
 413		}
 414
 415		if DatabaseKeys[install.App.Key] > 0 {
 416			_ = databaseRepo.Delete(ctx, databaseRepo.WithAppInstallID(install.ID))
 417		}
 418
 419		switch install.App.Key {
 420		case constant.AppMysql, constant.AppMariaDB, constant.AppMysqlCluster:
 421			if err = databaseUserGrantRepo.DeleteBy(
 422				ctx,
 423				repo.WithByType(install.App.Key),
 424				databaseUserGrantRepo.WithByDatabase(install.Name),
 425			); err != nil {
 426				return err
 427			}
 428			if err = databaseUserRepo.DeleteBy(
 429				ctx,
 430				repo.WithByType(install.App.Key),
 431				databaseUserRepo.WithByDatabase(install.Name),
 432			); err != nil {
 433				return err
 434			}
 435			_ = mysqlRepo.Delete(ctx, mysqlRepo.WithByMysqlName(install.Name))
 436		case constant.AppMongodb:
 437			_ = mongodbRepo.Delete(ctx, mongodbRepo.WithByMongodbName(install.Name))
 438		case constant.AppPostgresql, constant.AppPostgresqlCluster:
 439			_ = postgresqlRepo.Delete(ctx, postgresqlRepo.WithByPostgresqlName(install.Name))
 440		}
 441
 442		_ = backupRepo.DeleteRecord(ctx, repo.WithByType("app"), repo.WithByName(install.App.Key), repo.WithByDetailName(install.Name))
 443		uploadDir := path.Join(global.Dir.BaseDir, fmt.Sprintf("1panel/uploads/app/%s/%s", install.App.Key, install.Name))
 444		if _, err := os.Stat(uploadDir); err == nil {
 445			_ = os.RemoveAll(uploadDir)
 446		}
 447		if deleteReq.DeleteBackup {
 448			backupDir := path.Join(global.Dir.LocalBackupDir, fmt.Sprintf("app/%s/%s", install.App.Key, install.Name))
 449			if _, err = os.Stat(backupDir); err == nil {
 450				t.LogWithOps(task.TaskDelete, i18n.GetMsgByKey("TaskBackup"))
 451				_ = os.RemoveAll(backupDir)
 452				t.LogSuccessWithOps(task.TaskDelete, i18n.GetMsgByKey("TaskBackup"))
 453			}
 454		}
 455		_ = op.DeleteDir(appDir)
 456		parentDir := filepath.Dir(appDir)
 457		entries, err := os.ReadDir(parentDir)
 458		if err == nil && len(entries) == 0 {
 459			_ = op.DeleteDir(parentDir)
 460		}
 461		tx.Commit()
 462
 463		existApps, _ := appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background(), appInstallRepo.WithAppId(install.AppId))
 464		if len(existApps) == 0 {
 465			_ = appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.Delete(appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.WithAppID(install.AppId))
 466		}
 467		return nil
 468	}
 469	uninstallTask.AddSubTask(task.GetTaskName(install.Name, task.TaskUninstall, task.TaskScopeApp), uninstall, nil)
 470	go func() {
 471		if err := uninstallTask.Execute(); err != nil && !deleteReq.ForceDelete {
 472			install.Status = constant.StatusError
 473			_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &install)
 474		}
 475	}()
 476	return nil
 477}
 478
 479"cmt">// appImageID (struct)
 480type appImageID struct {
 481	name string
 482	id   string
 483}
 484
 485func getAppImageIDsByCompose(client docker.Client, envContent, composeContent []byte) ([]appImageID, error) {
 486	images, err := docker.GetImagesFromDockerCompose(envContent, composeContent)
 487	if err != nil {
 488		return nil, err
 489	}
 490	imageIDs := make([]appImageID, 0, len(images))
 491	for _, image := range images {
 492		imageID, err := client.GetImageIDByName(image)
 493		if err == nil && imageID != "" {
 494			imageIDs = append(imageIDs, appImageID{name: image, id: imageID})
 495		}
 496	}
 497	return imageIDs, nil
 498}
 499
 500func deleteAppImagesByCompose(t *task.Task, envContent, composeContent []byte, excludeImages []string) error {
 501	client, err := docker.NewClient()
 502	if err != nil {
 503		return err
 504	}
 505	defer client.Close()
 506
 507	imageIDs, err := getAppImageIDsByCompose(client, envContent, composeContent)
 508	if err != nil {
 509		return err
 510	}
 511	return deleteAppImagesByIDs(t, client, imageIDs, excludeImages)
 512}
 513
 514func deleteAppImagesByIDs(t *task.Task, client docker.Client, imageIDs []appImageID, excludeImages []string) error {
 515	delImageStr := i18n.GetMsgByKey("TaskDelete") + i18n.GetMsgByKey("Image")
 516	excludeImageIDs := make(map[string]struct{}, len(excludeImages))
 517	for _, image := range excludeImages {
 518		imageID, err := client.GetImageIDByName(image)
 519		if err == nil && imageID != "" {
 520			excludeImageIDs[imageID] = struct{}{}
 521		}
 522	}
 523	deletedImageIDs := make(map[string]struct{}, len(imageIDs))
 524	for _, image := range imageIDs {
 525		if _, ok := excludeImageIDs[image.id]; ok {
 526			continue
 527		}
 528		if _, ok := deletedImageIDs[image.id]; ok {
 529			continue
 530		}
 531		deletedImageIDs[image.id] = struct{}{}
 532		imgStr := delImageStr + image.name
 533		t.Log(imgStr)
 534		if err := client.DeleteImage(image.id); err != nil {
 535			t.LogFailedWithErr(imgStr, err)
 536			continue
 537		}
 538		t.LogSuccess(imgStr)
 539	}
 540	return nil
 541}
 542
 543func ensureAppMysqlDBUser(database model.Database, dbConfig dto.AppDatabase) error {
 544	const host = "%"
 545	mysqlService := NewIMysqlService()
 546	users, err := mysqlService.ListUsers(dto.MysqlUserSearch{Database: database.Name})
 547	if err != nil {
 548		return err
 549	}
 550	userExists := false
 551	passwordValid := false
 552	for _, user := range users {
 553		if user.Username != dbConfig.DbUser || user.Host != host || user.IsDelete {
 554			continue
 555		}
 556		userExists = true
 557		passwordValid = user.Password == dbConfig.Password
 558		break
 559	}
 560	if !userExists || !passwordValid {
 561		return buserr.New("ErrDbUserNotValid")
 562	}
 563
 564	grants, err := mysqlService.ListGrants(dto.MysqlUserSearch{Database: database.Name})
 565	if err != nil {
 566		return err
 567	}
 568	for _, grant := range grants {
 569		if grant.Database == dbConfig.DbName && grant.Username == dbConfig.DbUser && grant.Host == host {
 570			return nil
 571		}
 572	}
 573	return mysqlService.GrantUser(dto.MysqlGrantCreate{
 574		Database: database.Name,
 575		DB:       dbConfig.DbName,
 576		Username: dbConfig.DbUser,
 577		Host:     host,
 578	})
 579}
 580
 581func deleteLink(del dto.DelAppLink) error {
 582	install := del.Install
 583	resources, _ := appInstallResourceRepo.GetBy(appInstallResourceRepo.WithAppInstallId(install.ID))
 584	if len(resources) == 0 {
 585		return nil
 586	}
 587	for _, re := range resources {
 588		switch re.Key {
 589		case constant.AppMysql, constant.AppMariaDB:
 590			database, _ := mysqlRepo.Get(repo.WithByID(re.ResourceId))
 591			if reflect.DeepEqual(database, model.DatabaseMysql{}) {
 592				continue
 593			}
 594			if err := deleteMysqlDatabaseForResourceOwner(del.Ctx, dto.MysqlDBDelete{
 595				ID:           database.ID,
 596				ForceDelete:  del.ForceDelete,
 597				DeleteBackup: true,
 598				Type:         re.Key,
 599				Database:     database.MysqlName,
 600			}, dto.DBResource{Type: constant.TypeApp, Name: install.Name}); err != nil {
 601				if isMysqlDatabaseResourceInUseError(err) || !del.ForceDelete {
 602					return err
 603				}
 604			}
 605		case constant.AppPostgresql:
 606			pgsqlService := NewIPostgresqlService()
 607			database, _ := postgresqlRepo.Get(repo.WithByID(re.ResourceId))
 608			if reflect.DeepEqual(database, model.DatabasePostgresql{}) {
 609				continue
 610			}
 611			if err := pgsqlService.Delete(del.Ctx, dto.PostgresqlDBDelete{
 612				ID:           database.ID,
 613				ForceDelete:  del.ForceDelete,
 614				DeleteBackup: true,
 615				Type:         re.Key,
 616				Database:     database.PostgresqlName,
 617			}); err != nil {
 618				return err
 619			}
 620		}
 621	}
 622	return appInstallResourceRepo.DeleteBy(del.Ctx, appInstallResourceRepo.WithAppInstallId(install.ID))
 623}
 624
 625func handleUpgradeCompose(install model.AppInstall, detail model.AppDetail) (map[string]interface{}, error) {
 626	composeMap := make(map[string]interface{})
 627	if err := yaml.Unmarshal([]byte(detail.DockerCompose), &composeMap); err != nil {
 628		return nil, err
 629	}
 630	value, ok := composeMap["services"]
 631	if !ok || value == nil {
 632		return nil, buserr.New("ErrFileParse")
 633	}
 634	servicesMap := value.(map[string]interface{})
 635	if len(servicesMap) == 1 {
 636		index := 0
 637		oldServiceName := ""
 638		for k := range servicesMap {
 639			oldServiceName = k
 640			index++
 641			if index > 0 {
 642				break
 643			}
 644		}
 645		servicesMap[install.ServiceName] = servicesMap[oldServiceName]
 646		if install.ServiceName != oldServiceName {
 647			delete(servicesMap, oldServiceName)
 648		}
 649	}
 650	serviceValue := servicesMap[install.ServiceName].(map[string]interface{})
 651
 652	oldComposeMap := make(map[string]interface{})
 653	if err := yaml.Unmarshal([]byte(install.DockerCompose), &oldComposeMap); err != nil {
 654		return nil, err
 655	}
 656	oldValue, ok := oldComposeMap["services"]
 657	if !ok || oldValue == nil {
 658		return nil, buserr.New("ErrFileParse")
 659	}
 660	oldValueMap, ok := oldValue.(map[string]interface{})
 661	if !ok {
 662		return nil, buserr.New("ErrFileParse")
 663	}
 664	oldServiceValueInterface, ok := oldValueMap[install.ServiceName]
 665	if !ok || oldServiceValueInterface == nil {
 666		return nil, buserr.New("ErrFileParse")
 667	}
 668	oldServiceValue, ok := oldServiceValueInterface.(map[string]interface{})
 669	if !ok {
 670		return nil, buserr.New("ErrFileParse")
 671	}
 672	if oldServiceValue["deploy"] != nil {
 673		serviceValue["deploy"] = oldServiceValue["deploy"]
 674	}
 675	if oldServiceValue["restart"] != nil {
 676		serviceValue["restart"] = oldServiceValue["restart"]
 677	}
 678	if install.App.Key == constant.AppOpenresty {
 679		mergeOpenrestyModuleVolumes(serviceValue, oldServiceValue)
 680	}
 681	servicesMap[install.ServiceName] = serviceValue
 682	composeMap["services"] = servicesMap
 683	return composeMap, nil
 684}
 685
 686"cmt">// mergeOpenrestyModuleVolumes carries the dynamic module mounts of the old
 687"cmt">// compose over to the upgraded one when it does not declare them, so built
 688"cmt">// module artifacts and their load configuration stay mounted across upgrades.
 689func mergeOpenrestyModuleVolumes(serviceValue, oldServiceValue map[string]interface{}) {
 690	oldVolumes, ok := oldServiceValue["volumes"].([]interface{})
 691	if !ok {
 692		return
 693	}
 694	newVolumes, _ := serviceValue["volumes"].([]interface{})
 695	existing := make(map[string]struct{}, len(newVolumes))
 696	for _, volume := range newVolumes {
 697		if containerPath, ok := composeVolumeContainerPath(volume); ok {
 698			existing[containerPath] = struct{}{}
 699		}
 700	}
 701	for _, volume := range oldVolumes {
 702		containerPath, ok := composeVolumeContainerPath(volume)
 703		if !ok {
 704			continue
 705		}
 706		if !strings.Contains(containerPath, nginxModuleEnabledConfDir) && !strings.Contains(containerPath, "nginx/modules/1panel") {
 707			continue
 708		}
 709		if _, ok = existing[containerPath]; ok {
 710			continue
 711		}
 712		newVolumes = append(newVolumes, volume)
 713		existing[containerPath] = struct{}{}
 714	}
 715	serviceValue["volumes"] = newVolumes
 716}
 717
 718func composeVolumeContainerPath(volume interface{}) (string, bool) {
 719	volumeStr, ok := volume.(string)
 720	if !ok {
 721		return "", false
 722	}
 723	parts := strings.Split(volumeStr, ":")
 724	if len(parts) < 2 {
 725		return "", false
 726	}
 727	return parts[1], true
 728}
 729
 730func getUpgradeCompose(install model.AppInstall, detail model.AppDetail) (string, error) {
 731	if detail.DockerCompose == "" {
 732		return "", nil
 733	}
 734	composeMap, err := handleUpgradeCompose(install, detail)
 735	if err != nil {
 736		return "", err
 737	}
 738	envs := make(map[string]interface{})
 739	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(install.Env), &envs); err != nil {
 740		return "", err
 741	}
 742	config := getAppCommonConfig(envs)
 743	if config.ContainerName == "" {
 744		config.ContainerName = install.ContainerName
 745		envs[constant.ContainerName] = install.ContainerName
 746	}
 747	config.Advanced = true
 748	if err := addDockerComposeCommonParam(composeMap, install.ServiceName, config, envs); err != nil {
 749		return "", err
 750	}
 751	paramByte, err := json.Marshal(envs)
 752	if err != nil {
 753		return "", err
 754	}
 755	install.Env = string(paramByte)
 756	composeByte, err := yaml.Marshal(composeMap)
 757	if err != nil {
 758		return "", err
 759	}
 760	return string(composeByte), nil
 761}
 762
 763func buildNginx(parentTask *task.Task, nginxInstall model.AppInstall, catalogPath string) error {
 764	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 765	buildPath := path.Join(nginxInstall.GetPath(), nginxModuleBuildDir)
 766	if !fileOp.Stat(buildPath) {
 767		return buserr.New("ErrBuildDirNotFound")
 768	}
 769	modules, err := loadNginxModulesWithCatalog(nginxInstall, catalogPath)
 770	if err != nil {
 771		return err
 772	}
 773	previousModules := cloneNginxModules(modules)
 774	staticBuild := hasEnabledStaticNginxModules(modules)
 775	if err = configureStaticNginxModules(nginxInstall, modules, ""); err != nil {
 776		return err
 777	}
 778	if staticBuild {
 779		logStr := fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", i18n.GetMsgByKey("TaskBuild"), i18n.GetMsgByKey("Image"))
 780		parentTask.LogStart(logStr)
 781		cmdMgr := cmd.NewCommandMgr(cmd.WithTask(*parentTask), cmd.WithTimeout(120*time.Minute))
 782		if err = cmdMgr.Run("docker", "compose", "-f", nginxInstall.GetComposePath(), "build"); err != nil {
 783			return err
 784		}
 785		parentTask.LogSuccess(logStr)
 786	}
 787	modules, err = buildDynamicNginxModules(nginxInstall, modules, nil, false, "", catalogPath, parentTask)
 788	if err != nil {
 789		return err
 790	}
 791	return commitNginxModuleBuilds(nginxInstall, previousModules, modules, false, catalogPath)
 792}
 793
 794func skipCheckStatus(service types.ServiceConfig) bool {
 795	for key := range service.Labels {
 796		if key == "skipStatusCheck" {
 797			return true
 798		}
 799	}
 800	return false
 801}
 802
 803func getContainerNames(install model.AppInstall) ([]string, error) {
 804	envStr, err := coverEnvJsonToStr(install.Env)
 805	if err != nil {
 806		return nil, err
 807	}
 808	project, err := docker.GetComposeProject(install.Name, install.GetPath(), []byte(install.DockerCompose), []byte(envStr), true)
 809	if err != nil {
 810		return nil, err
 811	}
 812	containerMap := make(map[string]struct{})
 813	for _, service := range project.AllServices() {
 814		if service.ContainerName == "${CONTAINER_NAME}" || service.ContainerName == "" || skipCheckStatus(service) {
 815			continue
 816		}
 817		containerMap[service.ContainerName] = struct{}{}
 818	}
 819	var containerNames []string
 820	for k := range containerMap {
 821		containerNames = append(containerNames, k)
 822	}
 823	if len(containerNames) == 0 {
 824		containerNames = append(containerNames, install.ContainerName)
 825	}
 826	return containerNames, nil
 827}
 828
 829func coverEnvJsonToStr(envJson string) (string, error) {
 830	envMap := make(map[string]interface{})
 831	_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(envJson), &envMap)
 832	newEnvMap := make(map[string]string, len(envMap))
 833	handleMap(envMap, newEnvMap)
 834	envStr, err := gotenv.Marshal(newEnvMap)
 835	if err != nil {
 836		return "", err
 837	}
 838	return envStr, nil
 839}

 841func checkLimit(app model.App) error {
 842	if app.Limit > 0 {
 843		installs, err := appInstallRepo.ListBy(context.Background(), appInstallRepo.WithAppId(app.ID))
 844		if err != nil {
 845			return err
 846		}
 847		if len(installs) >= app.Limit {
 848			return buserr.New("ErrAppLimit")
 849		}
 850	}
 851	return nil
 852}
 853
 854func checkRequiredAndLimit(app model.App) error {
 855	if err := checkLimit(app); err != nil {
 856		return err
 857	}
 858	return nil
 859}
 860
 861func handleMap(params map[string]interface{}, envParams map[string]string) {
 862	for k, v := range params {
 863		switch t := v.(type) {
 864		case string:
 865			envParams[k] = t
 866		case float64:
 867			envParams[k] = strconv.FormatFloat(t, 'f', -1, 32)
 868		case uint:
 869			envParams[k] = strconv.Itoa(int(t))
 870		case int:
 871			envParams[k] = strconv.Itoa(t)
 872		case []interface{}:
 873			strArray := make([]string, len(t))
 874			for i := range t {
 875				strArray[i] = strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprintf("%v", t[i]))
 876			}
 877			envParams[k] = strings.Join(strArray, ",")
 878		case map[string]interface{}:
 879			handleMap(t, envParams)
 880		}
 881	}
 882}
 883
 884func downloadApp(app model.App, appDetail model.AppDetail, appInstall *model.AppInstall, logger *logrus.Logger) (err error) {
 885	if app.IsLocalApp() || app.IsCustomApp() {
 886		return nil
 887	}
 888	appResourceDir := path.Join(global.Dir.AppResourceDir, app.Resource)
 889	appDownloadDir := app.GetAppResourcePath()
 890	appVersionDir := path.Join(appDownloadDir, appDetail.Version)
 891	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 892	if !appDetail.Update && fileOp.Stat(appVersionDir) {
 893		return
 894	}
 895	if !fileOp.Stat(appDownloadDir) {
 896		_ = fileOp.CreateDir(appDownloadDir, constant.DirPerm)
 897	}
 898	if !fileOp.Stat(appVersionDir) {
 899		_ = fileOp.CreateDir(appVersionDir, constant.DirPerm)
 900	}
 901	if logger == nil {
 902		global.LOG.Infof("download app [%s] from %s", app.Name, appDetail.DownloadUrl)
 903	} else {
 904		logger.Printf("download app [%s] from %s", app.Name, appDetail.DownloadUrl)
 905	}
 906
 907	filePath := path.Join(appVersionDir, app.Key+"-"+appDetail.Version+".tar.gz")
 908
 909	defer func() {
 910		if err != nil {
 911			if appInstall != nil {
 912				appInstall.Status = constant.StatusDownloadErr
 913				appInstall.Message = err.Error()
 914			}
 915		}
 916	}()
 917
 918	if err = files.DownloadFileWithProxy(appDetail.DownloadUrl, filePath); err != nil {
 919		if logger == nil {
 920			global.LOG.Errorf("download app [%s] error %v", app.Name, err)
 921		} else {
 922			logger.Printf("download app [%s] error %v", app.Name, err)
 923		}
 924		return
 925	}
 926	if err = fileOp.Decompress(context.Background(), filePath, appResourceDir, files.SdkTarGz, ""); err != nil {
 927		if logger == nil {
 928			global.LOG.Errorf("decompress app[%s] error %v", app.Name, err)
 929		} else {
 930			logger.Printf("decompress app[%s] error %v", app.Name, err)
 931		}
 932		return
 933	}
 934	_ = fileOp.DeleteFile(filePath)
 935	appDetail.Update = false
 936	_ = appDetailRepo.Update(context.Background(), appDetail)
 937	return
 938}
 939
 940func copyData(task *task.Task, app model.App, appDetail model.AppDetail, appInstall *model.AppInstall, req request.AppInstallCreate) (err error) {
 941	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 942	appResourceDir := path.Join(global.Dir.AppResourceDir, app.Resource)
 943
 944	if app.Resource == constant.AppResourceRemote {
 945		err = downloadApp(app, appDetail, appInstall, task.Logger)
 946		if err != nil {
 947			return
 948		}
 949		go func() {
 950			RequestDownloadCallBack(appDetail.DownloadCallBackUrl)
 951		}()
 952	}
 953	appKey := app.Key
 954	installAppDir := path.Join(global.Dir.AppInstallDir, app.Key)
 955	if app.Resource == constant.AppResourceLocal {
 956		appResourceDir = global.Dir.LocalAppResourceDir
 957		appKey = strings.TrimPrefix(app.Key, "local")
 958		installAppDir = path.Join(global.Dir.LocalAppInstallDir, appKey)
 959	}
 960	if app.Resource == constant.AppResourceCustom {
 961		appResourceDir = path.Join(global.Dir.AppResourceDir, "custom")
 962	}
 963	resourceDir := path.Join(appResourceDir, appKey, appDetail.Version)
 964
 965	if !fileOp.Stat(installAppDir) {
 966		if err = fileOp.CreateDir(installAppDir, constant.DirPerm); err != nil {
 967			return
 968		}
 969	}
 970	appDir := path.Join(installAppDir, req.Name)
 971	if fileOp.Stat(appDir) {
 972		if err = fileOp.DeleteDir(appDir); err != nil {
 973			return
 974		}
 975	}
 976	if err = fileOp.Copy(resourceDir, installAppDir); err != nil {
 977		return
 978	}
 979	versionDir := path.Join(installAppDir, appDetail.Version)
 980	if err = fileOp.Rename(versionDir, appDir); err != nil {
 981		return
 982	}
 983	envPath := path.Join(appDir, ".env")
 984	envParams := make(map[string]string)
 985	if fileOp.Stat(envPath) {
 986		envs, _ := gotenv.Read(envPath)
 987		if envParams = maps.Clone(envs); envParams == nil {
 988			envParams = make(map[string]string)
 989		}
 990	}
 991	handleMap(req.Params, envParams)
 992	if err = env.Write(envParams, envPath); err != nil {
 993		return
 994	}
 995	if err := fileOp.WriteFile(appInstall.GetComposePath(), strings.NewReader(appInstall.DockerCompose), constant.DirPerm); err != nil {
 996		return err
 997	}
 998	return
 999}
1000
1001func runScript(task *task.Task, appInstall *model.AppInstall, operate string) error {
1002	workDir := appInstall.GetPath()
1003	scriptPath := ""
1004	switch operate {
1005	case "init":
1006		scriptPath = path.Join(workDir, "scripts", "init.sh")
1007	case "upgrade":
1008		scriptPath = path.Join(workDir, "scripts", "upgrade.sh")
1009	case "uninstall":
1010		scriptPath = path.Join(workDir, "scripts", "uninstall.sh")
1011	}
1012	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
1013	if !fileOp.Stat(scriptPath) {
1014		return nil
1015	}
1016	_ = fileOp.ChmodR(scriptPath, constant.DirPerm, false)
1017	logStr := i18n.GetWithName("ExecShell", operate)
1018	task.LogStart(logStr)
1019
1020	cmdMgr := cmd.NewCommandMgr(cmd.WithTimeout(10*time.Minute), cmd.WithWorkDir(workDir))
1021	if err := cmdMgr.Run("bash", scriptPath); err != nil {
1022		task.LogFailedWithErr(logStr, err)
1023		return err
1024	}
1025	task.LogSuccess(logStr)
1026	return nil
1027}
1028
1029func checkContainerNameIsExist(containerName, appDir string) (bool, error) {
1030	client, err := docker.NewDockerClient()
1031	if err != nil {
1032		return false, err
1033	}
1034	defer client.Close()
1035	var options container.ListOptions
1036	list, err := client.ContainerList(context.Background(), options)
1037	if err != nil {
1038		return false, err
1039	}
1040	for _, container := range list {
1041		if containerName == container.Names[0][1:] {
1042			if workDir, ok := container.Labels[composeWorkdirLabel]; ok {
1043				if workDir != appDir {
1044					return true, nil
1045				}
1046			} else {
1047				return true, nil
1048			}
1049		}
1050
1051	}
1052	return false, nil
1053}
1054
1055func upApp(task *task.Task, appInstall *model.AppInstall, pullImages bool) error {
1056	upProject := func(appInstall *model.AppInstall) (err error) {
1057		var (
1058			out    string
1059			errMsg string
1060		)
1061		if pullImages && appInstall.App.Type != "php" {
1062			envByte, err := files.NewFileOp().GetContent(appInstall.GetEnvPath())
1063			if err != nil {
1064				return err
1065			}
1066			images, err := docker.GetImagesFromDockerCompose(envByte, []byte(appInstall.DockerCompose))
1067			if err != nil {
1068				return err
1069			}
1070			imagePrefix := xpack.MultiNodeProvider.GetImagePrefix()
1071			dockerCLi, err := docker.NewClient()
1072			if err != nil {
1073				return err
1074			}
1075			defer dockerCLi.Close()
1076			for _, image := range images {
1077				if imagePrefix != "" {
1078					lastSlashIndex := strings.LastIndex(image, "/")
1079					if lastSlashIndex != -1 {
1080						image = image[lastSlashIndex+1:]
1081					}
1082					image = imagePrefix + "/" + image
1083				}
1084
1085				task.Log(i18n.GetWithName("PullImageStart", image))
1086				if err = dockerCLi.PullImageWithProcess(task, image); err != nil {
1087					errOur := err.Error()
1088					if errOur != "" {
1089						if strings.Contains(errOur, "no such host") {
1090							errMsg = i18n.GetMsgByKey("ErrNoSuchHost") + ":"
1091						}
1092						if strings.Contains(errOur, "Error response from daemon") {
1093							errMsg = i18n.GetMsgByKey("PullImageTimeout") + ":"
1094						}
1095					}
1096					appInstall.Message = errMsg + errOur
1097					installErr := errors.New(appInstall.Message)
1098					task.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("PullImage"), installErr)
1099					if exist, _ := dockerCLi.ImageExists(image); !exist {
1100						return installErr
1101					} else {
1102						task.Log(i18n.GetMsgByKey("UseExistImage"))
1103					}
1104				} else {
1105					task.Log(i18n.GetMsgByKey("PullImageSuccess"))
1106				}
1107			}
1108		}
1109		logStr := fmt.Sprintf("%s %s", i18n.GetMsgByKey("Run"), i18n.GetMsgByKey("App"))
1110		task.Log(logStr)
1111		out, err = compose.Up(appInstall.GetComposePath())
1112		if err != nil {
1113			if out != "" {
1114				appInstall.Message = errMsg + out
1115				err = errors.New(out)
1116			}
1117			task.LogFailedWithErr(logStr, err)
1118			return err
1119		}
1120		task.LogSuccess(logStr)
1121		return
1122	}
1123	exist, _ := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(appInstall.ID))
1124	if exist.ID > 0 {
1125		containerNames, err := getContainerNames(*appInstall)
1126		if err == nil {
1127			if len(containerNames) > 0 {
1128				appInstall.ContainerName = strings.Join(containerNames, ",")
1129			}
1130			_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), appInstall)
1131		}
1132	}
1133	if err := upProject(appInstall); err != nil {
1134		if appInstall.Message == "" {
1135			appInstall.Message = err.Error()
1136		}
1137		appInstall.Status = constant.StatusUpErr
1138		_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), appInstall)
1139		return err
1140	} else {
1141		appInstall.Status = constant.StatusRunning
1142		_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), appInstall)
1143		return nil
1144	}
1145}
1146
1147func rebuildApp(appInstall model.AppInstall) error {
1148	appInstall.Status = constant.StatusRebuilding
1149	_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &appInstall)
1150	go func() {
1151		dockerComposePath := appInstall.GetComposePath()
1152		out, err := compose.Down(dockerComposePath)
1153		if err != nil {
1154			_ = handleErr(appInstall, err, out)
1155			return
1156		}
1157		out, err = compose.Up(appInstall.GetComposePath())
1158		if err != nil {
1159			_ = handleErr(appInstall, err, out)
1160			return
1161		}
1162		containerNames, err := getContainerNames(appInstall)
1163		if err != nil {
1164			_ = handleErr(appInstall, err, out)
1165			return
1166		}
1167		appInstall.ContainerName = strings.Join(containerNames, ",")
1168
1169		appInstall.Status = constant.StatusRunning
1170		_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &appInstall)
1171	}()
1172	return nil
1173}
1174
1175func getAppDetails(details []model.AppDetail, versions []dto.AppConfigVersion) map[string]model.AppDetail {
1176	appDetails := make(map[string]model.AppDetail, len(details))
1177	for _, old := range details {
1178		old.Status = constant.AppTakeDown
1179		appDetails[old.Version] = old
1180	}
1181	for _, v := range versions {
1182		version := v.Name
1183		detail, ok := appDetails[version]
1184		if ok {
1185			detail.Status = constant.AppNormal
1186			appDetails[version] = detail
1187		} else {
1188			appDetails[version] = model.AppDetail{
1189				Version: version,
1190				Status:  constant.AppNormal,
1191			}
1192		}
1193	}
1194	return appDetails
1195}
1196
1197func getApps(oldApps []model.App, items []dto.AppDefine, systemVersion string, task *task.Task) (map[string]model.App, map[string]string) {
1198	apps := make(map[string]model.App, len(oldApps))
1199	pendingIcons := make(map[string]string, len(items))
1200	for _, old := range oldApps {
1201		old.Status = constant.AppTakeDown
1202		apps[old.Key] = old
1203	}
1204	for _, item := range items {
1205		config := item.AppProperty
1206		if config.Version > 0 && common.CompareVersion(strconv.FormatFloat(config.Version, 'f', -1, 64), systemVersion) {
1207			task.Log(i18n.GetWithName("AppVersionNotMatch", item.Name))
1208			continue
1209		}
1210		if config.Deprecated > 0 && common.CompareAppVersion(systemVersion, strconv.FormatFloat(config.Deprecated, 'f', -1, 64)) {
1211			continue
1212		}
1213
1214		key := config.Key
1215		app, ok := apps[key]
1216		if !ok {
1217			app = model.App{}
1218		}
1219		app.RequiredPanelVersion = config.Version
1220		app.Resource = constant.AppResourceRemote
1221		app.Name = item.Name
1222		app.Limit = config.Limit
1223		app.Key = key
1224		app.ShortDescZh = config.ShortDescZh
1225		app.ShortDescEn = config.ShortDescEn
1226		description, _ := json.Marshal(config.Description)
1227		app.Description = string(description)
1228		app.Website = config.Website
1229		app.Document = config.Document
1230		app.Github = config.Github
1231		app.Type = config.Type
1232		app.CrossVersionUpdate = config.CrossVersionUpdate
1233		app.Status = constant.AppNormal
1234		app.LastModified = item.LastModified
1235		app.ReadMe = item.ReadMe
1236		app.MemoryRequired = config.MemoryRequired
1237		app.Architectures = strings.Join(config.Architectures, ",")
1238		app.GpuSupport = config.GpuSupport
1239		app.BatchInstallSupport = config.BatchInstallSupport
1240		if item.Icon != "" {
1241			pendingIcons[key] = item.Icon
1242		}
1243		apps[key] = app
1244	}
1245	return apps, pendingIcons
1246}
1247
1248func handleLocalAppDetail(versionDir string, appDetail *model.AppDetail) error {
1249	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
1250	dockerComposePath := path.Join(versionDir, "docker-compose.yml")
1251	if !fileOp.Stat(dockerComposePath) {
1252		return buserr.WithName("ErrFileNotFound", "docker-compose.yml")
1253	}
1254	dockerComposeByte, _ := fileOp.GetContent(dockerComposePath)
1255	if dockerComposeByte == nil {
1256		return buserr.WithName("ErrFileParseApp", "docker-compose.yml")
1257	}
1258	appDetail.DockerCompose = string(dockerComposeByte)
1259	paramPath := path.Join(versionDir, "data.yml")
1260	if !fileOp.Stat(paramPath) {
1261		return buserr.WithName("ErrFileNotFound", "data.yml")
1262	}
1263	paramByte, _ := fileOp.GetContent(paramPath)
1264	if paramByte == nil {
1265		return buserr.WithName("ErrFileNotFound", "data.yml")
1266	}
1267	appParamConfig := dto.LocalAppParam{}
1268	if err := yaml.Unmarshal(paramByte, &appParamConfig); err != nil {
1269		return buserr.WithMap("ErrFileParseApp", map[string]interface{}{"name": "data.yml", "err": err.Error()}, err)
1270	}
1271	dataJson, err := json.Marshal(appParamConfig.AppParams)
1272	if err != nil {
1273		return buserr.WithMap("ErrFileParseApp", map[string]interface{}{"name": "data.yml", "err": err.Error()}, err)
1274	}
1275	var appParam dto.AppForm
1276	if err = json.Unmarshal(dataJson, &appParam); err != nil {
1277		return buserr.WithMap("ErrFileParseApp", map[string]interface{}{"name": "data.yml", "err": err.Error()}, err)
1278	}
1279	for _, formField := range appParam.FormFields {
1280		if strings.Contains(formField.EnvKey, " ") {
1281			return buserr.WithName("ErrAppParamKey", formField.EnvKey)
1282		}
1283	}
1284
1285	var dataMap map[string]interface{}
1286	err = yaml.Unmarshal(paramByte, &dataMap)
1287	if err != nil {
1288		return buserr.WithMap("ErrFileParseApp", map[string]interface{}{"name": "data.yml", "err": err.Error()}, err)
1289	}
1290
1291	additionalProperties, _ := dataMap["additionalProperties"].(map[string]interface{})
1292	formFieldsInterface, ok := additionalProperties["formFields"]
1293	if ok {
1294		formFields, ok := formFieldsInterface.([]interface{})
1295		if !ok {
1296			return buserr.WithName("ErrAppParamKey", "formFields")
1297		}
1298		for _, item := range formFields {
1299			field := item.(map[string]interface{})
1300			for key, value := range field {
1301				if value == nil {
1302					return buserr.WithName("ErrAppParamKey", key)
1303				}
1304			}
1305		}
1306	}
1307
1308	appDetail.Params = string(dataJson)
1309	return nil
1310}
1311
1312func handleLocalApp(appDir string) (app *model.App, err error) {
1313	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
1314	configYamlPath := path.Join(appDir, "data.yml")
1315	if !fileOp.Stat(configYamlPath) {
1316		err = buserr.WithName("ErrFileNotFound", "data.yml")
1317		return
1318	}
1319	iconPath := path.Join(appDir, "logo.png")
1320	if !fileOp.Stat(iconPath) {
1321		err = buserr.WithName("ErrFileNotFound", "logo.png")
1322		return
1323	}
1324	configYamlByte, err := fileOp.GetContent(configYamlPath)
1325	if err != nil {
1326		err = buserr.WithMap("ErrFileParseApp", map[string]interface{}{"name": "data.yml", "err": err.Error()}, err)
1327		return
1328	}
1329	localAppDefine := dto.LocalAppAppDefine{}
1330	if err = yaml.Unmarshal(configYamlByte, &localAppDefine); err != nil {
1331		err = buserr.WithMap("ErrFileParseApp", map[string]interface{}{"name": "data.yml", "err": err.Error()}, err)
1332		return
1333	}
1334	appDefine := localAppDefine.AppProperty
1335	app = &model.App{}
1336	app.Name = appDefine.Name
1337	app.TagsKey = append(appDefine.Tags, "Local")
1338	app.Type = appDefine.Type
1339	app.CrossVersionUpdate = appDefine.CrossVersionUpdate
1340	app.Limit = appDefine.Limit
1341	app.Recommend = appDefine.Recommend
1342	app.Website = appDefine.Website
1343	app.Github = appDefine.Github
1344	app.Document = appDefine.Document
1345	if appDefine.ShortDescZh != "" {
1346		appDefine.Description.Zh = appDefine.ShortDescZh
1347	}
1348	if appDefine.ShortDescEn != "" {
1349		appDefine.Description.En = appDefine.ShortDescEn
1350	}
1351	desc, _ := json.Marshal(appDefine.Description)
1352	app.Description = string(desc)
1353	app.Key = "local" + appDefine.Key
1354	app.Architectures = strings.Join(appDefine.Architectures, ",")
1355	app.GpuSupport = appDefine.GpuSupport
1356	app.MemoryRequired = appDefine.MemoryRequired
1357
1358	app.Resource = constant.AppResourceLocal
1359	app.Status = constant.AppNormal
1360	app.Recommend = 9999
1361	readMeByte, err := fileOp.GetContent(path.Join(appDir, "README.md"))
1362	if err == nil {
1363		app.ReadMe = string(readMeByte)
1364	}
1365	iconByte, _ := fileOp.GetContent(iconPath)
1366	if iconByte != nil {
1367		iconStr := base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(iconByte)
1368		app.Icon = iconStr
1369	}
1370	return
1371}
1372
1373func handleErr(install model.AppInstall, err error, out string) error {
1374	reErr := err
1375	install.Message = err.Error()
1376	if out != "" {
1377		install.Message = out
1378		reErr = errors.New(out)
1379	}
1380	install.Status = constant.StatusUpErr
1381	_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &install)
1382	return reErr
1383}
1384
1385func doNotNeedSync(installed model.AppInstall) bool {
1386	return installed.Status == constant.StatusInstalling || installed.Status == constant.StatusRebuilding || installed.Status == constant.StatusUpgrading ||
1387		installed.Status == constant.StatusSyncing || installed.Status == constant.StatusUninstalling || installed.Status == constant.StatusInstallErr
1388}
1389
1390func synAppInstall(containers map[string]container.Summary, appInstall *model.AppInstall, force bool) {
1391	oldStatus := appInstall.Status
1392	containerNames := strings.Split(appInstall.ContainerName, ",")
1393	if len(containers) == 0 {
1394		if appInstall.Status == constant.StatusUpErr && !force {
1395			return
1396		}
1397		appInstall.Status = constant.StatusError
1398		appInstall.Message = buserr.WithName("ErrContainerNotFound", strings.Join(containerNames, ",")).Error()
1399		_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), appInstall)
1400		return
1401	}
1402	notFoundNames := make([]string, 0)
1403	exitNames := make([]string, 0)
1404	exitedCount := 0
1405	pausedCount := 0
1406	runningCount := 0
1407	restartingCount := 0
1408	total := len(containerNames)
1409	for _, name := range containerNames {
1410		if con, ok := containers["/"+name]; ok {
1411			switch con.State {
1412			case "exited":
1413				exitedCount++
1414				exitNames = append(exitNames, name)
1415			case "running":
1416				runningCount++
1417			case "restarting":
1418				restartingCount++
1419			case "paused":
1420				pausedCount++
1421			}
1422		} else {
1423			notFoundNames = append(notFoundNames, name)
1424		}
1425	}
1426	switch {
1427	case exitedCount == total:
1428		appInstall.Status = constant.StatusStopped
1429	case runningCount == total:
1430		appInstall.Status = constant.StatusRunning
1431		if oldStatus == constant.StatusRunning {
1432			return
1433		}
1434	case restartingCount == total:
1435		appInstall.Status = constant.StatusRestarting
1436	case pausedCount == total:
1437		appInstall.Status = constant.StatusPaused
1438	case len(notFoundNames) == total:
1439		if appInstall.Status == constant.StatusUpErr && !force {
1440			return
1441		}
1442		appInstall.Status = constant.StatusError
1443		appInstall.Message = buserr.WithName("ErrContainerNotFound", strings.Join(notFoundNames, ",")).Error()
1444	default:
1445		var msg string
1446		if exitedCount > 0 {
1447			msg = buserr.WithName("ErrContainerMsg", strings.Join(exitNames, ",")).Error()
1448		}
1449		if len(notFoundNames) > 0 {
1450			msg += buserr.WithName("ErrContainerNotFound", strings.Join(notFoundNames, ",")).Error()
1451		}
1452		if msg == "" {
1453			msg = buserr.New("ErrAppWarn").Error()
1454		}
1455		appInstall.Message = msg
1456		appInstall.Status = constant.StatusUnHealthy
1457	}
1458	_ = appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), appInstall)
1459}
1460
1461func handleInstalled(appInstallList []model.AppInstall, updated, sync, checkUpdate bool) ([]response.AppInstallDTO, error) {
1462	var (
1463		res           []response.AppInstallDTO
1464		containersMap map[string]container.Summary
1465	)
1466	if sync {
1467		cli, err := docker.NewClient()
1468		if err == nil {
1469			defer cli.Close()
1470			containers, err := cli.ListAllContainers()
1471			if err == nil {
1472				containersMap = make(map[string]container.Summary, len(containers))
1473				for _, contain := range containers {
1474					containersMap[contain.Names[0]] = contain
1475				}
1476			}
1477		}
1478	}
1479
1480	for _, installed := range appInstallList {
1481		if updated && ignoreUpdate(installed) {
1482			continue
1483		}
1484
1485		if sync && !doNotNeedSync(installed) {
1486			synAppInstall(containersMap, &installed, false)
1487		}
1488
1489		resourceKeys := getAppInstallResourceKeys(installed.ID)
1490		envMap := make(map[string]interface{})
1491		_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(installed.Env), &envMap)
1492		installDTO := response.AppInstallDTO{
1493			ID:          installed.ID,
1494			Name:        installed.Name,
1495			AppID:       installed.AppId,
1496			AppDetailID: installed.AppDetailId,
1497			Version:     installed.Version,
1498			Status:      installed.Status,
1499			Message:     installed.Message,
1500			HttpPort:    installed.HttpPort,
1501			HttpsPort:   installed.HttpsPort,
1502			AppName:     installed.App.Name,
1503			AppKey:      installed.App.Key,
1504			AppType:     installed.App.Type,
1505			Path:        installed.GetPath(),
1506			CreatedAt:   installed.CreatedAt,
1507			WebUI:       installed.WebUI,
1508			App: response.AppDetail{
1509				Github:   installed.App.Github,
1510				Website:  installed.App.Website,
1511				Document: installed.App.Document,
1512			},
1513			Favorite:     installed.Favorite,
1514			SortOrder:    installed.SortOrder,
1515			Container:    installed.ContainerName,
1516			ServiceName:  strings.ToLower(installed.ServiceName),
1517			ResourceKeys: resourceKeys,
1518			Env:          envMap,
1519		}
1520
1521		if !updated && !checkUpdate {
1522			installDTO.LinkDB = hasLinkDBFromKeys(resourceKeys)
1523			res = append(res, installDTO)
1524			continue
1525		}
1526
1527		if installed.Version == "latest" {
1528			if checkUpdate {
1529				installDTO.CanUpdate = false
1530				installDTO.LinkDB = hasLinkDBFromKeys(resourceKeys)
1531				res = append(res, installDTO)
1532			}
1533			continue
1534		}
1535
1536		installDTO.DockerCompose = installed.DockerCompose
1537		installDTO.IsEdit = isEditCompose(installed)
1538
1539		details, err := appDetailRepo.GetBy(appDetailRepo.WithAppId(installed.App.ID))
1540		if err != nil {
1541			return nil, err
1542		}
1543
1544		var versions []string
1545		for _, appDetail := range details {
1546			ignores, _ := appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.List(runtimeRepo.WithDetailId(appDetail.ID), appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.WithScope("version"))
1547			if len(ignores) > 0 {
1548				continue
1549			}
1550			if common.IsCrossVersion(installed.Version, appDetail.Version) && !installed.App.CrossVersionUpdate {
1551				continue
1552			}
1553			versions = append(versions, appDetail.Version)
1554		}
1555
1556		if len(versions) == 0 {
1557			if checkUpdate {
1558				installDTO.CanUpdate = false
1559				installDTO.LinkDB = hasLinkDBFromKeys(resourceKeys)
1560				res = append(res, installDTO)
1561			}
1562			continue
1563		}
1564
1565		versions = common.GetSortedVersions(versions)
1566		lastVersion := versions[0]
1567
1568		if installed.App.Key == constant.AppMysql || installed.App.Key == constant.AppMysqlCluster {
1569			for _, version := range versions {
1570				majorVersion := getMajorVersion(installed.Version)
1571				if !strings.HasPrefix(version, majorVersion) {
1572					continue
1573				} else {
1574					lastVersion = version
1575					break
1576				}
1577			}
1578		}
1579
1580		if common.IsCrossVersion(installed.Version, lastVersion) {
1581			installDTO.CanUpdate = installed.App.CrossVersionUpdate
1582		} else {
1583			installDTO.CanUpdate = common.CompareVersion(lastVersion, installed.Version)
1584		}
1585
1586		if updated {
1587			if installDTO.CanUpdate {
1588				res = append(res, installDTO)
1589			}
1590		} else if checkUpdate {
1591			installDTO.LinkDB = hasLinkDBFromKeys(resourceKeys)
1592			res = append(res, installDTO)
1593		}
1594	}
1595
1596	return res, nil
1597}
1598
1599func getAppInstallByKey(key string) (model.AppInstall, error) {
1600	app, err := appRepo.GetFirst(appRepo.WithKey(key))
1601	if err != nil {
1602		return model.AppInstall{}, err
1603	}
1604	appInstall, err := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(appInstallRepo.WithAppId(app.ID))
1605	if err != nil {
1606		return model.AppInstall{}, err
1607	}
1608	return appInstall, nil
1609}
1610
1611func getAppInstallPort(key string) (httpPort, httpsPort int, err error) {
1612	install, err := getAppInstallByKey(key)
1613	if err != nil {
1614		return
1615	}
1616	httpPort = install.HttpPort
1617	httpsPort = install.HttpsPort
1618	return
1619}
1620
1621func updateToolApp(installed *model.AppInstall) {
1622	tooKey, ok := dto.AppToolMap[installed.App.Key]
1623	if !ok {
1624		return
1625	}
1626	toolInstall, _ := getAppInstallByKey(tooKey)
1627	if reflect.DeepEqual(toolInstall, model.AppInstall{}) {
1628		return
1629	}
1630	paramMap := make(map[string]string)
1631	_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(installed.Param), &paramMap)
1632	envMap := make(map[string]interface{})
1633	_ = json.Unmarshal([]byte(toolInstall.Env), &envMap)
1634	if password, ok := paramMap["PANEL_DB_ROOT_PASSWORD"]; ok {
1635		envMap["PANEL_DB_ROOT_PASSWORD"] = password
1636	}
1637	if _, ok := envMap["PANEL_REDIS_HOST"]; ok {
1638		envMap["PANEL_REDIS_HOST"] = installed.ServiceName
1639	}
1640	if _, ok := envMap["PANEL_DB_HOST"]; ok {
1641		envMap["PANEL_DB_HOST"] = installed.ServiceName
1642	}
1643
1644	envPath := path.Join(toolInstall.GetPath(), ".env")
1645	contentByte, err := json.Marshal(envMap)
1646	if err != nil {
1647		global.LOG.Errorf("update tool app [%s] error : %s", toolInstall.Name, err.Error())
1648		return
1649	}
1650	envFileMap := make(map[string]string)
1651	handleMap(envMap, envFileMap)
1652	if err = env.Write(envFileMap, envPath); err != nil {
1653		global.LOG.Errorf("update tool app [%s] error : %s", toolInstall.Name, err.Error())
1654		return
1655	}
1656	toolInstall.Env = string(contentByte)
1657	if err := appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &toolInstall); err != nil {
1658		global.LOG.Errorf("update tool app [%s] error : %s", toolInstall.Name, err.Error())
1659		return
1660	}
1661	if out, err := compose.Down(toolInstall.GetComposePath()); err != nil {
1662		global.LOG.Errorf("update tool app [%s] error : %s", toolInstall.Name, out)
1663		return
1664	}
1665	if out, err := compose.Up(toolInstall.GetComposePath()); err != nil {
1666		global.LOG.Errorf("update tool app [%s] error : %s", toolInstall.Name, out)
1667		return
1668	}
1669}
1670
1671func addDockerComposeCommonParam(composeMap map[string]interface{}, serviceName string, req request.AppContainerConfig, params map[string]interface{}) error {
1672	services, serviceValid := composeMap["services"].(map[string]interface{})
1673	if !serviceValid {
1674		return buserr.New("ErrFileParse")
1675	}
1676	imagePreFix := xpack.MultiNodeProvider.GetImagePrefix()
1677	if imagePreFix != "" {
1678		for _, service := range services {
1679			serviceValue := service.(map[string]interface{})
1680			if image, ok := serviceValue["image"]; ok {
1681				imageStr := image.(string)
1682				lastSlashIndex := strings.LastIndex(imageStr, "/")
1683				if lastSlashIndex != -1 {
1684					imageStr = imageStr[lastSlashIndex+1:]
1685				}
1686				imageStr = imagePreFix + "/" + imageStr
1687				serviceValue["image"] = imageStr
1688			}
1689		}
1690	}
1691
1692	service, serviceExist := services[serviceName]
1693	if !serviceExist {
1694		return buserr.New("ErrFileParse")
1695	}
1696	serviceValue := service.(map[string]interface{})
1697
1698	deploy := map[string]interface{}{}
1699	if de, ok := serviceValue["deploy"]; ok {
1700		deploy = de.(map[string]interface{})
1701	}
1702	resource := map[string]interface{}{}
1703	if res, ok := deploy["resources"]; ok {
1704		resource = res.(map[string]interface{})
1705	}
1706	resource["limits"] = map[string]interface{}{
1707		"cpus":   "${CPUS}",
1708		"memory": "${MEMORY_LIMIT}",
1709	}
1710	if req.GpuConfig {
1711		resource["reservations"] = map[string]interface{}{
1712			"devices": []map[string]interface{}{
1713				{
1714					"driver":       "nvidia",
1715					"count":        "all",
1716					"capabilities": []string{"gpu"},
1717				},
1718			},
1719		}
1720	}
1721	deploy["resources"] = resource
1722	serviceValue["deploy"] = deploy
1723
1724	if req.RestartPolicy != "" {
1725		if req.RestartPolicy == "on-failure" {
1726			serviceValue["restart"] = "on-failure:5"
1727		} else {
1728			serviceValue["restart"] = req.RestartPolicy
1729		}
1730	}
1731
1732	ports, ok := serviceValue["ports"].([]interface{})
1733	if ok {
1734		for i, port := range ports {
1735			portStr, portOK := port.(string)
1736			if !portOK {
1737				continue
1738			}
1739			portArray := strings.Split(portStr, ":")
1740			if len(portArray) == 2 {
1741				portArray = append([]string{"${HOST_IP}"}, portArray...)
1742			}
1743			ports[i] = strings.Join(portArray, ":")
1744		}
1745		serviceValue["ports"] = ports
1746	}
1747
1748	params[constant.CPUS] = "0"
1749	params[constant.MemoryLimit] = "0"
1750	if req.Advanced {
1751		if req.CpuQuota > 0 {
1752			params[constant.CPUS] = req.CpuQuota
1753		}
1754		if req.MemoryLimit > 0 {
1755			params[constant.MemoryLimit] = strconv.FormatFloat(req.MemoryLimit, 'f', -1, 32) + req.MemoryUnit
1756		}
1757	}
1758	_, portExist := serviceValue["ports"].([]interface{})
1759	if portExist {
1760		allowHost := "127.0.0.1"
1761		if req.Advanced && req.AllowPort {
1762			allowHost = req.SpecifyIP
1763		}
1764		params[constant.HostIP] = allowHost
1765	}
1766	services[serviceName] = serviceValue
1767	return nil
1768}
1769
1770func getAppCommonConfig(envs map[string]interface{}) request.AppContainerConfig {
1771	config := request.AppContainerConfig{}
1772
1773	if hostIp, ok := envs[constant.HostIP]; ok {
1774		config.AllowPort = hostIp.(string) != "127.0.0.1"
1775		config.SpecifyIP = hostIp.(string)
1776	} else {
1777		config.AllowPort = true
1778	}
1779	if cpuCore, ok := envs[constant.CPUS]; ok {
1780		numStr, ok := cpuCore.(string)
1781		if ok {
1782			num, err := strconv.ParseFloat(numStr, 64)
1783			if err == nil {
1784				config.CpuQuota = num
1785			}
1786		} else {
1787			num64, flOk := cpuCore.(float64)
1788			if flOk {
1789				config.CpuQuota = num64
1790			}
1791		}
1792	} else {
1793		config.CpuQuota = 0
1794	}
1795	if memLimit, ok := envs[constant.MemoryLimit]; ok {
1796		matches := re.GetRegex(re.NumberAlphaPattern).FindStringSubmatch(memLimit.(string))
1797		if len(matches) == 3 {
1798			num, err := strconv.ParseFloat(matches[1], 64)
1799			if err == nil {
1800				unit := matches[2]
1801				config.MemoryLimit = num
1802				config.MemoryUnit = unit
1803			}
1804		}
1805	} else {
1806		config.MemoryLimit = 0
1807		config.MemoryUnit = "M"
1808	}
1809
1810	if containerName, ok := envs[constant.ContainerName]; ok {
1811		config.ContainerName = containerName.(string)
1812	}
1813
1814	return config
1815}

1817func isHostModel(dockerCompose string) bool {
1818	composeMap := make(map[string]interface{})
1819	_ = yaml.Unmarshal([]byte(dockerCompose), &composeMap)
1820	services, serviceValid := composeMap["services"].(map[string]interface{})
1821	if !serviceValid {
1822		return false
1823	}
1824	for _, service := range services {
1825		serviceValue := service.(map[string]interface{})
1826		if value, ok := serviceValue["network_mode"]; ok && value == "host" {
1827			return true
1828		}
1829	}
1830	return false
1831}

1833func copyAppDetailMissing(fileOp files.FileOp, srcDir, dstDir string) error {
1834	return copyAppDetailMissingTracked(fileOp, srcDir, dstDir, nil)
1835}
1836
1837func copyAppDetailMissingTracked(fileOp files.FileOp, srcDir, dstDir string, createdPaths *[]string) error {
1838	entries, err := os.ReadDir(srcDir)
1839	if err != nil {
1840		return err
1841	}
1842	for _, entry := range entries {
1843		if strings.HasPrefix(entry.Name(), ".") {
1844			continue
1845		}
1846		srcPath := path.Join(srcDir, entry.Name())
1847		dstPath := path.Join(dstDir, entry.Name())
1848		if !fileOp.Stat(dstPath) {
1849			if createdPaths != nil {
1850				*createdPaths = append(*createdPaths, dstPath)
1851			}
1852			if entry.IsDir() {
1853				if err := fileOp.CopyDir(srcPath, dstDir); err != nil {
1854					return err
1855				}
1856				continue
1857			}
1858			if err := fileOp.CopyFile(srcPath, dstDir); err != nil {
1859				return err
1860			}
1861			continue
1862		}
1863		if !entry.IsDir() {
1864			continue
1865		}
1866		if err := copyAppDetailMissingTracked(fileOp, srcPath, dstPath, createdPaths); err != nil {
1867			return err
1868		}
1869	}
1870	return nil
1871}

1873func getRestartPolicy(yml string) string {
1874	var project docker.ComposeProject
1875	if err := yaml.Unmarshal([]byte(yml), &project); err != nil {
1876		return ""
1877	}
1878	for _, service := range project.Services {
1879		return service.Restart
1880	}
1881	return ""
1882}

1884func getMajorVersion(version string) string {
1885	parts := strings.Split(version, ".")
1886	if len(parts) >= 2 {
1887		return parts[0] + "." + parts[1]
1888	}
1889	return version
1890}

1892func ignoreUpdate(installed model.AppInstall) bool {
1893	if installed.App.Type == "php" || installed.Status == constant.StatusInstalling {
1894		return true
1895	}
1896	ignores, _ := appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.List(appDetailRepo.WithAppId(installed.AppId), appIgnoreUpgradeRepo.WithScope("all"))
1897	if len(ignores) > 0 {
1898		return true
1899	}
1900	if installed.App.Key == constant.AppMysql {
1901		majorVersion := getMajorVersion(installed.Version)
1902		appDetails, _ := appDetailRepo.GetBy(appDetailRepo.WithAppId(installed.App.ID))
1903		for _, appDetail := range appDetails {
1904			if strings.HasPrefix(appDetail.Version, majorVersion) && common.CompareVersion(appDetail.Version, installed.Version) {
1905				return false
1906			}
1907		}
1908		return true
1909	}
1910	if installed.App.Key == "sqlbot" {
1911		if common.CompareVersion("1.1.0", installed.Version) {
1912			return true
1913		}
1914	}
1915	return false
1916}

1925func getAppTags(appID uint, lang string) ([]response.TagDTO, error) {
1926	appTags, err := appTagRepo.GetByAppId(appID)
1927	if err != nil {
1928		return nil, err
1929	}

1931	for _, at := range appTags {

1933	}

1935	if err != nil {
1936		return nil, err
1937	}

1944				ID:   t.ID,
1945				Key:  t.Key,
1946				Name: name,
1947			}
1948			res = append(res, tagDTO)
1949		}
1950	}

1954func handleSiteDir(app model.App, appDetail model.AppDetail, req request.AppInstallCreate, t *task.Task) error {
1955	if app.Key == "openresty" && (app.Resource == "remote" || app.Resource == "custom") && common.CompareVersion(appDetail.Version, "1.27") {
1956		if dir, ok := req.Params["WEBSITE_DIR"]; ok {
1957			siteDir := dir.(string)
1958			if siteDir == "" || !strings.HasPrefix(siteDir, "/") {
1959				siteDir = path.Join(global.Dir.DataDir, dir.(string))
1960			}
1961			req.Params["WEBSITE_DIR"] = siteDir
1962			oldWebStePath, _ := settingRepo.GetValueByKey("WEBSITE_DIR")
1963			fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
1964			movePath := func(src, dst string) error {
1965				return cmd.NewCommandMgr().Run("mv", src, dst)
1966			}
1967			if oldWebStePath != "" && oldWebStePath != siteDir && fileOp.Stat(oldWebStePath) {
1968				t.Log(i18n.GetWithName("MoveSiteDir", siteDir))
1969				if fileOp.Stat(siteDir) {
1970					if fileOp.Stat(path.Join(siteDir, "conf.d")) {
1971						_ = movePath(path.Join(siteDir, "conf.d"), path.Join(siteDir, "conf.d.bak"))
1972					}
1973					if fileOp.Stat(path.Join(siteDir, "sites")) {
1974						_ = movePath(path.Join(siteDir, "sites"), path.Join(siteDir, "sites.bak"))
1975					}
1976					if err := movePath(path.Join(oldWebStePath, "sites"), path.Join(siteDir, "sites")); err != nil {
1977						return err
1978					}
1979					if err := movePath(path.Join(oldWebStePath, "conf.d"), path.Join(siteDir, "conf.d")); err != nil {
1980						return err
1981					}
1982				} else {
1983					err := movePath(oldWebStePath, siteDir)
1984					if err != nil {
1985						return err
1986					}
1987				}
1988				t.Log(i18n.GetMsgByKey("MoveSiteDirSuccess"))
1989			}
1990			if !fileOp.Stat(siteDir) {
1991				_ = fileOp.CreateDir(siteDir, constant.DirPerm)
1992				_ = fileOp.CreateDir(path.Join(siteDir, "conf.d"), constant.DirPerm)
1993			}
1994			err := settingRepo.UpdateOrCreate("WEBSITE_DIR", siteDir)
1995			if err != nil {
1996				return err
1997			}
1998			go RestartPHPRuntime()
1999		}
2000	}
2001	return nil
2002}

2004func handleOpenrestyFile(appInstall *model.AppInstall) error {
2005	websites, _ := websiteRepo.List()
2006	hasDefaultWebsite := false
2007	for _, website := range websites {
2008		if website.DefaultServer {
2009			hasDefaultWebsite = true
2010			break
2011		}
2012	}
2013	if err := handleSSLConfig(appInstall, hasDefaultWebsite, true); err != nil {
2014		return err
2015	}
2016	if len(websites) == 0 {
2017		return nil
2018	}
2019	if hasDefaultWebsite {
2020		if err := handleDefaultServer(appInstall); err != nil {
2021			return err
2022		}
2023	}
2024	return createAllWebsitesWAFConfig(websites)
2025}

2027func handleDefaultServer(appInstall *model.AppInstall) error {
2028	installDir := appInstall.GetPath()
2029	defaultConfigPath := path.Join(installDir, nginxModuleConfDir, "default", "00.default.conf")
2030	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
2031	content, err := fileOp.GetContent(defaultConfigPath)
2032	if err != nil {
2033		return err
2034	}
2035	newContent := strings.ReplaceAll(string(content), "default_server", "")
2036	if err := fileOp.WriteFile(defaultConfigPath, strings.NewReader(newContent), constant.FilePerm); err != nil {
2037		return err
2038	}
2039	return nil
2040}

2042func handleSSLConfig(appInstall *model.AppInstall, hasDefaultWebsite bool, sslRejectHandshake bool) error {
2043	sslDir := path.Join(appInstall.GetPath(), nginxModuleConfDir, "ssl")
2044	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
2045	if !fileOp.Stat(sslDir) {
2046		return errors.New("ssl dir not found")
2047	}
2048	hasDefaultSSL := fileOp.Stat(path.Join(sslDir, "fullchain.pem")) && fileOp.Stat(path.Join(sslDir, "privkey.pem")) && fileOp.Stat(path.Join(sslDir, "root_ssl.conf"))
2049	if !hasDefaultSSL {
2050		ca, _ := websiteCARepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByName("1Panel"))
2051		if ca.ID == 0 {
2052			global.LOG.Errorf("create openresty default ssl failed ca not found")
2053			return nil
2054		}
2055		caService := NewIWebsiteCAService()
2056		caRequest := request.WebsiteCAObtain{
2057			ID:      ca.ID,
2058			Domains: "localhost",
2059			KeyType: "RSA4096",
2060			Time:    99,
2061			Unit:    "year",
2062			Dir:     sslDir,
2063			PushDir: true,
2064		}
2065		websiteSSL, err := caService.ObtainSSL(caRequest)
2066		if err != nil {
2067			return err
2068		}
2069		defer func() {
2070			_ = NewIWebsiteSSLService().Delete([]uint{websiteSSL.ID})
2071		}()
2072	}
2073	defaultConfigPath := path.Join(appInstall.GetPath(), nginxModuleConfDir, "default", "00.default.conf")
2074	content, err := os.ReadFile(defaultConfigPath)
2075	if err != nil {
2076		return err
2077	}
2078	defaultConfig, err := parser.NewStringParser(string(content)).Parse()
2079	if err != nil {
2080		return err
2081	}
2082	defaultConfig.FilePath = defaultConfigPath
2083	defaultServer := defaultConfig.FindServers()[0]
2084	updateDefaultServer(defaultServer, appInstall.HttpPort, appInstall.HttpsPort, !hasDefaultWebsite, true)
2085	defaultServer.UpdateDirective("include", []string{"/usr/local/openresty/nginx/conf/ssl/root_ssl.conf"})
2086	defaultServer.UpdateDirective("http2", []string{"on"})
2087	if sslRejectHandshake {
2088		defaultServer.UpdateDirective("ssl_reject_handshake", []string{"on"})
2089	} else {
2090		defaultServer.RemoveDirective("ssl_reject_handshake", []string{})
2091	}
2092	if err = nginx.WriteConfig(defaultConfig, nginx.IndentedStyle); err != nil {
2093		return err
2094	}
2095	return nil
2096}

2098func SyncTags(remoteProperties dto.ExtraProperties) (err error) {
2099	tx, ctx := getTxAndContext()
2100	defer func() {
2101		if err != nil {
2102			tx.Rollback()
2103		}
2104	}()
2105	localTags, err := tagRepo.All()
2106	if err != nil {
2107		return err
2108	}
2109	localTagsMap := make(map[string]*model.Tag)
2110	for i := range localTags {
2111		localTagsMap[localTags[i].Key] = &localTags[i]
2112	}
2113	remoteTagsMap := make(map[string]*dto.Tag)
2114	for i := range remoteProperties.Tags {
2115		remoteTagsMap[remoteProperties.Tags[i].Key] = &remoteProperties.Tags[i]
2116	}
2117
2118	for key, localTag := range localTagsMap {
2119		if _, exists := remoteTagsMap[key]; !exists {
2120			if err = tagRepo.DeleteByID(ctx, localTag.ID); err != nil {
2121				return err
2122			}
2123		}
2124	}
2125
2126	for _, remoteTag := range remoteProperties.Tags {
2127		translations, _ := json.Marshal(remoteTag.Locales)
2128
2129		if existTag, exists := localTagsMap[remoteTag.Key]; exists {
2130			if needsUpdate(existTag, remoteTag, string(translations)) {
2131				existTag.Name = remoteTag.Name
2132				existTag.Sort = remoteTag.Sort
2133				existTag.Translations = string(translations)
2134
2135				if err = tagRepo.Save(ctx, existTag); err != nil {
2136					return err
2137				}
2138			}
2139		} else {
2140			newTag := &model.Tag{
2141				Key:          remoteTag.Key,
2142				Name:         remoteTag.Name,
2143				Sort:         remoteTag.Sort,
2144				Translations: string(translations),
2145			}
2146			if err = tagRepo.Create(ctx, newTag); err != nil {
2147				return err
2148			}
2149		}
2150	}
2151
2152	if err = tx.Commit().Error; err != nil {
2153		return err
2154	}
2155	return nil
2156}
2157
2158func needsUpdate(localTag *model.Tag, remoteTag dto.Tag, translations string) bool {
2159	return localTag.Name != remoteTag.Name ||
2160		localTag.Sort != remoteTag.Sort ||
2161		localTag.Translations != translations
2162}

2192func isEditCompose(installed model.AppInstall) bool {

2205func composeEqualExceptImage(expected, current string) (bool, error) {
2206	expectedCompose := make(map[string]interface{})
2207	if err := yaml.Unmarshal([]byte(expected), &expectedCompose); err != nil {
2208		return false, err
2209	}
2210	currentCompose := make(map[string]interface{})
2211	if err := yaml.Unmarshal([]byte(current), &currentCompose); err != nil {
2212		return false, err
2213	}
2214	removeComposeServiceImages(expectedCompose)
2215	removeComposeServiceImages(currentCompose)
2216	return reflect.DeepEqual(expectedCompose, currentCompose), nil
2217}
2218
2219func removeComposeServiceImages(composeMap map[string]interface{}) {
2220	services, ok := composeMap["services"].(map[string]interface{})
2221	if !ok {
2222		return
2223	}
2224	for _, service := range services {
2225		serviceMap, ok := service.(map[string]interface{})
2226		if !ok {
2227			continue
2228		}
2229		delete(serviceMap, "image")
2230	}
2231}

2233func getAppVersions(key string, details []model.AppDetail) []string {
2234	var (
2235		versionsRaw []string
2236		versions    []string
2237	)
2238	hasLatest := false
2239	latestVersion := ""
2240	for _, detail := range details {
2241		if key != "mssql" && strings.Contains(detail.Version, "latest") {
2242			hasLatest = true
2243			latestVersion = detail.Version
2244			continue
2245		}
2246		if key == "openresty" && !common.CompareAppVersion(detail.Version, "1.27") {
2247			continue
2248		}
2249		versionsRaw = append(versionsRaw, detail.Version)
2250	}
2251	versions = common.GetSortedVersions(versionsRaw)
2252	if hasLatest {
2253		versions = append([]string{latestVersion}, versions...)
2254	}
2255	return versions
2256}

  38func (u *BackupService) AppBackup(req dto.CommonBackup) (*model.BackupRecord, error) {

  40	if err != nil {

  42	}
