  46	}
  47	timeNow := time.Now().Format(constant.DateTimeSlimLayout)
  48	itemDir := fmt.Sprintf("app/%s/%s", req.Name, req.DetailName)
  49	backupDir := path.Join(global.Dir.LocalBackupDir, itemDir)
  50
  51	fileName := req.FileName
  52	if req.FileName == "" {
  53		fileName = fmt.Sprintf("%s_%s.tar.gz", req.DetailName, timeNow+common.RandStrAndNum(5))
  54	}
  55
  56	record := &model.BackupRecord{
  57		Type:              "app",
  58		Name:              req.Name,
  59		DetailName:        req.DetailName,
  60		SourceAccountIDs:  "1",
  61		DownloadAccountID: 1,
  62		FileDir:           itemDir,
  63		FileName:          fileName,
  64		TaskID:            req.TaskID,
  65		Status:            constant.StatusWaiting,
  66		Description:       req.Description,
  67	}
  68	if err := backupRepo.CreateRecord(record); err != nil {
  69		global.LOG.Errorf("save backup record failed, err: %v", err)
  70		return nil, err
  71	}
  72
  73	if !req.IsImmediate {
  74		if err = handleAppBackup(&install, nil, record.ID, backupDir, fileName, "", req.Secret, req.TaskID); err != nil {
  75			markBackupFailed(record.ID, err)
  76			global.LOG.Errorf("backup app %s failed, err: %v", req.DetailName, err)
  77			return nil, err
  78		}
  79		return record, nil
  80	}
  81
  82	backupTask, err := task.NewTaskWithOps(install.Name, task.TaskBackup, task.TaskScopeBackup, req.TaskID, install.ID)
  83	if err != nil {
  84		markBackupFailed(record.ID, err)
  85		record.Status = constant.StatusFailed
  86		record.Message = err.Error()
  87		return nil, err
  88	}
  89	if err = doAppBackup(&install, backupTask, backupDir, fileName, "", req.Secret); err != nil {
  90		markBackupFailed(record.ID, err)
  91		record.Status = constant.StatusFailed
  92		record.Message = err.Error()
  93		return nil, err
  94	}
  95	backupRepo.UpdateRecordByMap(record.ID, map[string]interface{}{"status": constant.StatusSuccess})
  96	record.Status = constant.StatusSuccess
  97	return record, nil
  98}
  99
 100func backupAppWithParentTask(install *model.AppInstall, parentTask *task.Task, fileName string) (*model.BackupRecord, error) {
 101	itemDir := fmt.Sprintf("app/%s/%s", install.App.Key, install.Name)
 102	backupDir := path.Join(global.Dir.LocalBackupDir, itemDir)
 103	record := &model.BackupRecord{
 104		Type:              "app",
 105		Name:              install.App.Key,
 106		DetailName:        install.Name,
 107		SourceAccountIDs:  "1",
 108		DownloadAccountID: 1,
 109		FileDir:           itemDir,
 110		FileName:          fileName,
 111		TaskID:            parentTask.TaskID,
 112		Status:            constant.StatusWaiting,
 113	}
 114	if err := backupRepo.CreateRecord(record); err != nil {
 115		return nil, err
 116	}
 117	if err := handleAppBackup(install, parentTask, record.ID, backupDir, fileName, "", "", parentTask.TaskID); err != nil {
 118		markBackupFailed(record.ID, err)
 119		record.Status = constant.StatusFailed
 120		record.Message = err.Error()
 121		return record, err
 122	}
 123	backupRepo.UpdateRecordByMap(record.ID, map[string]interface{}{"status": constant.StatusSuccess})
 124	record.Status = constant.StatusSuccess
 125	return record, nil
 126}
 127
 128func (u *BackupService) AppRecover(req dto.CommonRecover) error {
 129	app, err := appRepo.GetFirst(appRepo.WithKey(req.Name))
 130	if err != nil {
 131		return err
 132	}
 133	install, err := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByName(req.DetailName), appInstallRepo.WithAppId(app.ID))
 134	if err != nil {
 135		return err
 136	}
 137
 138	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 139	if !fileOp.Stat(req.File) {
 140		return buserr.WithName("ErrFileNotFound", req.File)
 141	}
 142	if _, err := compose.Down(install.GetComposePath()); err != nil {
 143		return err
 144	}
 145	if err := handleAppRecover(&install, nil, req.File, false, req.Secret, req.TaskID); err != nil {
 146		global.LOG.Errorf("recover app %s failed, err: %v", req.DetailName, err)
 147	}
 148	return nil
 149}
 150
 151func backupDatabaseWithTask(parentTask *task.Task, resourceKey, tmpDir, name string, databaseID uint) error {
 152	switch resourceKey {
 153	case constant.AppMysql, constant.AppMariaDB:
 154		db, err := mysqlRepo.Get(repo.WithByID(databaseID))
 155		if err != nil {
 156			return err
 157		}
 158		parentTask.LogStart(task.GetTaskName(db.Name, task.TaskBackup, task.TaskScopeDatabase))
 159		databaseHelper := DatabaseHelper{Database: db.MysqlName, DBType: resourceKey, Name: db.Name}
 160		if err := handleMysqlBackup(databaseHelper, parentTask, 0, tmpDir, fmt.Sprintf("%s.sql.gz", name), "", ""); err != nil {
 161			return err
 162		}
 163		parentTask.LogSuccess(task.GetTaskName(db.Name, task.TaskBackup, task.TaskScopeDatabase))
 164	case constant.AppPostgresql:
 165		db, err := postgresqlRepo.Get(repo.WithByID(databaseID))
 166		if err != nil {
 167			return err
 168		}
 169		parentTask.LogStart(task.GetTaskName(db.Name, task.TaskBackup, task.TaskScopeDatabase))
 170		databaseHelper := DatabaseHelper{Database: db.PostgresqlName, DBType: resourceKey, Name: db.Name}
 171		if err := handlePostgresqlBackup(databaseHelper, parentTask, 0, tmpDir, fmt.Sprintf("%s.sql.gz", name), "", ""); err != nil {
 172			return err
 173		}
 174		parentTask.LogSuccess(task.GetTaskName(db.Name, task.TaskBackup, task.TaskScopeDatabase))
 175	}
 176	return nil
 177}
 178
 179func handleAppBackup(install *model.AppInstall, parentTask *task.Task, recordID uint, backupDir, fileName, excludes, secret, taskID string) error {
 180	var (
 181		err        error
 182		backupTask *task.Task
 183	)
 184	backupTask = parentTask
 185	if parentTask == nil {
 186		backupTask, err = task.NewTaskWithOps(install.Name, task.TaskBackup, task.TaskScopeBackup, taskID, install.ID)
 187		if err != nil {
 188			return err
 189		}
 190	}
 191
 192	itemHandler := func() error { return doAppBackup(install, backupTask, backupDir, fileName, excludes, secret) }
 193	if parentTask != nil {
 194		return itemHandler()
 195	}
 196
 197	backupTask.AddSubTaskWithOps(task.GetTaskName(install.Name, task.TaskBackup, task.TaskScopeBackup), func(t *task.Task) error { return itemHandler() }, nil, 3, time.Hour)
 198	go func() {
 199		if err := backupTask.Execute(); err != nil {
 200			markBackupFailed(recordID, err)
 201			return
 202		}
 203		backupRepo.UpdateRecordByMap(recordID, map[string]interface{}{"status": constant.StatusSuccess})
 204	}()
 205
 206	return nil
 207}
 208
 209func handleAppRecover(install *model.AppInstall, parentTask *task.Task, recoverFile string, isRollback bool, secret, taskID string) error {
 210	var (
 211		err          error
 212		recoverTask  *task.Task
 213		isOk         = false
 214		rollbackFile string
 215	)
 216	recoverTask = parentTask
 217	if parentTask == nil {
 218		recoverTask, err = task.NewTaskWithOps(install.Name, task.TaskRecover, task.TaskScopeBackup, taskID, install.ID)
 219		if err != nil {
 220			return err
 221		}
 222	}
 223
 224	recoverApp := func(t *task.Task) error {
 225		fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 226		if !isRollback {
 227			rollbackFile = path.Join(global.Dir.TmpDir, fmt.Sprintf("app/%s_%s.tar.gz", install.Name, time.Now().Format(constant.DateTimeSlimLayout)))
 228			if err := handleAppBackup(install, recoverTask, 0, path.Dir(rollbackFile), path.Base(rollbackFile), "", "", taskID); err != nil {
 229				t.Log(fmt.Sprintf("backup app %s for rollback before recover failed, err: %v", install.Name, err))
 230			}
 231		}
 232
 233		if err := fileOp.TarGzExtractPro(recoverFile, path.Dir(recoverFile), secret); err != nil {
 234			return err
 235		}
 236		tmpPath := strings.ReplaceAll(recoverFile, ".tar.gz", "")
 237		if err := fileOp.TarGzExtractPro(tmpPath+"/app.tar.gz", tmpPath, ""); err != nil {
 238			return err
 239		}
 240		defer func() {
 241			if isRollback {
 242				_, _ = compose.UpWithoutBuild(install.GetComposePath())
 243			} else {
 244				_, _ = compose.Up(install.GetComposePath())
 245			}
 246			_ = os.RemoveAll(strings.ReplaceAll(recoverFile, ".tar.gz", ""))
 247		}()
 248
 249		if !fileOp.Stat(tmpPath+"/app.json") || !fileOp.Stat(tmpPath+"/app.tar.gz") {
 250			return errors.New(i18n.GetMsgByKey("AppBackupFileIncomplete"))
 251		}
 252		var backupInstall model.AppInstall
 253		appJson, err := os.ReadFile(tmpPath + "/app.json")
 254		if err != nil {
 255			return err
 256		}
 257		if err := json.Unmarshal(appJson, &backupInstall); err != nil {
 258			return fmt.Errorf("unmarshal app.json failed, err: %v", err)
 259		}
 260		if backupInstall.App.Key != install.App.Key || backupInstall.Name != install.Name {
 261			return errors.New(i18n.GetMsgByKey("AppAttributesNotMatch"))
 262		}
 263		backupEnvMap, err := getEnvMapByPath(path.Join(tmpPath, install.Name, ".env"))
 264		if err != nil {
 265			return err
 266		}
 267		installedEnvMap, err := getEnvMapByPath(install.GetEnvPath())
 268		if err != nil {
 269			return err
 270		}
 271		var mergedEnvContent string
 272		resources, _ := appInstallResourceRepo.GetBy(appInstallResourceRepo.WithAppInstallId(install.ID))
 273		for _, resource := range resources {
 274			var database model.Database
 275			switch resource.From {
 276			case constant.AppResourceRemote:
 277				database, err = databaseRepo.Get(repo.WithByID(resource.LinkId))
 278				if err != nil {
 279					return err
 280				}
 281			case constant.AppResourceLocal:
 282				resourceApp, err := appInstallRepo.GetFirst(repo.WithByID(resource.LinkId))
 283				if err != nil {
 284					return err
 285				}
 286				database, err = databaseRepo.Get(databaseRepo.WithAppInstallID(resourceApp.ID), repo.WithByType(resource.Key), repo.WithByFrom(constant.AppResourceLocal), repo.WithByName(resourceApp.Name))
 287				if err != nil {
 288					return err
 289				}
 290			}
 291			switch database.Type {
 292			case constant.AppPostgresql:
 293				db, err := postgresqlRepo.Get(repo.WithByID(resource.ResourceId))
 294				if err != nil {
 295					return err
 296				}
 297				newDB, err := reCreatePostgresqlDB(db.ID, database, backupEnvMap)
 298				if err != nil {
 299					return err
 300				}
 301				backupInstall.Env, mergedEnvContent, err = buildRecoverEnv(backupInstall.Env, backupEnvMap, installedEnvMap)
 302				if err != nil {
 303					return err
 304				}
 305				_ = appInstallResourceRepo.BatchUpdateBy(map[string]interface{}{"resource_id": newDB.ID}, repo.WithByID(resource.ID))
 306				taskName := task.GetTaskName(db.Name, task.TaskRecover, task.TaskScopeDatabase)
 307				t.LogStart(taskName)
 308				if err := handlePostgresqlRecover(dto.CommonRecover{
 309					Name:       newDB.PostgresqlName,
 310					DetailName: newDB.Name,
 311					File:       fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s.sql.gz", tmpPath, install.Name),
 312				}, parentTask, true); err != nil {
 313					t.LogFailedWithErr(taskName, err)
 314					return err
 315				}
 316				t.LogSuccess(taskName)
 317			case constant.AppMysql, constant.AppMariaDB:
 318				db, err := mysqlRepo.Get(repo.WithByID(resource.ResourceId))
 319				if err != nil {
 320					return err
 321				}
 322				newDB, err := reCreateDB(db.ID, database, backupEnvMap, install.Name)
 323				if err != nil {
 324					return err
 325				}
 326				backupInstall.Env, mergedEnvContent, err = buildRecoverEnv(backupInstall.Env, backupEnvMap, installedEnvMap)
 327				if err != nil {
 328					return err
 329				}
 330				_ = appInstallResourceRepo.BatchUpdateBy(map[string]interface{}{"resource_id": newDB.ID}, repo.WithByID(resource.ID))
 331				taskName := task.GetTaskName(db.Name, task.TaskRecover, task.TaskScopeDatabase)
 332				t.LogStart(taskName)
 333				if err := handleMysqlRecover(dto.CommonRecover{
 334					Name:       newDB.MysqlName,
 335					DetailName: newDB.Name,
 336					File:       fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s.sql.gz", tmpPath, install.Name),
 337				}, parentTask, true); err != nil {
 338					t.LogFailedWithErr(taskName, err)
 339					return err
 340				}
 341				t.LogSuccess(taskName)
 342			}
 343		}
 344
 345		appDir := install.GetPath()
 346		backPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s_bak", appDir)
 347		_ = fileOp.Rename(appDir, backPath)
 348		_ = fileOp.CreateDir(appDir, constant.DirPerm)
 349
 350		deCompressName := i18n.GetWithName("DeCompressFile", "app.tar.gz")
 351		t.LogStart(deCompressName)
 352		if err := fileOp.TarGzExtractPro(tmpPath+"/app.tar.gz", install.GetAppPath(), ""); err != nil {
 353			t.LogFailedWithErr(deCompressName, err)
 354			_ = fileOp.DeleteDir(appDir)
 355			_ = fileOp.Rename(backPath, appDir)
 356			return err
 357		}
 358		t.LogSuccess(deCompressName)
 359		_ = fileOp.DeleteDir(backPath)
 360
 361		if len(mergedEnvContent) != 0 {
 362			envPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s/.env", install.GetAppPath(), install.Name)
 363			file, err := os.OpenFile(envPath, os.O_WRONLY|os.O_TRUNC, 0640)
 364			if err != nil {
 365				return err
 366			}
 367			defer file.Close()
 368			_, _ = file.WriteString(mergedEnvContent)
 369		}
 370
 371		backupInstall.ID = install.ID
 372		backupInstall.Status = constant.StatusRunning
 373		backupInstall.AppId = install.AppId
 374		backupInstall.AppDetailId = install.AppDetailId
 375		backupInstall.App.ID = install.AppId
 376		if err := appInstallRepo.Save(context.Background(), &backupInstall); err != nil {
 377			global.LOG.Errorf("save db app install failed, err: %v", err)
 378			return err
 379		}
 380		isOk = true
 381
 382		return nil
 383	}
 384
 385	if parentTask != nil {
 386		return recoverApp(parentTask)
 387	}
 388
 389	rollBackApp := func(t *task.Task) {
 390		if isRollback {
 391			return
 392		}
 393		if !isOk {
 394			t.Log(i18n.GetMsgByKey("RecoverFailedStartRollBack"))
 395			if err := handleAppRecover(install, t, rollbackFile, true, "", ""); err != nil {
 396				t.LogFailedWithErr(i18n.GetMsgByKey("Rollback"), err)
 397				return
 398			}
 399			t.LogSuccess(i18n.GetMsgByKey("Rollback"))
 400			_ = os.RemoveAll(rollbackFile)
 401		} else {
 402			_ = os.RemoveAll(rollbackFile)
 403		}
 404	}
 405	recoverTask.AddSubTask(task.GetTaskName(install.Name, task.TaskRecover, task.TaskScopeBackup), recoverApp, rollBackApp)
 406	go func() {
 407		_ = recoverTask.Execute()
 408	}()
 409	return nil
 410}
 411
 412func doAppBackup(install *model.AppInstall, parentTask *task.Task, backupDir, fileName, excludes, secret string) error {
 413	fileOp := files.NewFileOp()
 414	tmpDir := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%s", backupDir, strings.ReplaceAll(fileName, ".tar.gz", ""))
 415	if !fileOp.Stat(tmpDir) {
 416		if err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDir, os.ModePerm); err != nil {
 417			return fmt.Errorf("mkdir %s failed, err: %v", backupDir, err)
 418		}
 419	}
 420	defer func() {
 421		_ = os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
 422	}()
 423
 424	remarkInfo, _ := json.Marshal(install)
 425	remarkInfoPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s/app.json", tmpDir)
 426	if err := fileOp.SaveFile(remarkInfoPath, string(remarkInfo), fs.ModePerm); err != nil {
 427		return err
 428	}
 429
 430	appPath := install.GetPath()
 431	parentTask.LogStart(i18n.GetMsgByKey("TaskBackup"))
 432	if err := fileOp.TarGzCompressPro(true, appPath, path.Join(tmpDir, "app.tar.gz"), "", excludes); err != nil {
 433		return err
 434	}
 435
 436	resources, _ := appInstallResourceRepo.GetBy(appInstallResourceRepo.WithAppInstallId(install.ID))
 437	for _, resource := range resources {
 438		if err := backupDatabaseWithTask(parentTask, resource.Key, tmpDir, install.Name, resource.ResourceId); err != nil {
 439			return err
 440		}
 441	}
 442	parentTask.LogStart(i18n.GetMsgByKey("CompressDir"))
 443	if err := fileOp.TarGzCompressPro(true, tmpDir, path.Join(backupDir, fileName), secret, ""); err != nil {
 444		return err
 445	}
 446	parentTask.Log(i18n.GetWithName("CompressFileSuccess", fileName))
 447	return nil
 448}
 449
 450func reCreateDB(dbID uint, database model.Database, envMap map[string]interface{}, appInstallName string) (*model.DatabaseMysql, error) {
 451	mysqlService := NewIMysqlService()
 452	ctx := context.Background()
 453	if err := deleteMysqlDatabaseForResourceOwner(
 454		ctx,
 455		dto.MysqlDBDelete{ID: dbID, Database: database.Name, Type: database.Type, DeleteBackup: false, ForceDelete: true},
 456		dto.DBResource{Type: constant.TypeApp, Name: appInstallName},
 457	); err != nil {
 458		return nil, err
 459	}
 460
 461	dbInfo := getDBCreateInfoFromEnv(envMap, "utf8mb4")
 462	createDB, err := mysqlService.Create(context.Background(), dto.MysqlDBCreate{
 463		Name:       dbInfo.Name,
 464		From:       database.From,
 465		Database:   database.Name,
 466		Format:     dbInfo.Format,
 467		Permission: "%",
 468	})
 469	if err != nil {
 470		return nil, err
 471	}
 472	if len(dbInfo.User) != 0 {
 473		if err := ensureMysqlDBUser(mysqlService, database, dbInfo); err != nil {
 474			return nil, err
 475		}
 476	}
 477	updateCronjobsDBRef(dbID, createDB.ID)
 478	return createDB, nil
 479}
 480
 481func ensureMysqlDBUser(mysqlService IMysqlService, database model.Database, dbInfo dbRecreateInfo) error {
 482	const host = "%"
 483	users, err := mysqlService.ListUsers(dto.MysqlUserSearch{Database: database.Name})
 484	if err != nil {
 485		return err
 486	}
 487	var oldUser dto.MysqlUser
 488	exists := false
 489	for _, user := range users {
 490		if user.Username == dbInfo.User && user.Host == host && !user.IsDelete {
 491			oldUser = user
 492			exists = true
 493			break
 494		}
 495	}
 496	if exists {
 497		if len(oldUser.Password) != 0 && oldUser.Password != dbInfo.Password {
 498			return buserr.New("ErrDbUserNotValid")
 499		}
 500	} else {
 501		if err := mysqlService.CreateUser(dto.MysqlUserCreate{
 502			Database: database.Name,
 503			Username: dbInfo.User,
 504			Host:     host,
 505			Password: dbInfo.Password,
 506		}); err != nil {
 507			return err
 508		}
 509	}
 510	return mysqlService.GrantUser(dto.MysqlGrantCreate{
 511		Database: database.Name,
 512		DB:       dbInfo.Name,
 513		Username: dbInfo.User,
 514		Host:     host,
 515	})
 516}
 517
 518func reCreatePostgresqlDB(dbID uint, database model.Database, envMap map[string]interface{}) (*model.DatabasePostgresql, error) {
 519	postgresqlService := NewIPostgresqlService()
 520	_ = postgresqlService.Delete(context.Background(), dto.PostgresqlDBDelete{
 521		ID:           dbID,
 522		Type:         database.Type,
 523		Database:     database.Name,
 524		DeleteBackup: false,
 525		ForceDelete:  true,
 526	})
 527
 528	dbInfo := getDBCreateInfoFromEnv(envMap, "UTF8")
 529	createDB, err := postgresqlService.Create(context.Background(), dto.PostgresqlDBCreate{
 530		Name:      dbInfo.Name,
 531		From:      database.From,
 532		Database:  database.Name,
 533		Format:    dbInfo.Format,
 534		Username:  dbInfo.User,
 535		Password:  dbInfo.Password,
 536		SuperUser: true,
 537	})
 538	if err != nil {
 539		return nil, err
 540	}
 541	updateCronjobsDBRef(dbID, createDB.ID)
 542	return createDB, nil
 543}
 544
 545"cmt">// dbRecreateInfo (struct)
 546type dbRecreateInfo struct {
 547	Name     string
 548	User     string
 549	Password string
 550	Format   string
 551}
 552
 553func getDBCreateInfoFromEnv(envMap map[string]interface{}, defaultFormat string) dbRecreateInfo {
 554	name, _ := envMap["PANEL_DB_NAME"].(string)
 555	user, _ := envMap["PANEL_DB_USER"].(string)
 556	password, _ := envMap["PANEL_DB_USER_PASSWORD"].(string)
 557	format, _ := envMap["format"].(string)
 558	if len(format) == 0 {
 559		format = defaultFormat
 560	}
 561	return dbRecreateInfo{
 562		Name:     name,
 563		User:     user,
 564		Password: password,
 565		Format:   format,
 566	}
 567}
 568
 569func updateCronjobsDBRef(oldDBID, newDBID uint) {
 570	cronjobs, _ := cronjobRepo.List(cronjobRepo.WithByDbName(fmt.Sprintf("%v", oldDBID)))
 571	for _, job := range cronjobs {
 572		_ = cronjobRepo.Update(job.ID, map[string]interface{}{"db_name": fmt.Sprintf("%v", newDBID)})
 573	}
 574}
 575
 576func buildRecoverEnv(appEnv string, backupEnvMap, installedEnvMap map[string]interface{}) (string, string, error) {
 577	currentHostVal, hasCurrentHost := installedEnvMap["PANEL_DB_HOST"]
 578	if hasCurrentHost && fmt.Sprintf("%v", currentHostVal) != "" {
 579		backupHost := fmt.Sprintf("\"PANEL_DB_HOST\":\"%v\"", backupEnvMap["PANEL_DB_HOST"])
 580		currentHost := fmt.Sprintf("\"PANEL_DB_HOST\":\"%v\"", currentHostVal)
 581		appEnv = strings.ReplaceAll(appEnv, backupHost, currentHost)
 582		if _, ok := backupEnvMap["CASDOOR_DATASOURCE_NAME"]; ok {
 583			backupEnvMap["CASDOOR_DATASOURCE_NAME"] = strings.ReplaceAll(
 584				fmt.Sprintf("%v", backupEnvMap["CASDOOR_DATASOURCE_NAME"]),
 585				fmt.Sprintf("%v", backupEnvMap["PANEL_DB_HOST"]),
 586				fmt.Sprintf("%v", currentHostVal),
 587			)
 588		}
 589		backupEnvMap["PANEL_DB_HOST"] = currentHostVal
 590	}
 591	newEnvMap := make(map[string]string, len(backupEnvMap))
 592	handleMap(backupEnvMap, newEnvMap)
 593	mergedEnvContent, err := gotenv.Marshal(newEnvMap)
 594	if err != nil {
 595		return appEnv, "", err
 596	}
 597	return appEnv, mergedEnvContent, nil
 598}
 599
 600func getEnvMapByPath(envPath string) (map[string]interface{}, error) {
 601	envMap := make(map[string]interface{})
 602	envFile, err := os.ReadFile(envPath)
 603	if err != nil {
 604		return envMap, err
 605	}
 606	lines := strings.Split(string(envFile), "\n")
 607	for _, line := range lines {
 608		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
 609		if line == "" || strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
 610			continue
 611		}
 612		parts := strings.SplitN(line, "=", 2)
 613		if len(parts) == 2 {
 614			value := strings.TrimSpace(parts[1])
 615			if len(value) >= 2 {
 616				if (value[0] == '"' && value[len(value)-1] == '"') || (value[0] == '\'' && value[len(value)-1] == '\'') {
 617					value = value[1 : len(value)-1]
 618				}
 619			}
 620			envMap[parts[0]] = value
 621		}
 622	}
 623	return envMap, nil
 624}

  19func (a *AppRouter) InitRouter(Router *gin.RouterGroup) {
  20	appRouter := Router.Group("apps")
  21
  22	baseApi := v2.ApiGroupApp.BaseApi
  23	{
  24		appRouter.POST("/sync/remote", baseApi.SyncApp)
  25		appRouter.POST("/sync/local", baseApi.SyncLocalApp)
  26		appRouter.GET("/checkupdate", baseApi.GetAppListUpdate)
  27		appRouter.POST("/search", baseApi.SearchApp)
  28		appRouter.GET("/:key", baseApi.GetApp)
  29		appRouter.GET("/detail/:appId/:version/:type", baseApi.GetAppDetail)
  30		appRouter.GET("/detail/node/:appKey/:version", baseApi.GetAppDetailForNode)
  31		appRouter.GET("/details/:id", baseApi.GetAppDetailByID)
  32		appRouter.POST("/install", baseApi.InstallApp)
  33		appRouter.GET("/tags", baseApi.GetAppTags)
  34		appRouter.GET("/icon/:key", baseApi.GetAppIcon)
  35
  36		appRouter.POST("/installed/check", baseApi.CheckAppInstalled)
  37		appRouter.POST("/installed/loadport", baseApi.LoadPort)
  38		appRouter.POST("/installed/conninfo", baseApi.LoadConnInfo)
  39		appRouter.GET("/installed/delete/check/:appInstallId", baseApi.DeleteCheck)
  40		appRouter.POST("/installed/search", baseApi.SearchAppInstalled)
  41		appRouter.GET("/installed/list", baseApi.ListAppInstalled)
  42		appRouter.POST("/installed/op", baseApi.OperateInstalled)
  43		appRouter.POST("/installed/sync", baseApi.SyncInstalled)
  44		appRouter.POST("/installed/port/change", baseApi.ChangeAppPort)
  45		appRouter.GET("/services/:key", baseApi.GetServices)

  47		appRouter.GET("/installed/params/:appInstallId", baseApi.GetParams)
  48		appRouter.POST("/installed/params/update", baseApi.UpdateInstalled)
  49		appRouter.POST("/installed/update/versions", baseApi.GetUpdateVersions)
  50		appRouter.POST("/installed/config/update", baseApi.UpdateAppConfig)
